# graft — map
One line per public item. Update when a public item changes.

## lib.rs
- `cli` (mod) — CLI argument parsing.
- `empty_state` (mod, `pub(crate)`) — the empty state's keys and view (m8-polish-and-ship/08): `OutsideState`, `Prompt` (`Open(TextField)`, `ConfirmInit`), `OutsideStep` (`Stay`, `Open(path)`, `Init(folder)`, `Quit`), `outside_edge(state, &Keymap, key)` (m8-09b: the outside screen's edge — the open prompt takes keys as typed, else `translate(EmptyState, …)` — `translate(Confirm, …)` while the init box is open, m8/09d D6; an unbound default only clears the problem line; swept by `empty_state::sweep_tests`), `outside_key(state, key)` (the `i` arm is gated on `!missing` — the one switch for offering init in a missing folder), `view(state, now) -> EmptyStateView`; m8/11: `OutsideState::{palette, config_problems}` — `?`/`⌃P`/`:` open the palette on the list (not over the init box; typed in the open prompt), rows from `update::palette::listing` in `EmptyState`, `⏎` runs a row's default chord through the edge's own step, a theme row is `OutsideStep::Retheme(kind)` (the loop calls `Session::choose`); `palette_rows`, `outside_paste` (palette query or open prompt), `render(state, keymap, theme, ascii, now, area, buf)` (the list, the palette over it, its keys on the bar row); `o`'s path resolution (`~`, relative to the folder) and `⇥` directory completion.
- `recent` (mod, `pub(crate)`) — the recent-repository list, `<config_dir>/graft/recent`, lines `<unix secs>\t<canonical path>`, newest first, at most `MAX_RECENT` (20): `read_recent` (missing file → empty; bad lines skipped and counted), `remember` (dedup, cap, atomic temp-file + rename), `config_dir()`, `RecentError`.
- `config` (mod) — config file loading and types.
- `crash` (mod) — panic handling and terminal restore on crash.
- `event_loop` (mod) — the run loop, input, signals.
- `history` (mod) — history walk, layout, streaming.
- `search` (mod) — `/` search over commit subject, author, sha prefix and body (m1-the-graph/26).
- `jobs` (mod) — background job runner.
- `refs` (mod) — refs on each commit and ahead/behind, for the ref badges.
- `layout` (mod) — the detail pane's saved split, `<common_dir>/graft/layout` (m2-details-and-diffs/10b, ADR-0028).
- `map` (mod) — the Map view's per-branch bucket counts: pure data, no drawing (m2-details-and-diffs/18).
- `repos` (mod) — repository store, opened once and cached.
- `repository` (mod) — facts about one open repository.
- `session` (mod) — startup and per-run session state.
- `terminal` (mod) — raw mode and terminal session setup/restore.
- `update` (mod) — app state and the update function.
- `view` (mod) — draws state to a frame. It is the **layout site** ADR-0040 clause 3 names: `view::frame` asks the same `GraphScreen` the rows were drawn with for its `column_boundaries` and snaps the confirm box onto them (`graft_ui::graph_screen::snap_to_columns`), clearing its band full width only if no snap fits inside the frame; the credential prompt's band it clears outright, at every width, footer untouched. Neither widget learns the graph's geometry. The three modal boxes (confirm, name prompt, credential) are drawn by one private `draw_modal_boxes`, called once in `view_with_graphics` **after** whichever screen or overlay drew — so the resolver, the commit editor and the status screen draw a live box too (m6-07 F10; pinned by `view::frame::tests::a_live_confirm_box_is_drawn_over_every_overlay`). A bottom row that stands in for the graph footer — the resolver's, and in the sub-100-column tab layout the Detail tab's hint row and the focused banner's key row — goes through the private `draw_status_or_hints`: problem, else notice, else keys (m6-07 F9; `…::the_detail_tab_below_100_columns_draws_the_status_line`), each indented one column like the graph footer's (rulings 07b Q3; `…::a_status_on_a_standin_footer_starts_at_column_one`). **Under a live box the bottom row is the box's keys and nothing else** (rulings 07b Q1): `draw_box_bars`, one site before `draw_modal_boxes`, redraws every bar row (`bar_rows`: the resolver's, else the primary pane's bottom row; none on the status screen) with `view::rows::box_context` — credential, else name prompt, else confirm — which `footer_context` and `hint_context` also return first (`…::the_bottom_row_under_a_live_box_offers_only_its_keys`). The confirm box over the resolver clears its band full width rather than snapping (rulings 07b Q2, ADR-0040; `…::the_start_over_box_clears_its_band_over_the_resolver`).
- `examples/gallery` (dev-only example, not in the binary) — walks every test repo with `]`/`[`, grouped as a progression; a three-line banner under the header names the stop, group, keys and tier, then `shows:` and `differs:` (m1-the-graph/38); `--list` prints the same lines, `--matrix` writes `docs/shots/35-coverage-matrix.txt` (m1-the-graph/35). Its pure half is `tests/it/gallery.rs`, included by `#[path]`; `Entry::clock()` (m1-the-graph/53) says which clock each stop views its dates at — `Fixed(graft_fixtures::CORPUS_NOW)` for a corpus shape, `Wall` for a real repository — and `show` passes it into the `Harness`. `--group graph|details` picks the progression (m2/23; default graph); `--list` prints both. `tests/it/gallery.rs`: `Group`, `stops(group)`, `keys_label`, `capture_stem`, `GIT_GIT_RELEASE_MERGE`. `--group details` walks M2's 19 key-prelude stops; `--stems` prints their capture stems (`tests/it/gallery.rs::capture_stem`, the coverage matrix's details rows); `--matrix` writes the whole matrix through `tests/it/coverage.rs::write_matrix` — the gallery's two tables (`tests/it/gallery.rs::gallery_matrix`) plus the M3 and M4 walks' tables from the stem constants `coverage::{M3_STEMS, M4_STEMS}`, so no walk is compiled here and `gallery`, `m3_walk` and `m4_walk --matrix` write the same file (m2-details-and-diffs/23, m4-remotes/07 fix).
- `examples/m5_walk` (dev-only example) — M5's acceptance walk (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/11): all 49 scenarios the ticket lists (m5/37, m5/38, m5/39 closed the last of them; `TESTING.md` §History numbers them), each a `Scratch` copy of a corpus shape — or, when `Scenario.remote` is `fixture::Remote::Local`/`Http`, a `LocalRemote` work clone whose `origin` is a bare over `file://` or that same bare over Basic-auth HTTP (`HttpRemote`, the one transport that makes git ask for a password) — with a history scripted by git (`fixture.rs`: `forked`, `forked_on_main`, `fast_forwardable`, `conflicting`, `three_to_stage`, `diverged`, `twice_conflicting`, `ahead_to_merge_and_push`, `one_to_push`; `USER`/`PASSWORD`/`SHORT`/`SECRETS`, `root()`, `requests()`), the app driven headless by `Step`s — keys, `Type`/`TypeSecret`, `CursorTo`/`CursorToSubject`, `MenuPick`/`MenuPickOpen`, a mouse `DragBadgeOnto`, `Claim`, `Confirm`/`Choose`, `KeyFor` (a key taken for its commands only, so `e`'s `$EDITOR` never starts), `Cancel` (`Esc` on the write in flight, the real `CancelJob` path), `Relaunch` — over the real job pool, then **truth read by running git** (`truth.rs`: `Truth` = `RepoTruth` + `head_symbolic` + `index_tree` + in-progress marker + journal + bare refs + statuses). The one `AppState` read is the claim (`PreviewState.plan`), judged by `claim.rs::ghost_equals_git` (ref moves, ghost parents/subjects, patch-ids — reversed for a revert — HEAD, worktree paths ⊆ changed files); `every_scenario::ghost_equals_git_is_the_only_claim_check` greps the scenario files for any other plan read. Same CLI as `m4_walk`. Split: `mod.rs`, `steps.rs`, `fixture.rs`, `truth.rs`, `claim.rs`, `run.rs` (`Walk`, `wait_for`, `Seen`, the askpass wrapper script that logs a helper's argv and environment before handing over, and `no_secret_leaked` — run after **every** scenario over the askpass log, every event, status line and open-prompt `{AppState:?}`, and every byte of every file the fixture leaves behind), `gestures.rs` (cursor, menu, drag, stage), `vocabulary.rs`, scenarios by promise (`preview.rs`, `merge_rebase.rs`, `pick_revert_reset.rs`, `conflicts.rs`, `time_machine.rs`, `remotes.rs`, `regressions.rs`), `every_scenario.rs` (one test per scenario, the captures/matrix check, `contact_sheet_captions_cover_every_m5_capture`). Its table is `tests/it/coverage.rs::history_matrix` over `M5_STEMS` (the fifth table). Captures: `.scratch/m5-history-and-ghost-preview/pty-walk-m5.py`, whose `SECRETS` is the same two strings as `fixture::SECRETS`. The binary also answers **askpass mode** itself (`cli::askpass` → `run::askpass::respond`), as `m4_walk` does — without it the HTTP scenario's push fails `authentication failed` and git never asks.
- `tests/it/m5_walk/` **M6 additions** (m6-conflicts/07) — the same table gains M6's 19 scenarios (`TESTING.md` §Conflicts walk): `resolving.rs` (`Shape::StoppedMerge`: takes, whole-file cards, CRLF, base, `n`/`N`, start over, `$EDITOR`, mergetool; `STOPPED` is git's status order of its 13 paths) and `resolved_operations.rs` (rebase stops twice, stash pop with and without the app, `kill -9`, `u` after `c`, the re-push lease, modality, the tiny frame). New `Step`s: `Kill` (drop the app with no shutdown and reopen; asserts `truth::operation_on_disk` and `truth::last_journal_line_is_pending`), `Resolve(path)` (open the resolver from the changes pane), `HandOver(label, key)` (carry out `e`/`M` with the loop's own `event_loop::outside` builders and git's own editor lookup, `GIT_EDITOR`/`VISUAL`/`EDITOR` stripped from that lookup). New `Wait`s: `ConflictOpened`, `ResolvedPath(path)`, `ConflictedPath(path)`, `ResolverClosed`. `fixture.rs` adds `as_built`, `merge_aborted` (the stop's merge aborted, so the walk begins it in the app — ruling F5), `with_an_editor`, `with_a_mergetool` (`EDITED`, `TOOL_WROTE`), `stash_to_pop_over_a_conflict`, `twice_conflicting_in_two_places`; `truth.rs` adds `resolved_bytes`, `side_bytes` (`git checkout --ours|--theirs` on a fresh copy), `unmerged`, and re-exports `graft_ops::operation::Side`. The M6 table is `tests/it/coverage.rs::conflicts_matrix` over `M6_STEMS` (the sixth table; `--matrix` also writes it alone to `M6_MATRIX_PATH`, `.scratch/m6-conflicts/matrix.txt`); `every_scenario::contact_sheet_captions_cover_every_m6_capture` keeps `docs/shots/m6/` captioned. Captures: `.scratch/m6-conflicts/pty-walk-m6.py` (`m6-06` relaunches the real `graft` after a real `SIGKILL`, finding the scratch through `GRAFT_CAPTURE_REPO_OUT`, which `examples/m5_walk --capture` writes).
- `tests/it/m5_walk/` **M6 journeys** (m6-conflicts/21) — `journeys.rs`: `parse_counts` reads the one ```` ```journey-counts ```` fence of `docs/design-notes/m6-conflicts.md` (`Row{id, keys, parts}`; `-superseded` ignored; a stray token is an error), `journey_id` (`name` or `name@variant`), `within_the_note` (run by `run_scenario` on every scenario: a journey sending more inputs than its row fails naming both numbers and the keys), `inputs_of` (UX 1.3: key/chord/click/`Type` = 1, waits and truth reads 0), `frame_sees`, `click_file` (clicks the list row as drawn, `…`-cut names too; `$GRAFT_WALK_FRAMES` dumps a failing frame). `conflict_journeys.rs`: 24 scenarios walking all 19 rows, landing asserted from the drawn frame after every event; ten of them carry the pty captures `m6-10`…`m6-19` (`captured`: keys from the app's first frame to the landing; `m6-18` is the launch itself, no keys — m6-conflicts/43); its tests `journey_counts_match_the_note` (ids both ways, whole = sum of parts, prints the route table) and `one_key_over_the_note_fails`. New `Step`s `Route(bool)`, `Click(path)`, `Frame(what, &[Sees])` (`Sees::{Has, Lacks, BottomStarts, Row, Above}`); new `Wait`s `LandedOnHead`, `OnTheBanner`, `BackInProgress`, `FrameSettled`. `fixture.rs` adds `three_blocks`, `two_files`, and the shell-begun `*_in_the_shell` variants plus `two_files_pulled_in_the_shell`.
- `tests/it/m7_walk.rs` **M7 acceptance** (m7-power-tools/26) — `noted_keys(id)` reads both `journey-counts` fences of `docs/design-notes/m7-power-tools.md` through `m5_walk::parse_counts`; `WALKS` maps each of the 29 ids to (file, test); `every_m7_journey_id_has_a_walk` fails on an id with no walk, a stale entry, or a file lacking the id literal or the test fn. `m7_walk/promise.rs` (`tree_bytes`: every working file's bytes) holds the promise scenarios `rewrite.rs` (11 keys), `trace.rs` (8), `recover.rs` (16, `core.autocrlf=true`, byte-exact `u`; a pinned-clock child like `journal::the_three_lost_work_journeys`) and `guard.rs` (a refusal's status line at 80 columns). `m7_walk/pull.rs::pull_current_branch` walks `pull-current-branch`; `blame::Route::keys` returns a route's keys. Run by `scripts/m7-accept.sh`.
- `examples/m4_walk` (dev-only example) — M4's acceptance walk (m4-remotes/07): 21 scenarios, each a `graft_fixtures::remote::LocalRemote` (a bare over `file://` and its work clone) or the same bare over `HttpRemote` (Basic auth) or a `HungRemote`, the app driven headless by `Step`s over the real job pool with an askpass helper, then **truth read by running git on both the work clone and the bare**. Same CLI as `m3_walk` (`--only`, `--list`, `--stems`, `--capture`, `--matrix`); the binary also answers **askpass mode** itself (`cli::askpass` → `run::askpass::respond`), so a captured push can ask for a password. Pure half `tests/it/m4_walk/` (split by seam, m4-remotes/07 fix): `mod.rs` (`scenarios()`, `stems_line`, `capture_prelude`, re-exports `Scenario`, `run_scenario`, `prepare_capture`), `fixture.rs` (`RemoteKind`, `Fixture` — `build`, `change_on_remote`, `many_objects_on_remote` — `USER`/`PASSWORD`/`WRONG`/`SECRETS`), `truth.rs` (`Truth`: work `RepoTruth` + bare refs + journal + every status line + the remote's counters; `truth`, `diff_truth`, `Marks`), `steps.rs` (`Step` — `TypeSecret` types a password the walk never prints — `Wait`, `Scenario`, `Capture` with `secret` and `When`), `run.rs` (`run_scenario`, `prepare_capture`, `askpass_helper` — `CARGO_BIN_EXE_graft` under test, `current_exe` in the example — `no_secret_leaked`, run after every scenario: `PASSWORD`/`WRONG` in no event, status line or journal byte), `vocabulary.rs` (the step and assertion helpers), the scenarios by promise (`fetch_pull.rs`, `push.rs`, `credentials.rs`, `hangs_and_undo.rs`) and `every_scenario.rs` (one test per scenario under `cargo test`, `#[cfg(test)]`). The remotes table is `tests/it/coverage.rs::remotes_matrix` over `M4_STEMS`; `--matrix` writes all four tables through `coverage::write_matrix`, so the three `--matrix` commands agree.
- `examples/m3_walk` (dev-only example) — M3's acceptance walk (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/07): 22 scenarios, each a `Scratch` copy of a corpus shape, a scripted dirty state, the app driven headless by `Step`s over the real job pool, then repository truth read by running git. `ok <name>` / `FAIL <name>: <≤20 lines>`, exit 1 on failure; `--only <name>`, `--list`, `--stems` (capture stem, scenario, expected text, key label), `--capture <name>` (setup + headless prefix, then the interactive loop with the capture's keys as the prelude — what `pty-walk-m3.py` records), `--matrix` (all four tables, through `tests/it/coverage.rs::write_matrix`). Pure half `tests/it/m3_walk.rs`: `Step`, `Capture`, `Scenario`, `Marks`, `scenarios()`, `run_scenario`, `prepare_capture`; under `cargo test`, one test per scenario (`every_scenario::*`). Git as evidence is shared with the M4 walk in `tests/it/repo_truth.rs` (`RepoTruth`, `git_truth`, `diff_truth`, `git_path`, `run_git`, `neutralise_global_config`, `fsck_clean`, `keys_label`); its table is `tests/it/coverage.rs::working_tree_matrix` over `M3_STEMS`.
- `examples/budgets` (dev-only example) — `budgets <repo>` times first paint, G, g, RSS through the real jobs (`tests/it/budget_run.rs`); `budgets --phases <repo>` (m1-the-graph/55, `examples/budgets/phases.rs`) times first paint phase by phase on one thread with no jobs, plus discriminator laps. Each phase prints its peak-RSS and footprint rise; `refs.ref_snapshot` also prints `TipStats` (m1-the-graph/58b). Without a commit-graph `--phases` continues through `examples/budgets/no_cg.rs` (m1-the-graph/59): open, first paint, date walk to end, `topological_order`, promote swap (labelled "synchronous path, not the app's": `Views::promote`, which the app no longer takes, m7/12e), `g`, each with footprint, interner, order and resident bytes.

## map
The Map view's per-branch bucket counts (m2-details-and-diffs/18): one bounded walk per local branch into fixed time buckets, built fresh on toggle-on and discarded on toggle-off. No drawing — that is `view::map`, a later ticket.
- `MAX_ROWS` (const, `usize = 200`) — rows drawn before the rest collapse into `MapData::more`.
- `MAX_COMMITS_PER_ROW` (const, `usize = 50_000`) — a row's walk cap; past it the row and `MapData` are `complete = false`.
- `MapRequest { buckets, clock, policy, previous_default_tips }` — what `build_map` is asked to build.
- `MapMark` (enum) — `Tag(Vec<u8>)` or `Radar(Severity)`, one mark per bucket per row (Radar: loudest wins).
- `MapBranch { name, identity, tip, tip_date, counts, is_current, is_default, merged_into, marks, complete }` — one row.
- `MapData { branches, more, from, to, buckets, complete }` — every row plus the shared time span.
- `MapData::bucket_of(date) -> usize`, `MapData::max_count() -> u32`.
- `MapError` (enum) — `Store`/`Walk`/`Read`/`Stopped`; one-line `Display`.
- `build_map(store, req, progress, stop) -> Result<MapData, MapError>` — local branches only (M2); default-branch merges only.
- Split into files, public items unchanged (m2 review M8): `walk.rs` (refs, span, default chain, `walk_branch`/`RowWalk`, one bounded walk per branch), `rows.rs` (`RowContext`, `build_row`/`build_rows`, one row per local branch); this file keeps the public data shapes and `build_map`, the entry point wiring the two together.

## jobs::rewrite_open
The read behind `R` (m7-power-tools/08). `Command::OpenRewrite { id, req: RewriteRequest }` (`Open { from }` on the graph, `FromRebase(params)` from a rebase preview, rebase-preview-keeps-drops) → `Task::OpenRewrite` → `Product::RewriteOpened(Result<RewriteOpened, String>)`.
- `RewriteOpened` (enum) — `Editor(Box<OpenedRewrite>)` | `Refused(Refusal)`.
- `OpenedRewrite` (struct) — `params` (`plan(req)`), `whole: HashMap<Oid, String>` (every row's whole message, `history::read_commit`), `base: BaseRow`.
- `BaseRow` (struct) — `oid` (`onto`), `subject`, `label` (`Open`: upstream's short name if `onto` is the merge-base with it, else the default branch's, else the short sha; `FromRebase`: `preview::onto_label(params.onto)`).
- `open_rewrite` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(repos, req: &RewriteRequest, default_branch, stop)`: the read itself.

## history
- `Wanted::Find { commit, rows, fallback }` — lay out until `commit` has a row, answer around it (search's far hit); `fallback` when the walk ends without it.
- `History::seeking` (field) — the commit a pending `Find` is to select.
- `History::seek` (fn) — select a commit: at once if on hand, else a `Find` request (a jump, so Esc withdraws it).
- `History::restore` (fn) — put cursor/top back (search cancelled) and ask for that window.
- `keep_cursor_in_view` (fn, `pub(crate)`, `update::in_view`, m7-power-tools/48) — `(before, after, commands)`: last-but-one step of `update`. When `graph_rows` shrank (a dirty status pinned the uncommitted row) and the cursor was on screen before, moves `top` so it stays drawn and asks `History::show` for the rows. A cursor the wheel left off screen stays off (m7/37); nothing while `on_working_tree`.
- `walk_is_reading` (fn, `pub(crate)`, `update`, m6-conflicts/85 A) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: `awaiting` and (no history yet, or `History::is_reading`). The one reading of "the walk is reading": `update::keys::escape_key`'s (`keys/escape.rs`) last arm cancels the walk only when it is true (a parked or complete walk: `Esc` does nothing, no command, no status), and the header's `WalkView` draws reading/jumping exactly then.
- `serve` (fn) — the history worker's loop: answers each `RowRequest`; between requests, `Views::idle_step` a chunk at a time to the end of history (every `stop` check also takes a waiting request, so a request or cancel ends a step within one pull), then parks in `recv` — no wake, no CPU (m7-power-tools/12 Part B). Lives in `history/serve.rs` (moved out of `views.rs`, 12c); `Views` in `history/views/mod.rs`, its promotion state machine (`begin_promotion`, `promote`, the pending layout, swap, `reach`, `aim`) in `history/views/promotion.rs` (12f). A request carrying `promote` starts `Views::begin_promotion` and, until the swap, is answered from the provisional layout with `promote` cleared (12 Part C).
- `Views::idle_step` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — one bounded step nobody asked for: while a promotion is pending, one chunk of the promoted layout (swapping and re-answering the last request once ready); otherwise ≤ one `advance(chunk_rows, stop)` of the view last asked for, `HistoryUpdate::LaidOut` once per layout at the end (again after a fold or promotion). Returns `IdleWork`.
- `Views::begin_promotion` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(view, &Promote) -> Result<bool, StreamError>`: `true` if that order is already in force; else keeps (or opens) the pending promoted layout, re-aims its anchor, and swaps at once if ready. Ready = the layout is done, or has ≥ one chunk laid out and a row for the anchor (12c: a swap before the first chunk left the race short of `Complete`).
- `IdleWork` (enum, `pub(crate)`) — `Advanced { rows }`, `Promoting { rows }` (a pending promotion took the step), `Caught` (end reached or nothing asked: park), `NobodyListening`.
- `WindowOrder` — not a type: `Window::promoted` is the spec's `WindowOrder` (true = rows in the promoted order; the UI swaps order and re-anchors only on such a window, 12 Part C).
- `HistoryUpdate::LaidOut { rows }` — the walk reached the end of history unasked; `rows` is every row of the view. Not a request's answer: the header's "reading" and `Esc` ignore it.
- `History::laid_out` (field) — `Option<usize>`, the last `LaidOut` total heard; nothing is drawn from it yet.
- `History::is_reading` (fn) — streaming and working on something asked (a jump, an outstanding request, or no window yet). With `awaiting`, what `Esc` cancels and what the header calls reading (`update::walk_is_reading`, m6-conflicts/85). Once cancelled or failed, motions clamp to the held window.
- `DetailState::file_written` (fn) — a file written outside any operation (a resolver take): drops that path's cached working-tree diffs and re-asks the cursor's diff when it is that path (m6-conflicts/85 B).
- `History::strip_rows` (field) — pixel tier's strip height; requests widen to whole strips plus the row above. `None` (text tiers) asks exactly as before.

## view::strips
- `strips` (mod) — pixel-tier rows: aligned strips covering the viewport, painted like the text tiers.
- `pixel_rows` (fn) — this frame's strips from `AppState`: one fold across strips, uncommitted row on `HEAD`, thread/focus/cursor emphasis; strips missing rows or the row above are left out; all wait while the cursor is in the viewport and its row is missing (no thread yet, 30b); a cursor the wheel left off screen draws unlit (37).
- `PixelRows` (struct) — `strips`, shared `lane_columns`, `marker`, `uncommitted_on_top` (`AppState::uncommitted_row_pinned`: history rows sit one screen row lower; the strips cover `graph_rows` history rows, 48), `backdrop` (`Backdrop::lowest` over every row of every strip: one per frame, ADR-0053). Each painted row carries the fold's stubs into `PlainRow::stubs` (39).
- `StripRows` (struct) — one strip: `first_row`; `row_count`, `uncommitted_at`, `with_layers` (hands its `PlainStrip` and the `Selection` over it, at the frame's backdrop, to `StripCache::{backdrop, emphasis, composited}`).
- `StripPaint` (struct) — `cell_width`, `cell_height`, `palette`, `muted`; `StripPaint::of(session)`.

## session
- `Startup` (struct) — what `main` decided before touching the terminal; `harness` is what a wrapper around `run` asks for (`update::Harness`, default in the binary).
- `Session` (struct) — what Graft is drawing with, for as long as this run lasts.
- `Session::icons` / `Session::max_lanes` — **gone** (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33). Both moved to `AppState`: they decide how wide a badge is and how wide the graph column is, so they decide where a *drag* has to hit a badge, and the mouse hit-test has no `Session`. See `update::AppState::icons`.
- `Session::settle` (fn) — resolve capabilities, theme, rendering info at startup.
- `Session::apply` (fn) — take in a re-detected cell size or theme; `true` when anything changed. Never touches the tier. A theme change goes through `retheme`.
- `Session::retheme` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-16) — rebuild the theme as `kind` with that kind's `[theme.*]` overrides (kept privately from `settle`'s `&Loaded`); the only mid-run rebuild path; `true` if the theme changed. Palette theme rows call it through `choose`.
- `Session::choose` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8/11) — `retheme(kind)` and remember it as his choice for the run: a later re-detected background (`apply`) keeps it. Called by the loop for `Command::Retheme` (`event_loop::running::turn`, `rethemed`, which flushes `Flush::Theme`).
- `TerminalChange` (enum) — a mid-session answer that changed: `CellSize(CellSize)`, `Theme(ThemeHint)`.
- `Clock` (enum) — `Wall` (default) or `Fixed(i64)`; `now_seconds()` is the one `SystemTime::now()` read on the date path (m1-the-graph/53). `now_millis()` is the same instant at millisecond resolution, read for the focus-refresh debounce only (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). `update::Harness::clock` carries it; `view::graph_view` takes the result. **The one `SystemTime::now()` read for anything a capture can show** (m5/25): the journal's `at_unix_ms` comes through it too, via `JobRunner::with_clock`, so a pinned clock pins the ages the journal browser draws.

## event_loop
- `Wake::Terminal` (variant) — a re-detected answer changed; `pump` applies it to the session and redraws.
- `Wake::TerminalFailed` (variant) — a re-query could not be written or read; ends the loop with the error.
- `pump` (fn) — idle, while nothing waits, rasterises the strips `PageDown`/`PageUp` would show (30h). Now takes `&mut Session`, so re-detection can update it.
- `input::spawn_reader` (fn) — forwards input; on `FocusGained` also asks the redetector for the theme; returns the `Forwarder`. Fourth arg `stamps: Option<Sender<Instant>>` (m8 G-trace): while tracing, each key/mouse event's forwarding time is sent just before the event.
- `input::Forwarder` (struct) — the running forwarder; `finish` waits for it to end (after `InputMux::hold`, a bounded drain, never a job wait).
- `signals::watch_resizes` (fn) — resize wakes; each also asks the redetector for the cell size.
- `redetect::Redetector` (struct) — handle to the re-query worker; `spawn(host, cell_size, theme, budget, wakes)`, `ask(Query)`.
- `redetect::Query` (enum) — `CellSize` or `Theme`.
- `Terminal` (struct, m8-polish-and-ship/08) — one terminal session for the process: crash hook, `InputMux`, one capability probe; `open()`, `close(Ran) -> io::Result<Exit>`. Every place `drive` runs shares it, so the alternate screen and the probe happen once.
- `Ran` (struct) — a place's outcome and its job-pool shutdown; `Ran::without_jobs`.
- `Exit::Open(PathBuf)` (variant) — a screen asks to open another place; `drive` handles it (re-`locate`s), never returns it. `run` (gallery, walks) treats it as a broken promise.
- `places::Place` (enum) — `Repo { repos, path }` or `Outside(OutsideStart)`. `places::OutsideStart` (struct) — `folder`, `missing`, `cursor`, `problem`.
- `places::locate(path, &Loaded) -> Result<Place, String>` (fn) — before the terminal is touched: missing path → `Outside{missing}`; `NotARepo` → `Outside`; another open error → `Err` (printed, exit 1).
- `places::drive(Terminal, Place, &Loaded) -> io::Result<Exit>` (fn) — runs places on one terminal until one ends Graft; remembers each opened repository (tty only); builds the one keymap from `[keys]` (`config::keymap_from`) and hands it to every place (`Arrival::keymap`); config problems seeded on the first repository screen only (`Arrival::config_problems`, then `AppState::seed_keys`), and shown in the empty state's palette while there (m8/11); the outside screen's keys go through `empty_state::outside_edge` (m8-09b); a place reached by an open lands on `HEAD` (`update::lands_on_head_at_open`). `main` uses it on a tty; off a tty it uses `run`, as before.

## refs
- `RefBadges` (struct) — refs on every commit that has one, plus the `HEAD` and stash commits.
- `RefBadges::on` (fn) — the refs on one commit, in drawing order.
- `RefBadges::head` (fn) — the commit `HEAD` resolves to; `None` while unborn.
- `RefBadges::stash` (fn) — the newest stash entry's commit.
- `RefBadges::count` (fn) — labels over every commit; the frame trace's `refs=` (m8 G-trace).
- `RefBadges::remotes` (fn) — `(Oid, &[u8])` per remote-tracking branch, short name (`origin/main`): what `history::preview_seeds` colours ghost lanes from (m5/08).
- `RefBadges::tags` (fn) — `(Oid, &[u8])` per tag, short name (`v1.2.0`): what the name prompt checks a typed tag name against, so `t` can say `exists` before it runs anything (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10).
- `RefBadges::stashes` (fn) — `&[(Oid, usize)]`: every stash entry's commit and index, newest first, so each one gets its own badge rather than only `stash@{0}` (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `RefBadges::is_stash` (fn) — `(id) -> bool`: whether `id` is any stash entry's own commit; `selection::follow_selection` uses it to keep stash commits away from the evil-merge probe (m6-conflicts/66).
- `RefLabel` (enum) — one ref before sanitising: detached HEAD, branch, remote, tag, `Stash(usize)` (the entry's index, drawn `stash@{n}`; m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `Upstream` (enum) — a branch against its upstream: none, in sync (remote), apart (counts).
- `AheadBehindCache` (struct) — counts keyed by (branch tip, upstream tip), shared by refs jobs.
- `AheadBehindCache::get_or_count` (fn) — cached counts, walking only for a new pair.
- `read` (fn) — read the repository's refs into `RefBadges` (a job's work); reads the stash entries through `graft_git::stash::commits` (reflog only, no `diff-tree`).
- `badges` (fn) — the pure half of `read`: refs, HEAD, upstreams, stashes, a counter → `RefBadges` (the `stashes` argument added m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).

## crash
- `install` (fn) — install a panic hook that restores the terminal before dying.
- `Panicked` (struct) — a panic that was caught rather than allowed to end the process.
- `catching` (fn) — run `work`, catching a panic as `Panicked` instead of aborting.

## trace (m8-polish-and-ship G-trace, `pub(crate)`)
- `VAR` (const) — `GRAFT_PERF_TRACE`; renamed in G-rename (`TRIB_PERF_TRACE`).
- `FrameRecord` (struct) — one drawn frame's numbers: `at_us`, `wakes`, `motions`, `queued`, `update_us`, `commands`, `draw_us`, `bytes`, `write_us`, `fence_us` (how long the previous frame's fence waited for its answer; `-` when none came), `input_age_us`.
- `Header` (struct) — tier, columns, rows, `history_rows` (laid out so far), `refs` (`RefBadges::count`), term word; written before the first frame and on change.
- `FrameTrace` (struct) — the file; `from_env`/`from_var` (`Ok(None)` unset or empty), `writing_to`, `header`, `record` (flushed per line).
- `term_word` (fn) — `$TERM_PROGRAM` cut to `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`, ≤32, `-` when unset.
- `Meter` (struct), `Metered<W>` (struct) — bytes and time spent in the writes that reach the terminal; `Metered` sits under a `LineWriter`.
- `TerminalOut` (enum) — the terminal's writer: `Plain(Stdout)` or, tracing, `Metered(LineWriter<Metered<Stdout>>)`; `for_meter`.
- `Tracer` (struct) — the loop's side: `off`, `from_env`/`open` → `Opened { tracer, meter, stamps }`, `on`, `clock`, `took`, `updated`, `drew`, `fenced` (records the answer's wait for the next line), `unreported` (the failure reason, once).
- Tests: `trace::tests` (format, header, meter, stamps, failures); `scripts/m8-trace-summary.sh` (`--self-test` on `scripts/testdata/m8-trace-sample.trace`); `scripts/m8-trace-monorepo.sh` (real pty run on MONOREPO → `docs/perf/m8-trace-monorepo.txt`).

## terminal
- `SessionOptions` (struct) — what the session turns on at startup.
- `RawMode` (trait) — a `termios` raw-mode switch on the controlling terminal.
- `ProcessRawMode` (struct) — raw mode on the real controlling terminal.
- `TerminalSession` (struct) — RAII guard over terminal modes Graft turns on.
- `TerminalSession::on_stdout` (fn) — set up the real terminal: escapes plus raw mode.
- `TerminalSession::new` (fn) — set up terminal modes, undoing partial setup on failure.
- `TerminalSession::enable_kitty_keyboard` (fn) — turn on the kitty keyboard protocol.
- `TerminalSession::restorer` (fn) — get a handle that can restore from elsewhere (panic hook).
- `TerminalSession::restore` (fn) — put every applied mode back; idempotent.
- `TerminalSession::suspend` (fn) — restore every applied mode and hand back a `Suspended` naming them, so `$EDITOR` (or a `SIGTSTP`) gets the terminal as Graft found it; a failure mid-way puts back what it took off before returning the error (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05).
- `TerminalSession::resume` (fn) — re-apply exactly the modes a `Suspended` names, nothing else; a mode `suspend` did not find is not turned on.
- `Suspended` (struct) — the modes a `suspend` took off, the only way to `resume` them.
- `TerminalRestorer` (struct) — restore handle usable from a panic hook.
- `TerminalRestorer::restore` (fn) — put every applied mode back; idempotent.

## update
Split into files, public items unchanged (m2 review M8): `mod.rs` keeps only the dispatcher (`update`, `answer`, `core_event`); `geometry.rs` is the pure screen geometry a mouse event needs (`Bounds`, the detail pane's hit-testing, `context_menu_area`, `badge_at`); `mouse.rs` is what a mouse event does once `geometry` says where it landed. `geometry::badge_at(state, column, row) -> Option<DragSource>` is the one exception to "pure geometry restated here": it delegates the column to `crate::view::badge_index_at` and the row to the already-tested `graph_row_at`, so the only description of where a ref badge is drawn stays in `graft_ui` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10). It answers `None` for a detached `HEAD`, a remote branch, a tag and a stash — only a local branch is draggable. Both buttons **pick** the row they land on (`select_graph_row`): a right-click moves the cursor there and then opens that row's menu, so the menu's label and the verb the menu hands back down `key_event` can never name different commits (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/18 F1; pinned by `it::preview::a_right_click_moves_the_cursor_to_the_row_it_opens_the_menu_on` and the four `*_after_a_right_click_*` tests).
- `AppEvent` (enum) — events fed into `update`, including job products. `AppEvent::Rested` (m8-polish-and-ship/03, G-settle): sent only by the loop when the cursor rests after a burst; `settle` runs the follow it held back.
- `AppState::defer_follow` / `AppState::followed` (`pub(crate)` fields, G-settle) — the loop writes `defer_follow` before each update (mid-burst); `followed` is the `FollowKey` the pane and the evil probe last followed.
- `FollowKey` / `follow_key` / `follow_behind` (`pub(crate)`, `update::selection`, G-settle) — the selection's identity (`Commit(Oid)`, `WorkingTree`); `follow_behind` = something was followed and the cursor has left it without the follow running. `selection::follow_waits` (private to `update`) holds the follow mid-burst when it would start work (the pane follows, or a merge to probe); `settle` then calls `DetailState::abandon` (pane follows) or `DetailState::abandon_probe` (it does not: only the probe is cancelled).
- `pane_follows` (`pub(crate)`, `update::selection`, m8-03 review M1) — the pane follows the cursor: shown, and not a menu compare (no base pinned). The one predicate read by `followed`, `follow_waits` and `view::detail::following` (the title's `reading…`).
- `Progress` (enum) — what a running job hands over before it ends. `CredentialNeeded { prompt, pulse_seed }` is git asking for a password or a username through the askpass helper: it opens `AppState::credential` and the job blocks until `AnswerCredential`/`CancelCredential` comes back (m4-remotes/06). `pulse_seed` is where the masked field's keystroke pulse starts, drawn at random per prompt by `jobs::askpass::pulse_seed` and carried on the event so `update` stays pure (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/04, ADR-0038 §2).
- `Task` (enum) — jobs identified so their results can be told apart from stray ones; `ReadRefs` reads the badges; `ReadWorkingTree` reads full working-tree status; `PromoteHistory` sorts for ADR-0008 promotion; `Search` scans for `/`; `WriteCommitGraph`/`DeclineCommitGraph` the offer's jobs; `Operate` one working-tree write (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04). `Progress::Search` carries one chunk's hits; `Progress::Operating { description }` a running write's description; `ListFiles { spec }`, `FileDiff { key }`, `ContainedIn { oid }` the detail pane's reads; `EvilProbe { merge }` the open merge's evil-merge check; `Map` the Map view's build (m2-details-and-diffs/18), announced under `update`'s own id like the detail pane's own jobs. `ReadStashes` the stash list behind the `S` overlay (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). `Undo` one undo or redo and `ReadJournal` the journal read behind it (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06); both announce a `Progress::Operating { description }` like `Operate`. `ReadRemotes` the remote facts behind `f`/`p`/`P`; `Progress::RemoteProgress { brief }` a remote operation's git progress, already shortened by `RemoteProgress::brief` (m4-remotes/05).
- `Product` (enum) — what a job brought back; `Refs` carries `RefBadges` or why they failed; `WorkingTree` carries `Result<Arc<graft_git::status::WorkingTreeStatus>, String>` — full status, not just counts, shared for the changes pane and the uncommitted-row alike (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04); `TopologicalOrder` carries a sort's order and how long it took (`SortedOrder`); `CommitGraph(Result<Written, String>)` and `CommitGraphDeclined(Result<(), String>)` the offer's answers; `Files(Result<(Arc<FileListView>, Arc<FileList>), String>)`, `FileDiff(DiffKey, Result<Arc<DiffView>, String>)`, `ContainedIn(Oid, Result<Contained, String>)` the detail pane's answers; `EvilProbe(Oid, EvilVerdict)` one merge's evil-merge verdict (never a `Result` — `NotCheckable` is one of the verdict's own answers); `Map(Result<Arc<MapData>, String>)` the Map view's build, or why not; `Operated(Box<crate::jobs::operate::Operated>)` one working-tree write's answer — boxed (large-variant lint), not a plain `Result`, so `ApplyError::AfterState`'s "it ran, only part of the after-state could not be read" case cannot be collapsed away. `Undone(Box<crate::jobs::undo::Undone>)` one `u`/`U`'s answer — boxed and not a `Result`, for the same reason as `Operated`; `Journal(Result<Arc<crate::jobs::undo::JournalRead>, String>)` the journal as read from disk, entries plus the unreadable ones, and `Stashes(Result<Arc<Vec<graft_git::stash::StashEntry>>, String>)` the stash list behind `S` (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). `ExternalEditor(Result<String, String>)` carries what `$EDITOR` saved, **unstripped** — the commit editor strips `#` lines itself (`commit_editor::strip_comments`), the finish summary strips nothing (`#123 fixes login` must land, m6-conflicts/19) — or why the handover failed, and `HandedOver(Result<(), String>)` reports a handover with nothing to bring back (`OpenExternalEditor`, `Suspend`) — both produced by the loop, not the pool (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05). `Remotes(Result<Arc<crate::jobs::remote::RemoteFacts>, String>)` the remotes and HEAD's upstream, or why not (m4-remotes/05). `ConflictSet(Result<Arc<graft_ops::conflict::ConflictSet>, String>)` the resolver's file list, or why git's list could not be read (m6-conflicts/13). `BaseLoaded(Result<(Arc<ConflictFile>, usize), (String, bool)>)` the resolver's `B`: the document with every open block's base attached and how many, or `graft_ops::conflict::BaseError`'s own words plus whether they are a problem (only `Diff3` or the repo not opening; `NoBase`/`Mismatch` are facts) (m6-conflicts/05). `FinishSummary(Result<Arc<graft_ops::finish::FinishSummary>, String>)` the finish summary's read and `FinishDiff(Result<Arc<graft_ui::detail::DiffView>, String>)` one file's staged diff under it (m6-conflicts/19, built by 34).
- `GitCheck` (enum) — what running `git --version` found.
- `AppState` (struct) — the whole app state.
- `AppState::stopped` (field) — `Option<Stop>`: a live operation that stopped, as **state, not a `Status`** (m6-conflicts/12), so nothing that takes the status slot wipes it. Set by `operate::operated`'s and `banner::resumed`'s `Conflicted` and by `relaunch::land_on_live_operation`; cleared by `undo::operation_ended` and by a working-tree read with no `in_progress`. `Stop { origin: LiveOrigin, again, kind, paths_at_stop }`.
- `Stop::rewrite` (field, 07b) — `Option<Result<banner::RewriteStop, String>>`: what the job thread read at a rebase stop (`rewrite::progress` + `edit_stop`); `Err` is a failed read (today's banner, status says so). `AppState::edit_amend` (field, 07b) — the pending `HEAD`-message read is `a` at an `edit` stop: its answer amends at once, no editor.
- `AppState::clash` (field) — `Option<ClashState>`: a stash put back that clashed (m6-conflicts/72b), **not** a `Stop`. Set by `update::clash::clashed` and `refresh_clash`. Helpers `clash_facts_seen` (the launch's first facts are in), `clash_drop: Option<(JobId, Option<usize>)>` (the `DropPutBack` the last file done launched, and the index its words name), `clash_pane_owed` (the landing's pane waits for a dirty working-tree read) — all `pub(crate)`.
- `ClashState` (struct) — `{ clash: graft_ops::clash::StashClash, origin: LiveOrigin }`; `pub` because `AppState` is.
- `AppState::clash_index` (field, `pub(crate)`) / `IndexRead` (enum: `Unknown` / `Pending { id, stale }` / `Read(Result<IndexState, String>)`) — the index read that ties a clash candidate to the index (m6-conflicts/82, ADR-0045 §2 amended). Asked only while `clash_candidate` is `Some`; a read landing while one is out marks it `stale`, and its answer asks again. `clash_index_out()` (fn, `pub`) — a read is out (the harness's `quiet` waits on it).
- `AppState::status_read` (field) — `Option<JobId>`: the newest working-tree read asked for and not answered; while `Some`, `c` does not count the cached status (m6-conflicts/26).
- `AppState::undo_landing_owed` (field, `pub(crate)`) — `bool`: an undo or redo answered `Done` with nothing live; the newest working-tree read after it decides whether it put an operation back. Spent by `update::undo::land_after_undo` (ticket 41).
- `AppState::land_on_head` / `head_landing` / `live_landing_owed` (fields) — the landings owed after a finished `--continue` (`HeadLanding { refs, walk }`: the refs read and the walk it waits on, by `JobId`) and after a launch a modal deferred. Also set by `operate::operated`'s `Done` for a write whose launch said `Operation::moves_head` (`lands`, `pub(crate)`: `Option<(JobId, operate::Landing)>`, the launching job id and where it lands, beside `operating`; cleared by any answer or by `write_job_ended`) — m7-power-tools/03; m7/24-a widened it from `lands_on_head` so a put-back or `b` lands on its commit (`HeadLanding::on`, `pub(crate)`). The harness prelude (`update::prelude`) feeds no key while `land_on_head` is owed.
- `AppState::quit` (field) — set when Graft is leaving; terminal restored before reporting.
- `AppState::quit_when_done` (field, m8-13b) — `Option<update::quit::QuitWait>`: `[ Quit ]` over a `Stoppable::No` write; Graft quits when that write answers done. `shown_status` draws its line over the status (after an unseen failure) while the write runs.
- `AppState::remotes` / `AppState::remote` (fields) — the remote facts `f`/`p`/`P` decide from (`Loading<Arc<jobs::remote::RemoteFacts>>`, read once the repository opens and again on `Ctrl+R`), and `update::remote::RemoteState` (m4-remotes/05).
- `AppState::harness` (field) — `Harness`: a `banner` pinned under the header (its rows come off `graph_rows` and the mouse row math) and `hand_back` keys that end the run with `Quit::HandedBack(key)`; never while the search prompt is open (m1-the-graph/35, 38).
- `Harness` (struct) — `banner: Option<Banner>`, `hand_back: Vec<KeyCode>`, `clock: Clock`, `offer_commit_graph: bool`; `Default` asks for nothing (`Clock::Wall`, offer on; the gallery turns the offer off). `banner_rows()` is the rows the banner takes, 0 without one. `prelude: Vec<KeyPress>` (m2/23): keys pressed for the user before the first frame, fed one at a time by `update::prelude` once the first window, the refs answer and the working-tree status are on hand and nothing an earlier key started is in flight — the radar context, a search, the pane's file list or diff, and since m3/07 an operation (`operating`), a journal or stash read and the commit editor's prefill, and since m5/11 a Ghost Preview whose plan is still `Pending`; empty in the binary.
- `Banner` (struct) — `title: String` (the view appends ` · <tier>` for the tier in force), `lines: Vec<String>`; `rows()` is `1 + lines.len()` (m1-the-graph/38).
- `AppState::stash` (field) — `update::stash::StashState`: the `S` overlay's list, cursor, file preview and save prompt (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `AppState::status` (field) — what the status line has to say.
- `AppState::size` (field) — terminal's last reported size, in character cells.
- `AppState::repo_path` (field) — repo path; starting point, filled in by `event_loop::run`.
- `AppState::repository` (field) — repository facts once the opening job answers.
- `AppState::system_git` (field) — system git check result once that job answers.
- `AppState::commit_graph` (field) — `CommitGraphOffer`. Facts arriving only take the offer away (only from `NotNeeded`/`Offered`); it is raised by `update::commit_graph::sort_landed` when this run's topological sort, taken up, took `SLOW_SORT` (300 ms) or more (m7-power-tools/15).
- `update::commit_graph::offer_after_sort(fact, sort_took) -> bool` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — pure: `Absent { declined: false }` and `sort_took >= SLOW_SORT`. `SLOW_SORT` (const) 300 ms.
- `update::commit_graph::offer_keys_live(state) -> bool` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — the offer's `B`/`X` answer: actionable and `graph_has_focus`. Read by `commit_graph_key` and by `commit_graph_line` (`CommitGraphLine::Offered { keys }`); in the pane `B` is blame (m7-power-tools/36).
- `SortedOrder` (struct) — `Product::TopologicalOrder`'s payload: `order`, and `took: Duration`, timed in the sort job (`jobs::runner::promote_history`).
- `AppState::commit_graph_line` (fn) — the offer's footer line, if shown (waits for the git check; no usable git → `NoGitToBuild`); `graph_rows` subtracts its row.
- `CommitGraphOffer` (enum) — `NotNeeded`, `Offered` (`B` → `Command::WriteCommitGraph`, `X` → `Command::DeclineCommitGraph`), `Writing(Option<JobId>)`, `Written { commits }` (cleared by the next key), `Declining(Option<JobId>)`, `Declined`, `Failed` (problem line; no retry this run), `Suppressed`. Ids are `None` until `Started` (m1-the-graph/54).
- `AppState::awaiting` (field) — the history walk the screen is waiting for; stale answers dropped. **Set when `update` asks for the walk (`Command::ReadHistory`), never first on its `Started` event** (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/05): learning the id late left a window in which a second `Ctrl+R` had no name to cancel, so the walk it replaced stayed parked on a worker and two of those filled the pool. Every `Started` handler for a walk checks `id == awaiting` and drops anything else.
- `AppState::promoting` (field) — the topological sort still wanted; any other sort's order is dropped.
- `AppState::search_prompt` (field) — the `/` prompt's typed text while open; every key but Ctrl-C is text.
- `AppState::search` (field) — the running or finished search, its footer line; Esc cancels a running one (restoring the selection), then closes it.
- `AppState::history` (field) — the history walk shown on screen, if one was asked for.
- `AppState::refs` (field) — `Option<Arc<RefBadges>>`: refs on each commit, once the refs job answers; shared so the per-event state clone never copies them (m1-the-graph/69).
- `AppState::refs_read` (field) / `RefsRead` (enum: `Unknown` / `Pending(JobId)` / `Current(JobId)`) — whether `refs` answers the last refs read asked. `asked(id)` beside each `Command::ReadRefs` (the launch in `init`, `keys::refresh_key`); `answered(id, ok)` in `took_fact` where the refs land. An older answer over a current one → `Unknown`. Read by `push_params` and `remote_failed`, and by the harness waits (`prelude::settled`, the walks' `ready`) (m6-conflicts/49).
- `AppState::working_tree` (field) — `Option<Arc<graft_git::status::WorkingTreeStatus>>`, full working-tree status once that job answers (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04): shared so the changes pane, the header counts and the uncommitted row read the same `Arc` rather than three copies.
- `AppState::operating` (field) — `Option<JobId>`, the write the screen is waiting for. **Set only by `operate::claim_write`**, which refuses in words while it is `Some` (`wait for <running> to finish`) — one write at a time, enforced where the slot is taken (m6-conflicts/78, Decided 3). Some keys still check first and stay silent (`p`, `u`, stash save; pre-existing).
- `AppState::unseen` (field, `pub(crate)`) — `Option<Status>`: a failure that arrived while a modal owned the bottom row and was then replaced before the row drew it (m6-conflicts/78, Decided 1). Set and cleared only by `update::keep_unseen_failure`; `Esc` with no modal up dismisses it (`keys::dismiss_unseen`) before anything else. Two failures under one box: only the later is held.
- `AppState::modal_owns_bottom_row` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — true while the credential prompt, the name prompt, a confirm box or the Ghost Preview is open. Moved from `view::status` (m6-conflicts/78), unchanged; every bottom-row rung asks it, and it hides the status, never clears it.
- `AppState::shown_status` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `&Status` the rungs draw once no modal owns the row: `unseen` while it stands, else `status`. Every drawn status reader goes through it (`view::status`, `frame::bars::footer_notice`, `view::stash`, `view::commit_editor`).
- `AppState::commit_editor` (field) — `Option<CommitEditor>`, the open commit/amend editor; modal, so nothing behind it takes a key (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05).
- `AppState::commit_draft` (field) — `Option<CommitEditor>`, what `Esc` put aside, so reopening with `c` brings the typed message back; cleared once a commit lands.
- `AppState::external_editor` (field) — `Option<JobId>`, the `$EDITOR` handover in flight; an answer under any other id is dropped, so `Esc` in between cannot let the editor overwrite a retyped draft.
- `AppState::suspend_on_ctrl_z` (field) — `[keys] suspend_on_ctrl_z` (ADR-0002): `Ctrl+Z` stops the process instead of undoing. Off by default.
- `AppState::keymap` (field, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b) — `Arc<graft_ui::keymap::Keymap>` (m8/10: `SharedKeymap` removed, `Keymap` now derives `Clone + Debug + Eq`): his `[keys]` remaps, read by the edge and by every hint bar, menu and footer the view draws. Default = the default keymap.
- `AppState::config_problems` / `config_notice` (fields, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b) — the config file's problems as words (`keymap_from`'s second half), listed by F2's `view::status::notes`; `config_notice` keeps the bottom row's `config: N problem(s) · ? shows them` (`view::frame::bars::config_notice`, muted, after `interrupted_notice`; a failure wins) until the first key or click (`keys::acknowledge`). Never drawn with zero problems.
- `AppState::seed_keys` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b) — `(&mut self, Arc<Keymap>, Vec<String>)`: startup's one write of the keymap and the problems; raises `config_notice` only when there are problems.
- `update::edge` (mod, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b, ADR-0055 §3–4) — the keymap's edge, `core_event`'s `Key` arm: `key(next, commands, pressed)` — a text field open (`text_field_open`) or a chord `from_press` cannot name → `dispatch_key` as pressed; else `translate(context_of(state), chord)`: `Pass` → as pressed, `Default(d)` → `d.to_press()` (the harness's `hand_back` still reads the raw press), `Unbound` → what a key nobody answers does (`keys::before_any_key`, a badge drag dropped, the `.` menu closed in `Menu`; nothing else). Dispatchers below see only default chords and are unedited.
- `update::edge::context_of` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&AppState) -> ContextId`: the context the claim chain would hand a key to, in its order — credential, drag (Graph), confirm, preview, rewrite editor, finish, resolver, picker, focused banner, blame, history, `.` menu, commit editor, then J / stash / Map only while the graph has focus, then Changes (working-tree pane focused) or Diff (another pane focused), else Graph. `Palette` while the palette is up (second, after credential; m8/11). Never `Tour`/`EmptyState`.
- `update::dispatch_key` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress)` → `()` (the old `Key` arm's effect: state and commands): a key past the edge, untranslated — the palette's (ticket 11) way to run an action's default chord. Internally `dispatch(next, commands, key, raw)`.
- `update::keys::before_any_key` (fn, `pub(super)`, m8-09b) — `(&mut AppState, &KeyPress) -> bool`: what every key does before it is answered (`dismiss_unseen`, `acknowledge`); `true` = the key was spent. `key_event` and the edge's unbound key share it.
- `AppState::line_landing` (field) — `Option<update::detail::LineLanding>`: a `show_line` still waiting on the seek, the file list or the diff (m7-power-tools/20b).
- `AppState::on_changes_pane` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> bool`; the pane is shown and its spec is `DetailSpec::WorkingTree` — the only place the write keys mean anything (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04). The footer also asks whether the pane has the keyboard before hinting them: `changes_pane_write_key` refuses every one of them with `DetailFocus::Graph` (m5/31).
- `view::rows::row_verbs` (fn, private, m7-power-tools/40) — the cursor row's `RowVerbs`: `NONE` unless the graph has focus, the cursor is off the working tree and no operation is live; `take` = `update::can_take`, `amend` = cursor is `HEAD`'s oid and `!amend_blocked`. Read only when a drawn cursor row exists (`hint_context`); `footer_context` with no rows answers `PlainRow { verbs: NONE }`. `bound.rs` gains `("o", "take")`; `a amend` left `update::tests::NEVER_HINTED`.
- `AppState::graph_has_focus` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> bool`; `detail.focus == DetailFocus::Graph`. The one answer to "does the graph have the keyboard", read by `S`, `J`, `M` and `O` (each opens something standing where the graph stands) and by `view::rows::hint_context`, which is the point: the four keys were guarded four times over and the bar drew them anyway (m5/31).
- `AppState::graph_overlay` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<GraphOverlay>`; which overlay stands where the graph would be, in the frame's own precedence: `Finish` (`finish.is_some()`, m6-conflicts/19), then `Resolver` (`resolver.is_some()`; first, because it covers the detail pane too and `⏎` on a `CONFLICTS` row can fire with the Map, the journal or the stash list up), `CommitEditor` (`commit_editor.is_some()`), `Map`, `Journal`, `Stash`. The **one enumeration** of them: `view::frame::draw_screen` matches on it and `update::banner::graph_screen_on_frame` asks it, so a fifth overlay is a fifth variant and the frame stops compiling until it is drawn, while the banner needs no edit (review M3, m5/31).
- `GraphOverlay` (enum) — `Finish` (first: the finish summary, m6-conflicts/19) | `Resolver` | `CommitEditor` | `Map` | `Journal` | `Stash` | `Place` (a shown blame place, m7-power-tools/20e; comes after `Finish` and `Resolver`) | `Rewrite` (`rewrite.editor.is_some()`, m7-power-tools/08; after `Place`, before `CommitEditor`; drawn by `view::frame::overlays::draw_rewrite` (08-b)); an overlay that replaces the graph screen and carries no operation banner of its own.
- `AppState::confirm` (field) — `Option<update::confirm::PendingConfirm>`, the discard confirm box; modal like the context menu (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04).
- `AppState::credential` (field) — `Option<update::credential::CredentialPrompt>`, git's credential question as an in-app prompt; modal over everything, including the confirm box (m4-remotes/06).
- `AppState::screen` (field) — which screen is on show; graph by default.
- `AppState::lanes_expanded` (field) — `+` pressed: lanes draw unfolded; a view flag, the rows are untouched.
- `AppState::focus` (field) — Focus mode (`F`; `Esc` leaves it before cancelling anything): rows off the selection's thread fade; paint only.
- `AppState::hover` (field) — the absolute row under the pointer, set by mouse movement; drives the footer reveal when set (m1-the-graph/41). The `Enter` peek is gone: `Enter` opens the detail pane (ADR-0023).
- `AppState::detail` (field) — `DetailState`, the detail pane; hidden by default (m2-details-and-diffs/06).
- `AppState::local_jobs` (field) — `LocalJobs`, ids for the jobs `update` names itself (the detail pane's reads).
- `AppState::on_working_tree` (field) — the uncommitted-changes row's one-bit selection, beside `History`'s cursor rather than a position of it (m2-details-and-diffs/06b); rules in `update::selection`.
- `AppState::compare_base` (field) — `Option<update::detail::CompareBase>`, the pinned base of a live compare (`x`, m2-details-and-diffs/11); set/cleared by `update::detail::toggle_compare`, followed by `update::selection::follow_selection`.
- `AppState::stash_only` (field) / `StashOnlyMarks` (struct, re-exported at `update`) — which commits only a stash reaches (m7-power-tools/16): `job: Option<JobId>` (the read in flight; any other id's answer is dropped), `commits: Arc<HashMap<Oid, usize>>` (commit → newest `stash@{n}` reaching it; kept while the next read runs, emptied by a refs read with no stash or a failed read); `entry(id)`. Set by `update::stash_entries_read` after every `Product::Refs(Ok)` with ≥ 1 stash (`Command::ReadStashOnly`), landed by `update::stash_only_answered` (failure → `Status::Failed("couldn't read which commits only a stash holds: …")`). Drawn by `view::rows::mark_stash_only`.
- `AppState::context_menu` (field) — `Option<update::context_menu::OpenMenu>`: the `.`/right-click popup, if one is open — the row it opened on (`update::context_menu::WORKING_TREE_ROW` for the uncommitted-changes row), the `ContextMenu` the widget draws, and the `HistoryVerb`s its `History(i)` items index into (`context-actions-menu`, m2-details-and-diffs/12; the verbs, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/08 piece 6).
- `AppState::radar` (field) — `update::radar::RadarState`: the Mistake Radar's default-branch context and `[radar]` policy (m2-details-and-diffs/13, 13b).
- `AppState::radar_column_seen` (field) — `Cell<bool>`, sticky once any row's marker has been `Some` this session; keeps the marker column showing after the row scrolls off-screen (m2-details-and-diffs/13b).
- `AppState::map` (field) — `update::map::MapState`: the Map view's visibility and its build, if any (m2-details-and-diffs/18).
- `AppState::last_focus_refresh` (field) — `Option<i64>`, Unix milliseconds: when the last focus-gained working-tree read ran. Terminals report focus in pairs and tmux reports it per pane switch, so reports inside 500 ms are ignored (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `AppState::journal` (field) — `update::journal::JournalState`: the undo journal as this run has read it, and the browser's own cursor (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `update::journal` (m7/24-a) — J's list is `graft_ops::journal_list::build`'s, rebuilt as each stage lands: `RowKey` (enum: `Graft(EntryId)`, `Outside { refname, old, new, at }`, `Lost(Oid)`, `LostEdit(EntryId)`), `row_key`, `index_of`, `row_commit` (what `b` names), `preselect(list, is_fetch)` (the newest `✗` under a `$` newer than every non-fetch `●`), `rebuild(state)` (`pub(crate)`: fixtures that set `entries` call it), `outside_landed` (stage 2; `Ready` asks `Command::FindLost` with its records, a failure sets `lost` `Failed("couldn't read git's own records: …")`) / `lost_landed` (stage 3) / `recover_answered` (`⏎`'s answer), `journal_enter` (stage 1 now; asks `ReadJournal` + `ReadOutside`), `close` (cancels stages 2/3 and a `RecoverFor` still out). Since m7/30o (`pub(crate)`): `undo_from_j` (the one `u`/`⏎` undo from J, closes J on `Launched` only), `not_undone_kind(entry)` (`Some("fetch"|"push"|…)` where `graft_ops::journal::undoable` is false: `⏎` refuses and the bar offers no `⏎`), `switched_from(subject) -> Option<SwitchTarget>` (the one `checkout: moving from` parse, used by `⏎` and the bar). `⏎` on a `$`/`✗`/lost-edit row asks `Command::RecoverFor` with the list on screen. `JournalState`: `cursor`/`expanded` are `Option<RowKey>`; `selected()` (list index), `selected_graft_row()` (index into `newest_first`, what `view::journal` draws), `is_expanded(&EntryId)`, `finding_lost()` (`pub`: stage 2 or 3 still out — the title's `finding lost commits…`); `outside`, `lost`, `list`, `offered`, `recovering`, `touched`.
- `update::journal::find` (m7/30o-c) — J's `/` find, key for key as blame's (§2.8, not incremental): `JournalFind { typing: Option<String>, kept: Option<String>, hits: Vec<usize> }` (`JournalState.find`); `hits(&JournalState, query) -> Vec<usize>` (list indices of rows whose naming words hold it — `●` description via one `HashMap<&EntryId, &str>` per call, `$` words/git subject/ref short name, `✗` sha7/subject/branch, lost edit paths; never verdict words; blank → empty); `refresh_hits(&mut JournalState)` (m7/30p-d: recomputes `find.hits` from `kept`; called by `⏎`, `Esc` over a kept find and `rebuild` only); `count` (`at`, `of` for the title, as blame's `FindTitle`; reads the cached `hits`, `partition_point` on the selection — no list scan per frame or per `n`); `typing(&AppState)` (asked by `text_field_open`); `find_key(&mut AppState, KeyPress) -> bool` (run by `update::core_event` before the graph search: prompt keys, `/`, `n`/`N` and `Esc` over a kept find, graph focus and no modal only); `find_paste` (the paste router, after `name_prompt_paste`).
- `update::recover` (m7/24-a) — a put-back's `Done`: `done_status(params, subject_of) -> Status` (`put back ✓ · main has "S" again · u undoes`, `spike is back at "S" ✓`, `your edit to p is back ✓`), `put_back_tags(params)` (`PutBack` / `WasHere`), `tag_rows` (called by `operated`'s `Done`), `subject_in` (history window, else J's lost row). 24-a2, the put-back box's words: `preview_title(params, subject_of)` (`Preview: put main back to <sha7> "S"` / `make spike again at …` / `put back your edit to p`), `preview_lines(params, files, subject_of)`, `files_words(&FilesEffect)` (`your files: …`; clauses come back · go · replaced (`what's in p[ +N more] now is replaced`, m7/30o) · still staged (`p stays staged as you had it` / `p +N more stay staged as you had them`, m7/30p R2) · set aside).
- `jobs::journal_list` (m7/24-a) — `LostFound` (struct: `unreached`, `edits`, `scan`, `subjects`; `subject_of(tip)`); `read_outside`, `find_lost`, `recover_for` (`Err` is the status words). `LostFound` is `pub` (it rides in `Product::LostFound`). Dispatched by `runner::{read_outside, find_lost, recover_for}` for `Command::{ReadOutside { id }, FindLost { id, outside }, RecoverFor { id, list, row }}`, answered `Product::{Outside, LostFound, RecoverFor}`, routed in `update::answer`.
- `AppState::live_entries` (field) — `Vec<EntryId>`, the entries git is still part-way through, from the last journal read (`jobs::undo::JournalRead::live`, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/14). Whatever is named here is **not** announced as interrupted and is **not** drawn as torn: it is the banner's, and `c`/`a` end it. Also set straight from an `Operated::Conflicted` answer's `entry` (m6-conflicts/06).
- `AppState::interrupted_edits` (field, 30c) — `Vec<(EntryId, graft_ops::EditsAt)>`: where recovery found an interrupted set-aside's edits (`graft_ops::apply::Recovered::set_aside` via `jobs::undo::JournalRead::interrupted_edits`), merged in by `update::journal::journal_event` from the read that closed the entry; a later read brings none, so it is kept. Read only by `undo::interrupted_notice`.
- `AppState::resuming` (field) — `Option<(JobId, graft_ops::apply::Resume)>`: the resume `operating` waits for, kept until its answer so `resumed_words` knows what was asked. Set only by `banner::resume`; taken by `banner::resumed`, or by `operate::write_job_ended` when the job dies unanswered (m6-conflicts/68).
- `AppState::ended_by_answer` (field) — `bool`: a resume's `Done` cleared `in_progress` on the answer's word and the next working-tree read has not confirmed it yet (`update::undo::operation_ended` / `reread_disagrees`, gap row 569).
- **Answers beat re-reads** (rule, m6-conflicts/06): where a job's own answer already states a fact — a push's tip (`refs_after`), a resume's end, a conflict's entry — `update` takes it from the answer in the same update. The re-read that follows confirms; if it contradicts, the status line says so rather than silently flipping. `ReadWorkingTree`/`WorkingTree` still carry no `JobId`, so a read scheduled *before* the answer can land after it and be taken as the contradiction (named in NOTES of m6-conflicts/06).
- `AppState::strip_shown` (field) — `bool`, off until `O` asked (no config default, M8 polish); the overview strip draws only while this holds and `map.shown` does not (m2-details-and-diffs/20).
- `AppState::uncommitted_row_shown` (fn) — does the working-tree row draw this frame: a dirty tree, and nothing else. Pinned at row 0 from m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04; it used to need `HEAD` on top too, which hid the user's own uncommitted work as soon as they scrolled (closes `KNOWN_GAPS.md` m2/06b). Means the row exists (`k` at the top reaches it); whether it takes a screen row is `uncommitted_row_pinned` (48), which `view::entries::uncommitted_shown` and `view::strips` read.
- `AppState::uncommitted_row_attached` (fn) — is `HEAD` (or an unborn branch) the topmost visible row this frame. It no longer decides whether the row draws, only how: attached it hangs off `HEAD`'s lane, detached it is a lone `◌` with no lane under it (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04).
- `AppState::selected_spec` (fn) — the `DetailSpec` the cursor now names: `WorkingTree` on the flag, else the history cursor's commit.
- `AppState::renderer_override` (field) — the tier-drop key (`F3`) forced this tier for the rest of the run, or `None` for whatever `Session::settle` decided (m1-the-graph/19).
- `AppState::settled_tier` (field) — `Session::settle`'s tier, copied in at loop start; `None` without a session (m1-the-graph/24d).
- `AppState::icons` (field) — the badge glyph set, seeded from `ui.icons` at startup (`config::IconSet::glyphs`). On the state, not the session, because the glyphs decide how wide a badge is and so where a **drag** has to hit it, and the mouse hit-test has no `Session` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33, review M1).
- `AppState::max_lanes` (field) — `graph.max_lanes` as configured, or `None` to let the width breakpoint decide. Resolved per frame by `view::max_lanes`; on the state for the same reason as `icons` — the cap is the graph column's width, and the badges' columns move with it.
- `AppState::tier` (fn) — tier in force: F3's override, else settled, else Unicode. F3 (`cycle_tier`) cycles Pixel→Unicode→Ascii→settled tier, never above it; back at the top the override clears. History pads to strips only at Pixel (m1-the-graph/33).
- `Screen` (enum) — `Graph` (default) or `Status`.
- `Screen::toggled` (fn) — the other screen; `F2` swaps.
- `Quit` (enum) — why Graft is leaving: `Requested`, `HandedBack(KeyCode)`, `Fatal(String)`, `StoppedHalfWay { job, what }` (the stop box's `y`; the loop adds the seconds waited and returns `Exit::StoppedHalfWay`; `job` is the write whose answer its exit line reads, 13d).
- `Quit::should_quit` (fn) — is the loop finished?
- `AppState::text_field_open` (fn) — `(&self) -> bool`: is a text field taking the keyboard (the search prompt, the commit editor, the stash save prompt, the credential prompt, the name prompt, the branch picker while shown)? Every global binding — `u`, `U`, `Ctrl+Z`, `Esc` on a failure — asks this first, so a letter typed into a field cannot fire a keybinding. **A new text field belongs in this list** (m3/06 review, Critical 1). Since m7/30o-c also J's `/` find prompt (`journal::find::typing`). **R4 (m8/09c, ADR-0055):** while it holds, the keymap edge passes every key untranslated — even inside a remappable place (finish Subject/Body, the rewrite editor's `r` field) — and no bar or box footer draws a key his remap moves (`update::tests::keymap_rules::a_text_field_draws_no_key_his_remap_moves`). R4 covers the **bar and footers** (the keys for what is typed now); pane text that describes the place (J's legend, blame's find header) spells his live keys even while the field is open (m8/09d, question 1).
- `AppState::modal_owns_keys` (fn) — `(&self) -> bool`: `text_field_open()`, or a confirm box, a Ghost Preview, the resolver or a focused banner is up — every modal that claims every key but `Ctrl+C` (§2.1). `update::core_event` answers `/`, `n`, `N` as search keys only when this is false or the search prompt itself is open (m6-07 review, Major 1: `/` typed in the commit subject opened a search). Not the Map, journal or stash overlay: their bars offer `/ search`, and it works there. Pinned by `update::search::tests`.
- `Status` (enum) — status-line state; never reads as an error when nothing is wrong. `Copied { short }`: `y` sent the SHA (OSC 52); footer notice until the next key; worded as what was sent (m1-the-graph/45). `Busy(description)`: a write is running — drawn as `<description>…` in the footer, set the moment the key is pressed (`update::operate::launch`), and not wiped by a `JobFinished`. `Done { what, also: Option<String>, keys: Option<String> }`: it finished — drawn `<what> ✓`, then ` · <also>` (a fact that is not a key, `your edits are back`; m6-conflicts/84), then ` · <keys>` when it names the next key (`c finishes the merge`), by `view::status::done_line` alone; key-driven paths (`undo::land_after_undo`) replace `keys` and leave `also`; cleared by the next key like `Copied`. `Status::done(what)` / `Status::done_with_keys(what, keys)` build it. The tick is the renderer's, so the keys are kept apart as `Sticky`'s are and `what` never holds `✓`: glued in, the tick landed after the key (`… · c finishes the merge ✓`, ticket 41). `Waiting(words)`: a key could not act yet because a read it needs is in flight (`still reading the refs/remotes — try again in a moment`, set by `update::remote::refusal`) — a footer notice, never a `problem`; cleared by the next key (review 47-49, m1). `Failed(message)`: a read failed on its own — the graph screen's `problem` line, not the footer; cleared by the next job that works, or by `Esc`. `Sticky { reason, keys: Option<String> }`: **a failure that must outlive the refresh it triggers** — a hook that rejected a commit, a patch git would not apply, an amend whose `HEAD` message could not be read, an `$EDITOR` that would not start. **Never written by a key that leaves a modal open** (m6-conflicts/69, Decided 3): the name prompt's empty-name refusal moved to `NamePrompt::refusal`; while a modal is open every bottom-row rung hides the status (`AppState::modal_owns_bottom_row`), and `esc` shows it again; a failure that lands under the modal and is replaced there is held in `AppState::unseen` (m6-conflicts/78). `Status::is_failure` (fn, `pub(crate)`): `Failed | Sticky`. Drawn the same as `Failed`, but a `JobFinished` never clears it: the refresh the failure itself set off would otherwise wipe the reason a moment after it appeared (m3/04 review). Only `Esc` dismisses it. Named `WriteFailed` until the m3/05 review, which is what three of its uses are not. Reshaped from a single `Sticky(String)` in `hint-bar-redesign` (ADR-0037 §4): `keys`, when present, is the line's key hints (`"p pull first · P force with lease"`), kept apart from `reason` so `graph_screen::hints::fit_status_line` can cut the reason with `…` and keep the keys whole — a blind right-cut once lost `P force` at 80 columns. `Status::sticky(reason)` is the plain constructor (`keys: None`); `update::remote::remote_failed` still builds its combined `"reason · keys"` string and splits it once, downstream, since the convention is `ops`'s (`split_reason_and_keys`) — not worth a shape change in `graft-ops`. `view::status::problem` still joins the two back for the status screen/stash overlay (single-line contract); `view::status::graph_problem` (returns `graft_ui::graph_screen::ProblemLine`) is the graph footer's own reading, kept apart.
- `Size` (struct) — terminal size in character cells.
- `Size::columns` (field) — width in character cells.
- `Size::rows` (field) — height in character cells.
- `Command` (enum) — effects `update` asks the loop to carry out; `ReadHistory(JobId, Order)` starts the history walk under an id `update` chose (`queue_as`), so `awaiting` can name it before the pool reports it (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/05); `ShowRows(JobId, RowRequest)` asks a history job for rows; `ReadRefs(JobId)` reads refs and ahead/behind off the loop's thread under an id `update` chose, so a finished `--continue` can wait on its own read (m6-conflicts/12); `ReadWorkingTree(JobId)` reads full working-tree status off the loop's thread under an id `update` chose (`queue_as`), built only by `update::status_read::ask` (m6-conflicts/26); `PromoteHistory` sorts a provisional walk's range topologically; `Search(Query)` starts a search job. `ListFiles(JobId, DiffSpec, DiffOptions)`, `ReadFileDiff(JobId, DiffKey, FileEntry)`, `ContainedIn(JobId, Oid)`, `EvilProbe(JobId, Oid)` run the detail pane's reads under ids `update` chose (`CancelJob` stops them). `CopyToClipboard(String)` is terminal output the loop writes itself (through the session's tmux transport; a write error ends the loop), never dispatched to jobs (m1-the-graph/45). `SaveLayout(PaneSize)` is written to disk by the loop itself (`crate::layout::save_split`); a failure is expected and non-fatal (m2-details-and-diffs/10b). `BuildMap(JobId, MapRequest)` runs the Map view's build under an id `update` chose itself, like the detail pane's own jobs (m2-details-and-diffs/18). `Operate(JobId, Box<graft_ops::operation::Operation>)` runs one working-tree write under an id `update` chose — `jobs::operate::run_operation` opens the journal and calls `graft_ops::apply::apply`; the seam rule (`tests/it/seam_rule.rs`) keeps every other write detail out of `app` (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04). Boxed like `Product::Operated` for the same enum-size lint. `Undo(JobId, bool)` and `Redo(JobId, bool)` run one undo or redo under an id `update` chose — the `bool` is "the drift guard was answered, go anyway"; `ReadJournal(JobId)` reads the journal, and `ReadStashes(JobId)` reads the stash list behind the `S` overlay (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). `ReadSwitchList(JobId)` reads the branch picker's rows (m7-power-tools/10; dispatched by `jobs::runner::read_switch_list`, answered `Product::SwitchList`). `OpenExternalEditor { path, line }`, `EditCommitMessage { id, message }` and `Suspend` are carried out by the frame loop itself, not the job pool: each hands the terminal over and takes it back, which only the thread owning the session and the input mux can do (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05). `ReadRemotes(JobId)` reads the remote facts under an id `update` chose (m4-remotes/05). `AnswerCredential(JobId, update::credential::Answer)` hands one typed secret to the waiting job and `CancelCredential(JobId)` tells it nobody will answer; both are delivered by `JobRunner`, never by a worker, and the `Answer` is the only value in `Command` carrying a secret (m4-remotes/06). `ReadConflictSet(JobId)` reads the resolver's file list (`jobs::conflict::conflict_set` → `graft_ops::conflict::operation_conflicts`, m6-conflicts/13). `LoadBase(JobId, PathBuf, Box<ConflictFile>)` runs the resolver's `B` under its own id (`jobs::conflict::load_base` → `graft_ops::conflict::base_for`), never `job`'s id — a base load must not race an open read or a take answer for the same document (m6-conflicts/05). `ReadFinishSummary(JobId)` reads the finish summary (`jobs::finish::summary` → `graft_ops::finish::finish_summary`) and `ReadFinishDiff(JobId, PathBuf)` one file's staged diff (`jobs::finish::staged_diff`), both under ids `update` chose (m6-conflicts/19). `Unstoppable(JobId)` (m8-13b): marks a `Stoppable::No` write before its command, so no cancel or shutdown signals it (`JobRunner::unstoppable`).
- `Command::ReadStashOnly(JobId, Vec<Oid>)` / `Task::StashOnly` / `Product::StashOnly(Result<Arc<graft_git::history_reads::StashOnly>, String>)` — the stash-only read (m7-power-tools/16): every stash entry's own commit, `stash@{0}` first; run by `jobs::stash_only::read_stash_only` (`only_in_stash`, `CAP` 500) as a cancellable detail-style job under `update`'s id.
- `CHROME_ROWS` (const) — screen rows that are not the graph without a harness banner below 30 rows; `graft_ui::graph_screen::CHROME_ROWS`. `AppState::graph_pane_rows` reads `graft_ui::graph_screen::chrome_rows(size.rows)` (4 from 30 rows: blank row and rule, m8-polish-and-ship/06) and adds `Harness::banner_rows`; `update::geometry::header_rows` (header + blank row) is what `rows_above_graph` and `picker_box_in` read. `view::rows::graph_view` builds the header's `Location` (repo name, `~` path from `$HOME`, branch or `detached <sha7>`) and sets `spaced`.
- `AppState::graph_rows` (fn) — history rows the graph pane shows at the current size: `graph_pane_rows` less the pinned uncommitted row; never zero. Every scroller's viewport height, and exactly what `view::entries`/`view::strips` draw (m7-power-tools/48: they used to subtract the uncommitted row themselves while scrollers did not, so held `j` left the cursor one row below the pane).
- `AppState::graph_pane_rows` (fn) — rows of the graph pane: terminal less chrome, banner, commit-graph line, tabs, preview box, pending block; never zero. Only the mouse's bounds check (`geometry::graph_row_at`) and `ghostty_shot`'s crop read it (48).
- `AppState::uncommitted_row_pinned` (fn) — the uncommitted row takes the pane's top row: `uncommitted_row_shown` and `graph_pane_rows() >= 2`. Every offset of drawn or clicked rows by the uncommitted row reads this (through `view::entries::uncommitted_shown`); a one-row pane is never pinned and draws the cursor's row (48).
- `update` (fn) — the pure state transition function. Its last step, every event, is `keep_unseen_failure(before, next)` (private): a failure replaced under a standing modal goes to `AppState::unseen`; it is dropped once `status` shows it again (m6-conflicts/78).
- `opening` (fn) — `(PathBuf, Size) -> (AppState, Vec<Command>)`: the state Graft starts in and the jobs that fill it (open the repository, check git, walk history, read refs, read the working tree). The real loop and every integration harness go through it, because the history walk's id is minted here and `awaiting` set from it (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/05).
