- `lands_on_head_at_open` (fn, m8-polish-and-ship/08) — arms the owed `HEAD` landing on `opening`'s own refs read and walk: a repository opened from the empty state lands on `HEAD`'s row.

- `AppState::amend_into` (field) — `update::amend_into::AmendInto`: the put-in plan in flight, the planned put-in its preview draws, and the tag read after it lands (m7-power-tools/09).
- `AppState::rewrite` (field) — `update::rewrite_editor::RewriteState`: the `R` read in flight, the editor on screen, and the editor `y` launched until its write answers (m7-power-tools/08). `modal_owns_keys` is true while the editor is up; `text_field_open` while its `r` field is open.
- `AppState::row_tags` (field) — `update::row_tags::RowTags`: which commits carry a `graft_ui::graph_screen::RowTag` after their subject; cleared by `update::operate::claim_write` (m7-power-tools/09).

## update::amend_into
`a` on an older commit and absorb from the `.` menu (m7-power-tools/09).
- `amend_blocked(&AppState) -> bool` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m7-power-tools/40) — `a`'s cheap blocks: a live operation (P14), a pending plan, an open commit editor. `a_key` runs the same checks in the same order; `view::rows::row_verbs` reads this for `a amend`.
- `AmendInto` (struct) — private fields; `shown_for(op)` (`pub(crate)`): the box words when `op` is the planned put-in; `checking()`: a plan job is in flight.
- `a_key` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `a`, from `update::keys::commit::commit_key`: P14's refusal while an operation is live; a second `a` while checking starts nothing; an older commit row → the staged check (`Refusal::NothingStagedFor`'s words), then `Command::PlanRewrite { AmendInto }`; `HEAD`'s row, the uncommitted row, the changes pane → today's amend (`commit_editor::open`) unless refs show another local branch, then `PlanRewrite` with `head` (`checking what's built on <branch>…`). Refs not yet read: every row takes the `HEAD` path (review m7-09..21c Minor 3).
- `reword_confirmed` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-polish-and-ship/14) — the commit editor's `⏎` on a reword it was opened for (`CommitEditor::reword_line`): plans `RewriteRequest::RewordHead { message }`; the preview's `y` runs it. `AmendInto::reword_read_answered(id)` tells `head_message_arrived` the editor is that reword's. `keep_reword(state, editor)` / `kept_reword(state) -> Option<CommitEditor>` (`amend_into/reword.rs`, review m8-14 Majors 1-2): a reword editor closed by `Esc`, refused at plan, or whose preview closed is kept in `AmendInto::reword_draft`, keyed on `HEAD`'s oid — never in `c`'s `commit_draft` — and the next `a` on that same `HEAD` reopens it; cleared on landing. `landed`, `tags_resolved`, `write_claimed` live in `amend_into/landing.rs`.
- `absorb_chosen` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — the `.` menu's `ContextAction::Absorb`: P14's refusal in `a`'s words while an operation is live (review m7-09..21c Minor 4), else `PlanRewrite { Absorb }`.
- `answer` / `planned` / `tags_resolved` (fns, `pub(crate)`) — the plan's answer: the preview (`opened_by` `a` or `.`), a refusal's own words (sticky), or `NothingBuiltOnHead` → today's amend; the tag read's answer sets `RowTag::Changed`, or adds `couldn't mark the changed commit: <why>` to the landing's line.
- `landed` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — from `operate::operated`'s `Done`: only for the very `Operation::Rewrite` planned, the status `<what> ✓ · <also> · u undoes` and `Command::ResolveRowTags`.
- `job_ended` / `forget` (fns, `pub(crate)`) — a plan or tag job that will not answer; the preview closed.
- `write_claimed` (fn, `pub(crate)`, review m7-09-to-21c Minor 2) — called by `operate::claim_write`: a tag read still out is dropped, so its answer after a write tags nothing.

## update::rewrite_editor
The rewrite editor (`R`, m7-power-tools/08, note R1, keymap §2.7). 08-a: state, keys, routing, `y`; 08-b draws it.
- `RewriteState` (struct) — `opening: Option<JobId>`, `editor: Option<Box<RewriteEditor>>` (`Some` ⇔ `GraphOverlay::Rewrite`), `launched` (after `y`); private: the row `R` was on, and stale job ids whose answers are dropped.
- `RewriteEditor` (struct) — private fields (todo, moves, edits by `Oid`, cursor, `r` field, AFTER plan, right side, notice). `params()`: validate, then the op `y` runs / AFTER plans, messages rebuilt per ruling 8. `view_facts()` → `ViewFacts`.
- `ViewFacts<'a>` (struct) — `branch`, `since`, `rows: Vec<RowFacts>` (newest first), `base: &BaseRow`, `cursor` (index into rows; `rows.len()` = base), `field: Option<(screen row, text, caret)>`, `messages: Vec<MessageFacts>`, `after: AfterFacts`, `changes: Option<(Oid, &Loading<Arc<FileList>>)>`, `narrow_shows_right`, `notice`, `handing_over`.
- `RowFacts<'a>` (struct) — `action`, `commit`, `subject` (a reword's edited subject), `moved`, `marked: Option<MarkedBy>` (was `by_name: bool`; any mark of his clears it), `keeps` (`n in one`; 0 when not a kept row), `problem` (`nothing below to meld into`).
- `MessageFacts` (struct) — `kept`, `kept_subject`, `squashed` (group vs reword), `text`.
- `AfterFacts<'a>` (enum) — `Checking` | `Failed(&str)` | `Refused(&str)` | `Invalid(String)` | `Ready(&Planned)`.
- `field_open` (fn) — the `r` field is open (`text_field_open`).
- `MarkedBy` (enum, rebase-preview-keeps-drops) — `Name` (autosquash: view draws `marked by its name`), `AlreadyIn` (a rebase's suggested drop: `already in <base label> · p keeps it`).
- `open_rewrite` (fn) — `R` on the graph: `preview_refusal`'s words, or `Command::OpenRewrite { id, req: Open { from } }` (cursor row; `HEAD` on the uncommitted row) and `reading <branch>…`. A read already out is cancelled (its id goes stale, as `Esc`'s) and the new one starts: the newest `R` wins (30n Q2).
- `open_rewrite_from_rebase` (fn, rebase-preview-keeps-drops) — `(state, commands, next_id, params: RebaseParams)`: `R` in a rebase preview; same guards, words and newest-wins as `open_rewrite`, `Command::OpenRewrite { id, req: FromRebase(params) }`. Its answer opens with every non-pick row `MarkedBy::AlreadyIn` and the cursor on the newest `drop` row.
- `opened` (fn) — the read's answer: the editor and its first AFTER plan, `Refusal::explain` (sticky), or `couldn't open the rewrite editor: <why>`. Never over another box (`keys::edit_stop::no_box_is_up` false): the editor is not set and the status is `Sticky` `rewrite editor not opened · another box was open`, keys `R opens it` (`Open`) or `r then R opens it` (`FromRebase`) — hidden under the box, read when it closes (30n Q2). An editor opened from a rebase with every row still a suggested drop says `nothing kept yet · p keeps a commit` on AFTER and `y`, not `EverythingDropped`'s words (30n Q1); `RewriteEditor::new` starts `After::NotAsked` (drawn checking) or the todo's own `validate` problem (30n m5).
- `owns` (fn) — is `id` one of the editor's jobs (open read, AFTER plan, `⏎` file list, `$EDITOR` handover) or a stale one; `update::update` routes those products to the next four before any shared handler.
- `after_planned` / `files_listed` / `message_returned` / `job_ended` (fns) — answers to its jobs; stale ids dropped.
- `write_answered` (fn) — from `operate::operated`, after its `operating` guard: a refusal restores the editor with `refusal_status`'s words; anything else ends it.
- `rewrite_key` / `rewrite_mouse` (fns) — modal: every key but `Ctrl+C` while up; `Esc` while the read is out cancels it. The mouse is claimed whole (nothing while the field is open): a left press on a row (`graft_ui::rewrite_editor::rewrite_row_at` over `view::rewrite_editor`'s view and area) selects it and starts a drag; a drag moves the cursor's row toward the row under the pointer one swap at a time (the base refuses as `Alt+↓` does) and replans once per drag event; release ends it (08-b).

## update::row_tags
- `RowTags` (struct) — `set(oid, tag)`, `on(oid)`, `clear()` (all `pub(crate)`); `view::entries::row_entry` reads `on` into `GraphEntry::tag`.

## update::selection
The uncommitted-changes row's one-bit selection: pure rules for setting, clearing and following `AppState::on_working_tree` (m2-details-and-diffs/06b).
- `move_from_graph` (fn) — a graph motion key, before `History::move_cursor` sees it: sets or clears the flag, returns the motion still left for `History` (or `None`).
- `reconcile` (fn) — called once at the end of every event with the history cursor's value from the start of it; clears the flag when the row stopped showing or the cursor moved under it; returns whether it cleared.
- `follow_selection` (fn) — a shown, non-`Compare` pane follows the selection onto or off the working-tree row; while `AppState::compare_base` is pinned it instead follows the cursor as a live compare against that base (fresh `head` each move, base kept), a no-op back on the base's own row (m2-details-and-diffs/11).

## update::context_menu
The `.`/right-click popup: opening it, its own keys, and carrying out whatever it dispatches (`context-actions-menu`, m2-details-and-diffs/12).
Tests live in `context_menu/tests.rs`, not inline, and hold the fixture builders both test files read (`ref_badges`; m5-history-and-ghost-preview/17, review Major 6). Public items unchanged by the split.
- `open_for_history_row`/`open_for_working_tree`/`open_selected` (fns) — open the menu for a given row, the working-tree row, or whatever the cursor now names. `marked` asks `view::radar::findings_for` for the row under the cursor — the same source of truth the marker column reads (`explain-reachable-from-the-menu`, m2-details-and-diffs/24) — not a hand-maintained flag.
- `context_menu_key` (fn) — the menu's own keys while open. It claims **exactly four** and reports `true` for them, which is what makes `key_event` return: `j`/`k` (and the arrows) move, `Enter` dispatches and closes, `Esc` closes without acting. Every other key closes the menu and reports **`false`**, so it falls through the rest of `key_event` and means what it always means — a trailing `(key)` on a label is a promise the letter is live right now (`docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.1: an overlay that needs only a cursor is not a modal pane). Pinned by `update::context_menu::tests::context_menu_key_closes_and_hands_a_verb_key_on` and `it::preview::every_verb_the_menu_advertises_is_live_while_it_is_open` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/13).
- `apply_context_action` (fn) — `(state, action, row, oid: Option<Oid>, verbs: &[HistoryVerb], next_id) -> Vec<Command>`; `oid` is `None` only for the working-tree row, where `CopySha`/`CompareWithThis`/`Explain` are unreachable (`debug_assert!`, no command). `Fetch`/`Pull`/`Push`/`Undo` call the very functions `f`/`p`/`P`/`u` call (`remote::fetch_key`, `remote::pull_key`, `remote::push_key`, `undo::undo_key`), proved equivalent by `update::context_menu::tests::the_menu_runs_what_the_dropped_keys_run`. `History(i)` opens the Ghost Preview of `verbs[i].op` (`PreviewSubject::Operation { opened_by: "." }`) and runs nothing — `.` ends in a picture (PROMPT.md §1.7) — **except Checkout**, which goes through `update::switch::switch_to` (m7-power-tools/10b): at once on a clean tree, the preview with edits or a detached target.
- `OpenMenu` (struct) — `{ row, menu, verbs }`: one answer to one `.`, kept in one field so the labels on screen and the operations behind them cannot drift apart.
- `HistoryVerb` (struct, re-exported from `verbs`) — `{ label: SanitizedText, op: graft_ops::operation::Operation }`.

## update::context_menu::slots
Where the `.` menu's history verbs sit (m6-conflicts/71; moved out of `verbs` in m6-conflicts/76). Builds the verbs and their slots in one pass from `verbs`' per-verb builders.
- `history_slots` (fn), `Slots` (struct, `pub(crate)`; `Slots::empty()`) — the verbs in menu order plus `leading` (2: Checkout then Cherry-pick go above `Explain` and the row's own actions; 1 when slot 1 is dim) and `dim_first` (`(label, reason)`: `Checkout` / `already on <b>` on the `HEAD` row whose only local branch is the current one, named by `verbs::checkout_verbs`' own answer; no verb, dispatches nothing). A stash row keeps the pre-71 order, `leading` 0 (conductor 2026-09-25: not a daily task).
- `Slots::dimmed` (field) + `Slots::menu_items()` (fn) — m7-power-tools/11: `(index into verbs, reason)` for the one verb drawn dim in its place — slot 2 `Cherry-pick (o)` where `o` would refuse (`verbs::pick_slot`); `menu_items` pairs every label with its dim for `ContextMenu::for_row`. Held on `Slots`, not `HistoryVerb`, because `update::drag` builds `HistoryVerb`s too. The stash-row Cherry-pick is `pick_verb`, no `(o)`, never dim.

## update::context_menu::verbs
The history verbs behind `.` on a commit row, and the operation each one means (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/08 piece 6). Pure: no `AppState` write, no preview, no write.
- `take_check(id, parents, &Tip) -> Result<(), Refusal>` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m7-power-tools/40) — the one take predicate (`PickParams::check_commit`): `pick_slot` reads its `Err` for slot 2's dim words. `can_take(&AppState) -> bool` (fn, `pub`, re-exported at `update::can_take`) — the cursor's history row is a non-stash commit, `HEAD` has a tip and `take_check` is clear: the bar's `o take`.
- `history_verbs` (fn, test-only since m6-conflicts/76) — `(id, parents, subject, &RefBadges, &[Finding]) -> Vec<HistoryVerb>`, `slots::history_slots(..).verbs`: the flat list, in menu order (m6-conflicts/71): the first `Checkout <b>` for a local badge `HEAD` is not on (`Checkout <sha7>, detached` on a row with no local badge), then `Cherry-pick <sha7> (o)` (m7-power-tools/11; dim `Cherry-pick (o)` where `o` refuses), then any further `Checkout <b>`, then `Merge <b> into <cur> (m)` and `Rebase <cur> onto <b> (r)` — with Cherry-pick's `(o)`, the only labels that name a key, because only those keys exist (the very operations `m`/`r` build — same `preview::source_at`/`current_branch`, absent on a detached `HEAD` and on the current branch's own tip), `Revert <sha7>` (`, -m 1` on a merge), `Reset <cur> to here, mixed`/`, hard`, `Delete branch <b>` (never the current one), `Delete tag <t>`, then the three Fix… items a Foxtrot finding earns, all three stated against the finding's `real_tip`; `Fix: rebase instead` only while `HEAD` is on the merge. Empty on an unborn `HEAD`; the Fix items are absent when `real_tip` is not among the merge's parents. **A trailing `(key)` is reserved for keys that are live right now** (`docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.1): a qualifier that is not a key — a reset mode, `detached`, `-m 1` — is written after a comma, never parenthesised (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/12; they wore the key shape until then). **This is the boundary the `CheckoutTarget`/`ResetMode` mapping lives at** — `graft-ui` sees only an index and a `SanitizedText` label (ARCHITECTURE.md, `DESIGN.md` §8.2).
- `pick_params(id, subject, &Tip) -> PickParams` (fn, `pub(in crate::update)`) — m7-power-tools/11: the one builder `o` (`preview::take_key`) and slot 2 share. `pick_slot` (private to the menu) — slot 2 on a commit row: `Cherry-pick <sha7> (o)`, or `Cherry-pick (o)` with `o`'s refusal words as its dim reason (`already on <b>` on `HEAD`'s row, `a merge · taking a merge isn't in Graft yet` on 2+ parents), decided by `PickParams::check_commit` from the row alone — an ancestor of `HEAD` stays live (its plan refuses). `head_tip` is `pub(in crate::update)` since 11 (`take_key` reads it).

## update::operate
- `Landing` (enum, `pub(crate)`, m7/24-a) — `Head` (default), `Commit(Oid)`, `Changes(PathBuf)`; `landing_of(op) -> Option<Landing>`: `moves_head` → `Head`; a put-back → its commit, or its first file on the changes pane; `BranchCreate` → its commit; the rest `None` (listed). Spent by `Done`, `StagingNotKept` and (m8/13 ruling 3) `Incomplete`, which lands and tags as `Done` and keeps its sticky `<desc> — could not confirm it fully: <error>`. A `Pull` whose entry reads equal before/after on `HEAD`, refs, index and worktree (`pulled_nothing`) lands nowhere and says `up to date ✓` (`UP_TO_DATE`, m8/13 ruling 1).
Working-tree writes from the changes pane (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04). Which region has focus decides the unit: in the file list `Space`/`s`/`d` mean the whole file (or every file of a section heading), in the diff region the hunk or line under the line cursor. **Section membership decides direction** — a row under STAGED unstages, anything else stages — because a path that is staged *and* unstaged has a row in both groups with identical status flags. `Ctrl+A` stages every path. Seam rule: builds an `Operation` and a `Command::Operate` from it, nothing else — `graft_ops` decides what the write does.
- `refusal_status` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&Operation, &Refusal, columns: u16) -> Status` (`columns` m7/30t: the status row's width, `state.size.columns` at every caller; the rewrite editor's notice row passes the same, since the editor fills the screen). `InTheWay` → `Sticky { reason: the longest `explain_rungs` rung whose `reason · keys` fits `columns - 1` (the graph footer's text; J's full-width row fits it too), else the last; keys: the next step, plus ` · esc dismiss` when it names no key (an ignored file) }`; chosen once, a resize keeps it. `history_refusal` routes a write-time `InTheWay` here too (was `explain()`'s one string). Otherwise: the one wording of a refusal of an operation he asked for, preview or write (m6-conflicts/67). `DirtyTree` → `<verb> needs a clean tree · S stash N changed file(s)` (verb from `describe`); anything else `Refusal::explain`. Callers: `update::preview`'s `Refused` answer, and `history_refusal` for the write (op from `Running::op`).
- `done_status` (private) — since m7-power-tools/11 a one-commit `CherryPick` lands as `took "<subject>" onto <branch>` with keys `u undoes` (and `your edits are back` in `also` when set aside); the journal keeps `describe_pick`'s `cherry-pick <sha7> "…"`.
- `operation_in_flight` (fn) — `AppState::operating.is_some()`; the early guard many write keys still run.
- `InFlight` (struct, `pub(crate)`) + `InFlight::words` — `running: String` (the running write's description, or `the write`); `words()` is `wait for <running> to finish`, **the one wording** `f`, `preview_refusal`, the name prompt and every refused launch use (m6-conflicts/78).
- `in_flight` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&AppState) -> Option<InFlight>`: the write running now.
- `claim_write` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(&mut AppState, JobId) -> Result<(), InFlight>`: **the only setter of `AppState::operating`**; writes nothing on `Err`. `launch_with`, `undo::launch`, `undo::revert_to_forced`, `banner::resume` and the preview's `RevertTo` confirm call it.
- `say_if_refused` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(&mut AppState, Result<(), InFlight>)`: `Err` → `Status::Failed(busy.words())`, for a caller whose modal (if any) closed on the key.
- `launch` / `launch_unstoppable` (fn, `pub(super)`, `#[must_use]`) — return `Result<(), InFlight>` (m6-conflicts/78), so the compiler lists every caller; each words an `Err`. Every `Operation` launched from a key or a box passes `launch_with`: one job id, `Busy`, `Running`, one `Command::Operate`. No branch check for a push: `graft_ops::apply`'s `check_push` refuses a push of a branch `HEAD` is not on (`Refusal::BranchChanged`) against a fresh `HEAD` read — gap row 550 closed on that line (m6-06 review Major 1). `launch_with` sets `AppState::lands_on_head` to the job id when `op.moves_head()` (m7-power-tools/03).
- `stage_selected` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, next_id) -> bool`; `Space`/`s` in the file list: stages or unstages the cursor's file — or every file under the section heading the cursor is on, stopping at the next heading. Direction comes from the row's `WorkingTreeSection`, never from the file's status flags; `false` (no-op) lets the caller fall back to `Space`'s other meaning (folding a directory row). A `WorkingTreeSection::Unmerged` row answers the key with `conflict_refusal`'s words instead of staging: staging a conflicted path is how git marks it resolved, a different act with its own confirmation (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/04 seam).
- `stage_at_diff_cursor` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, next_id) -> bool`; in `DiffMode::SideBySide` it refuses in words (`press v for the unified view…`) rather than write a hunk under a cursor that view does not draw (m3/04 review M5). `Space`/`s` in the diff region: `Target::Patch` built by `graft_git::diff::hunk_patch` from `DetailState::diff_raw` — the whole hunk on a `@@` heading or a context line, that one line on a `+`/`-` line. `PatchDirection::Forward`/`PatchSide::Worktree` when staging, `Reverse`/`Index` when unstaging. An untracked file stages whole (ADR-0034). A `PatchError` answers the key and sets `Status::Failed` with the reason and the way out — never a key that silently does nothing. Same for a conflicted file: `patch_direction` returns `None` (a file with conflict markers has no clean side to cut a hunk against) and the key is answered with `conflict_refusal`'s words.
- `request_discard_at_diff_cursor` (fn) — `(&mut AppState) -> bool`; refuses in `DiffMode::SideBySide` like `stage_at_diff_cursor`. `d` in the diff region: the confirm box for discarding that hunk or line — buttons `Cancel`/`Discard`, so the question and the button name the same act (m3/04 review M6), the patch always built `Reverse`, the consequence counting the patch's own `+`/`-` lines. Untracked falls back to `request_discard`; a conflicted file is refused in words, like `stage_at_diff_cursor`.
- `stage_all` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, next_id) -> Result<(), NotStaged>` (30n m6; was `bool`); both callers (`Ctrl+A`, edit-stop `space`) put `NotStaged::status()` on the status line. stages every **unstaged or untracked** path `AppState::working_tree` lists. A staged deletion, a staged rename's vanished old name and an unmerged path are skipped: `git add` on the first two is `fatal: pathspec ... did not match any files` (exit 128, nothing staged at all), and on an unmerged path it marks the conflict resolved with the markers still in the file (m3/04 review M2).
- `stalled_line` (private fn) — replaces `conflicted_line` (m6-conflicts/12): the stop itself is `AppState::stopped` and its words `banner::stop_reason`; a `Sticky` is set only when git gave a stalled reason, in git's words. Otherwise `Conflicted` leaves `Status::Ready`. History of the old line:
- `conflicted_line` (removed) — `(&str, graft_git::state::InProgress, &[PathBuf], Option<&str>) -> String`; the status line for an operation git stopped part-way through. Worded as a state, never a failure: what was being done, that the operation is still open, and what is in front of the user. Names no key — `resume` has none yet. One path is named; several point at CONFLICTS; none says so rather than claiming a conflict that is not there (a rebase can stop with nothing unmerged) (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/07).
- `request_discard` (fn) — `(&mut AppState) -> bool`; opens `AppState::confirm` with the exact `git` command a whole-file discard of the cursor's file class runs (`clean -f`, `restore --staged --worktree`, or `restore --worktree`), `Cancel` under the cursor. A `WorkingTreeSection::Unmerged` row is refused in words instead (`conflict_refusal`): a conflicted path has no "before" to discard to, and its `UU` entry would otherwise reach the "both go back to HEAD" box and throw away the resolution in progress (m5-04 review Major 3). The consequence is **per class**, with line counts from the pane's own file list: untracked says the file is deleted; a file both staged and unstaged says both changes go (`restore --source=HEAD` throws away the staged one too, whichever row the cursor was on); otherwise which side the change comes back from (m3/04 review M6).
- `operated` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, jobs::operate::Operated, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`; drops a stale answer; `Done`/`Incomplete` both refresh — `Incomplete` still refreshes because the write happened even though part of its after-state could not be read (`ApplyError::AfterState`'s contract, `as-built.md`). `Failed` (via `operation_failed`/`failure_line`) sets the status line **and** refreshes on the scope the job reports (`Operated::Failed::refresh`): `ApplyError::Git` means git ran part-way, so the file list and `diff_raw` on screen are stale (m3/04 review M1). `Done` also closes the commit editor and its draft (`commit_editor::committed`) and words a remote op or a stash-clash drop. `Conflicted` sets `AppState::stopped` — a state, worded each frame by `banner::stop_reason` — gives the banner focus, and leaves `Status::Ready`, unless git gave a stalled reason (then a sticky `stalled_line` in git's words); it always refreshes `Everything`, then `banner::land_in_resolver` opens the resolver on the first conflicted path (m6-conflicts/18) — the wide re-read is what puts the unmerged paths under CONFLICTS and fills `AppState::in_progress`; the commit editor and its draft are left alone, since no commit was made (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/07 seam). Its `entry` becomes `AppState::live_entries` on the answer, ahead of the journal re-read (gap row 505, m6-conflicts/06). `StagingNotKept` → as `Done` (editor closed, refresh, landing) with `staging_not_kept_status`'s sticky line (30b). `Clashed` (a stash put back clashed) → `clash::clashed`: the resolver on the first clashed file, no banner (m6-conflicts/72b). `Refresh::WorkingTree` emits `ReadWorkingTree` + the pane's reload; `Refresh::Everything` runs `update::keys::refresh_key` — `Ctrl+R`'s own path, walk cancellation, `OpenRepository` and radar reset included, so the two cannot drift (m3/04 review M8). `Done` of a write whose launch set `AppState::lands_on_head` (from `Operation::moves_head`) also calls `banner::finished_lands_on_head` with the refresh's commands: the graph cursor lands on the new `HEAD` as after a `--continue` (m7-power-tools/03).
- `staging_not_kept_rungs` / `staging_not_kept_status` (fns, `pub(crate)`, `operate/outcome/staging_not_kept.rs`) — `(what, paths, came_back)` → the fallback's wordings, longest first (`<what> ✓ · your edits are back · [<came-back words> · ]staging not kept: <first>[ +N more] · u undoes it, staging included`, then without `your edits are back`, then without `<what> ✓ · `, then — only when `came_back` is non-empty — without the came-back words); `(what, paths, came_back, columns)` → `Status::Sticky` of the first rung that fits the status row (`fitting_line`'s rule), the `u undoes it, staging included` half as `keys` (30b).
- `edits_status` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(failure, at: graft_ops::EditsAt) -> Status`: sticky, `reason` the operation's own failure, `keys` the clause by where `graft_ops` says the edits are — `Stash{n}` `your edits are in stash@{n} · S p puts them back`; `Back{Some(n)}` `your edits are back · a copy stays in stash@{n}`; `Back{None}` `… a copy may still be in S`; `Backup{Some(n)}` `u restores the backup · a copy is in stash@{n}`; `Backup{None}` `u restores the backup`. The clause is `keys`, so `fit_status_line` keeps it whole and cuts the failure. For `Operated::Failed { edits: Some(at) }` (m6-conflicts/84, was `edits_in_status`, 74). Used by `operation_failed` and `banner::resumed`.
- `edits_clause` (fn, `pub(crate)`, 30c) — `(graft_ops::EditsAt) -> String`: that clause alone, shared by `edits_status` and `undo::interrupted_notice`.
- `conflict_line` (fn, `update::preview`, private) — `(op, put_back: Option<&ConflictForecast>) -> String`: the preview's forecast line, folding the put-back (m6-conflicts/84): `Clean`+`None|Clean` `no conflicts expected`; `Clean`+`Likely` `your edits may clash when put back: <paths>`; `Clean`+`NotChecked(r)` `your edits' put-back: not checked (<r>)`; an op that may stop, with edits aside, its own line + ` · put-back not checked`. `StopsAt` (m7/05) gets a bare `may stop at step N of M (<sha7>): <paths> · not checked past it` here; `plan_lines` draws it by `line_rows::forecast_rungs` instead.
- `update::preview::line_rows` (private mod, m7/05) — the chain rows `plan_lines` adds for a `Rebase`/`Rewrite` op (`ready_box` now takes the op): `is_line` (more than one ref move), `ref_rows` (`branches: ` label; dependents `· built on yours · follows`), `already_in_row(plan, op, keeps)` (`already in <onto>: <b>'s N commits match <sha7> "<subject>" · dropped`, `· R to keep them` appended when `keeps` — `R` live per `keeps_drops`; or `… not checked (<why>)`), `onto_label(&Source)` (`pub(crate)`, re-exported as `update::preview::onto_label`, rebase-preview-keeps-drops: `main` / short sha — the `already in <x>:` name, and the base row label of an editor opened by `R` from the preview), `forecast_rungs` (the stop ladder for `fitting_line`: paths, then subject, then `· not checked past it`; step never cut), `published_row` (`on origin: a, b · P will need force after`), `left_behind_rungs(left, more) -> Vec<String>` (`pub(crate)`, m7/37: longest first — `left behind: b stays on the old <sha7>` / `left behind: b, c stay on old commits` (`+N` for the uncounted), names only, `left behind: <first> +N`; empty when none), `left_behind_row(plan, rewrite, columns)` (the fitted rung of `not_forks`; `plan_lines` draws it under the fork rows, never for a `Rewrite`, ruling R1). m8/14c (ADR-0059): `ref_rows` skips a fork that follows; `ForkTone::{Follows, Warning, Muted}` and `ForkRow{text, tone}` (`pub(crate)`, re-exported); `fork_rows(plan, rewrite) -> Vec<ForkRow>` (`pub(crate)`, re-exported): one row per `plan.forks`, in its order — `<b> follows · from <at7>` / `· 1 commit from` / `· N commits from` (Follows), `! <b> stays on <at7> · would conflict in …` (Warning), `<b> stays on <at7> · <WhyStays::words>` (Muted) — then, for a rewrite, each branch parked at the old tip, `<b> stays on <sha7> · on the old tip` (Muted), then `+1 more stays` / `+N more stay`; `fork_lines(plan, line, rewrite)` (the texts, at the `branches:` indent on a line; the box draws one tone, R3); `not_forks` (private): `left_behind` minus the forks, its `more` only for a plan with no forks.

## update::commit_editor
The commit/amend editor (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05). Modal: while
`AppState::commit_editor` is `Some`, no key reaches the screen behind it.
Seam rule: builds a `graft_ops::operation::Operation` and a
`Command::Operate`, never a `GitInvocation`.
- `CommitEditor` (struct) — `subject: TextField`, `body: TextArea`, `focus: Field`, `amend`, `sign_off`, `no_verify`, `allow_empty`, `head_short`, `template_applied`, `from_draft`, `reword_line` (m8-polish-and-ship/14: `⏎` plans a reword of `HEAD` with its line, not an amend).
- `CommitEditor::new` / `CommitEditor::amend` (fn) — a fresh editor, or one prefilled from `HEAD`'s short id and message.
- `CommitEditor::load_message` (fn) — replace both fields from one message (first line the subject, the rest the body); used by the template, `HEAD`'s message and `$EDITOR`'s answer alike, so the three cannot split the message differently.
- `CommitEditor::message` (fn) — the fields back as one message: subject, a blank line, body; no trailing blank.
- `CommitEditor::params` / `CommitEditor::operation` (fn) — the `graft_ops::operation::CommitParams` the ticks describe, and the `Operation` (`Commit` or `Amend`) built from it.
- `CommitEditor::refusal` (fn) — `(Option<&WorkingTreeStatus>, Option<(InProgress, Begun)>) -> Option<String>`: the preview of why `[ Commit ]` is dimmed — an operation in progress (one Graft began names the way to the banner's keys — `<kind> in progress — esc, ⇥ banner, then c continue  a abort`; a merge begun in the shell `merge begun in the shell — esc, ⇥ banner, then c finishes it` (m6-conflicts/39); any other begun in the shell, which the banner's keys refuse, names git's own command — `rebase begun in the shell — finish it there: git rebase --continue`, m6-07 F12; a bisect names its command), nothing staged, or an empty subject. A preview only: `Operation::check` is what actually refuses.
- `Begun` (enum) — `InGraft` / `InShell`: who began the operation git is part-way through, which decides whether the banner's `c`/`a` can end it (m6-07 F12). Since m6-conflicts/20 the banner's `c` finishes an `InShell` **merge** too (ops routes it to `Operation::FinishMerge`); the refusal names the route to it: `Esc` closes the editor into the changes pane, whose `c`/`a` answer in words while the operation is live (`open` refuses, m6-conflicts/48), two `⇥` (Files → Diff → banner, ticket 39 NOTES n = 2) reach the banner, and its `c` opens the summary — words `merge begun in the shell — esc, ⇥ banner, then c finishes it` (ticket 39).
- `live_operation` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<(InProgress, Begun)>`: `in_progress`'s kind, `InGraft` iff `live_entries` is non-empty (the last journal read's live `Pending` entries — the fact `graft_ops`'s `resume_target` checks). The one argument every `refusal` caller passes: the view, the submit guard, `tests/it/m3_walk.rs`.
- `CommitEditor::is_empty` / `CommitEditor::options` (fn) — whether anything was typed (so `Esc` can drop rather than stash a draft), and the four checkboxes with their ticks.
- `OperationRefusal` (struct) — `{ reason: String, keys: Option<String> }`: why a commit cannot start while an operation is live; `editor_words()` is the editor's row (`{reason} — esc, {keys}`, or `reason` alone), `status()` the pane's `Status::Sticky { reason, keys }` (the `u` refusal's variant). One value makes both, so they cannot drift (m6-conflicts/48).
- `operation_refusal` (fn) — `((InProgress, Begun), tabs: u8) -> OperationRefusal`: the words for a live operation; `CommitEditor::refusal`'s operation arms call it. Both keyed arms name `tab_keys(tabs)` (m6-conflicts/70); a Graft-begun merge says `c finish`, not `c continue` (n2).
- `refusal_tabs` (fn, m6-conflicts/70) — `(&AppState) -> u8`: the `⇥` count a refusal names, `banner::tabs_to_banner` (1 where no banner is drawn, whose arms name no keys). `CommitEditor::refusal(status, in_progress, tabs)` takes it too.
- `NotStaged` (enum, `pub(crate)`, 30n m6) — why `stage_all` staged nothing: `NothingToStage` (`nothing to stage`, sticky), `OnlyConflicted` (`stage all does not act on a conflicted file — resolve the conflict first`), `Unread` (`still reading the changes — try again in a moment`, waiting), `WriteRunning(InFlight)` (`InFlight::words()`, failed, as `f`). `status() -> Status`.
- `update::keys::edit_stop::no_box_is_up` (fn, `pub(crate)`, 30n) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: no modal owns the keys and no graph overlay is up. `space_stages_here`'s keyboard test (with graph focus) and `rewrite_editor::opened`'s gate.
- `update::keys::edit_stop::space_stages_here` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m7-power-tools/07d) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: the graph has the keyboard (no modal, no overlay), the stop is a rewrite's `edit` stop, and the cursor is on the stopped commit's row or the Uncommitted row. One predicate, two callers: `graph_key`'s `space` (then `operate::stage_all`, `Ctrl+A`'s route) ahead of the fold, and `view::rows::live_wrapped`'s `space_stages` (the bar's `space stage`).
- `pane_c_acts` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m6-conflicts/52) — `((InProgress, Begun)) -> bool`: `c` in the graph or the changes pane is the banner's `c` — true for any merge and a rebase, cherry-pick or revert Graft began; false for a bisect and a shell-begun non-merge (M3a's words stay). The one place this is decided: `update::keys::commit_key` routes on it and `view::rows::live_c` labels the bar from it.
- `open` (fn) — `c`/`a` from the graph or the changes pane (via `update::keys::commit_key`, which sends `c` to `banner::continue_pressed(.., ReturnTo::Banner, ..)` first wherever `pane_c_acts`, m6-conflicts/52). With an operation live (`live_operation`) it opens nothing, sends no read, leaves `commit_draft` alone and sets `state.status = operation_refusal(live).status()` (m6-conflicts/48). Otherwise it takes back `AppState::commit_draft` when there is one, else a fresh editor plus the jobs for `commit.template` and, on an amend, `HEAD`'s message.
- `head_message_arrived` / `template_arrived` (fn) — fill an editor still waiting, dropping an answer for an editor that has since closed; `head_message_arrived` refuses as `open` does if an operation became live while `a`'s read was in flight (m6-conflicts/48); the template never overwrites text the user typed first.
- `amend_at_edit_stop` (fn, crate, 07b) — `a` at a rewrite's `edit` stop with the cursor on `HEAD`, the uncommitted row or the changes pane: reads `HEAD`'s message (`edit_amend`), and `head_message_arrived` (now also `commands`, `next_id`) launches `Operation::Amend` with it kept; lands `✓ amended the stopped commit · c continues`. `at_edit_stop` (fn, crate) — a live rebase whose stop read an `edit` stop.
- `editor_returned` (fn, `commit_editor/returned.rs` with `template_arrived`) — `$EDITOR`'s answer, accepted only while `AppState::external_editor` still names that job.
- `paste` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &str) -> bool`; a bracketed paste into whichever text field has focus (the subject drops newlines, the body keeps them). `true` whenever the editor is open, because it is modal. Before this the editor dropped every paste, though Graft turns bracketed paste on (m3/05 review M3).
- `editor_key` (fn) — the editor's whole key map: text into whichever field has focus, `Tab`/`Shift+Tab` and `←`/`→` for focus, `Space` on an option, `Enter` on `[ Commit ]` **or in the subject** (m6-conflicts/51, P16: the same `commit` path and refusals as `Ctrl+Enter`; a refused press leaves focus on the subject; `Down`/`Tab` reach the body, where `Enter` is a newline; a job running eats it like every key but `Esc`), `Ctrl+Enter` anywhere (ADR-0034: the button is the way that always works, `Ctrl+Enter` the shortcut where the terminal reports it), `Ctrl+E` for `$EDITOR`, `Ctrl+S` for a sign-off trailer, `Esc` to dismiss a failure if one is showing and otherwise put the draft aside — the two-stage `Esc` the graph screen has (m3/05 review M1).
- `committed` (fn) — a commit that landed: closes the editor and drops the draft.
- `strip_comments` (fn) — drop `#` lines from what an editor or a template gave back, the way `git commit` does.

## update::confirm
The discard confirm box: opened by `update::operate::request_discard`, answered here, modal like the context menu (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/04).
- `PendingConfirm` (struct) — `view: graft_ui::confirm::ConfirmView`, `then: ConfirmThen` (what `Enter` on `Confirm` dispatches). `yes(&self) -> Option<ConfirmYes>` (`pub(crate)`, m6-conflicts/50): **the one decision for `y`** — `then.takes_y()`, then the button at index 1 read through `ConfirmYes::from_label`; `confirm_key` acts on it and `view::rows::box_context` draws it.
- `ConfirmThen::operate(op: Operation) -> Self` (m8/14b) — boxes `op` into `Operate`.
- `ConfirmThen::takes_y(&self) -> bool` (`pub(crate)`, m6-conflicts/50) — ADR-0044 §3 as one exhaustive match: `false` for a lease push (`Operate(Push)` with `lease`), `UndoAfterPush` and `RevertTo`, and for any `Operate` no box opens today; `true` otherwise.
- `ConfirmThen` (enum) — `Operate(Box<graft_ops::operation::Operation>)` (the discard box; boxed because rewrite params dwarf every other variant, clippy `large_enum_variant`, m8/14b; build it with `ConfirmThen::operate(op)`), `Undo { redo: bool }` (the drift guard: the undo the box was opened for, run with `force`) (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06), `RevertTo { entry, description }` (the time machine past the same guard — the description rides along so the busy line names the entry rather than its ULID, m5/17) or `Resume(graft_ops::apply::Resume)`, or `UndoAfterPush { push, repush }` (the three-answer box `u` opens when a push has already sent the work the undo would drop: `push` is the journal entry that sent it, `repush: Option<Box<PushParams>>` the leased `PushParams` that would move the remote back, `None` when the box offered no third choice — ADR-0038 §4, m5/15, m5/25), or `RestartConflict { path, kind: RestartKind }` (the resolver's `d`: `checkout <kind.checkout_flag()> -- <path>`, run through `Operate(Operation::RestartConflict(RestartParams { path, kind }))` — m6-conflicts/05; `kind` m6-conflicts/58), or `Quit { then: update::quit::QuitThen }` (13b) (`update::quit`'s box, m8/13: `[ Quit ]` stops that write if still running, then quits; `takes_y` true; `yes()` is `ConfirmYes::Quit`, so `y` quits and the bar leads `y quit`).
- `update::quit` (`pub` module since the 13b review fix, so the it harness can play the loop's clock; m8/13 ruling 4; 13b) — **A stuck write** (13b review fix): `STOP_OFFER_AFTER` (10 s, `pub(crate)`); `offer_pending(state) -> Option<JobId>` (`pub(crate)`: the wait the loop counts toward the offer; `None` once long); `pub fn waited_long(state)` (the loop at 10 s: `QuitWait::long`, line `<op> · still running after 10 s · q stop it now · esc stays`, keys spelled from the keymap); then `q`/`Ctrl+C` open `Quit now?` (`QuitThen::StopNow(id)`, cursor on Cancel, `y` quits) → `Command::ForceStop(id)` + `Quit::StoppedHalfWay { job: id, what }` — the only path that emits `ForceStop`. `pub fn exit_line(bin, what, after, at: Option<graft_ops::apply::StoppedAt>, undo: &str) -> String` (13c item 10 / 13d): the one shell line a write stopped on the way out leaves, by where git was left — `Paused(k)` `<bin>: stopped <op> after <n> s · the <k> is paused · <bin> here offers continue or abort`; `Landed` `<bin>: <op> finished · a git hook after it was stopped after <n> s`; `BeforePause{true}` `…, before git could pause it · <undo> in <bin> puts your files back`; `Stopped{true}` `… · <undo> in <bin> puts your files back`; either `{false}` `… · nothing changed`; `None` (no answer within `jobs::FORCED_WAIT`) `… · <bin> here shows where git left it`. `<op>` sanitised, `<n>` whole seconds, `undo` the keymap's spelling of `app.undo`. Before 10 s, `q`/`Ctrl+C` while waiting change nothing. `asked(state, key)` (from the graph and the focused banner's `banner_key`): nothing changes while a wait stands, until it is long; a write running → box `Quit?` worded by its `Stoppable`: `Yes` → `<desc> is still running · quit stops it` (`QuitThen::Stop`), `No` → `<desc> is still running · quit waits for it to finish` / `if it stops at a conflict or fails, you stay to see it` (`QuitThen::WaitFor`); `Ctrl+C` boxes only over a `Stoppable::No` write, else quits; git stopped with nothing running → quits, no box (the user's ruling on 13b). `confirmed(state, commands, QuitThen)`: `Now` quits; `Stop(id)` → `remote::stop_write` then quit; `WaitFor(id)` sets `AppState::quit_when_done` (no `CancelJob`). `QuitThen` (enum `Now | Stop(JobId) | WaitFor(JobId) | StopNow(JobId)`), `QuitWait { job, line, long }` (no time in it: `update` stays pure, the clock is `event_loop::running::quit_clock::QuitClock`). `Esc` while waiting is spent on the wait in `keys::key_event` right after the box and before every modal pane (finish summary, resolver, rewrite editor), so a modal never swallows it. `pub(crate)`: `write_ended(state, job, done)` — called last by every `Stoppable::No` answer path (`operate::operated`, `banner::resumed`, `undo::reverted_to`): `done` quits, anything else drops the wait; `drop_wait(state) -> bool` (`Esc` after a held failure's dismiss, before `stop_write`); `waiting(state) -> Option<&QuitWait>` (only while its job is `operating`).
- `confirm_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`; `Left`/`Right` move the cursor **clamped** (`←` on `Cancel` stays on `Cancel` — the acting choice is never one key-repeat away from where the cursor rests, ADR-0038 §1; m5-04 review Major 1), `Tab`/`BackTab` cycle, `Enter` acts on whichever choice is under it, plain `y` acts on index 1 from wherever the cursor is when `PendingConfirm::yes` is `Some` (m6-conflicts/50; both go through the private `act`, so they cannot answer differently), `Esc` closes without acting, `Ctrl+C` passes through to `quit::asked`: quits, or over a `Stoppable::No` write boxes like `q` (keymap §2.2), **every other key is swallowed: the box stays open**, cursor and status untouched (m6-conflicts/47); `true` for every key but `Ctrl+C` while a box is open, so the caller sends it nothing else.

## update::credential
Git's credential question as an in-app prompt (m4-remotes/06, design note §7). Opened by `Progress::CredentialNeeded` from the running remote job, answered by `Enter`, cancelled by `Esc`; modal over everything, drawn last. Every string git sends is sanitised here, before it is kept.
- `PromptField` (enum, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/01, ADR-0038 §2) — `Open(graft_ui::text_input::TextField)` for a username, `Secret(graft_ui::text_input::SecretField)` for a password; `new(masked: bool, pulse_seed: bool)` picks the variant (`pulse_seed` reaches `SecretField::new`; the open field has no pulse and ignores it), and `insert`/`insert_str`/`backspace`/`delete`/`left`/`right`/`home`/`end`/`clear` dispatch to whichever field is live so `credential_key` never matches on the variant itself. `into_bytes(self) -> Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>` moves the typed answer out (`mem::take` on the `Secret` arm, no clone) for `Answer::new`.
- `CredentialPrompt` (struct) — `title`/`prompt` (`SanitizedText`), `field: PromptField`, `job: JobId`. `masked(&self) -> bool` reports `matches!(field, PromptField::Secret(_))` (replaces the old `masked: bool` field: the prompt's kind now lives in which `PromptField` variant it holds, not a separate flag that could drift from it). Its `Debug` prints the title, the mask and the job and **never the field** — an `AppState` dump cannot contain a typed password.
- `Answer` (struct) — `Arc<graft_git::run::askpass::Secret>`, the one secret-carrying value in `Command`: `new(Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>)` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/01: takes the already-zeroizing buffer `PromptField::into_bytes` hands it, moving the bytes out via `mem::take` rather than cloning), `expose(&self) -> &[u8]`, `into_secret(self) -> Secret` (unwraps the `Arc` when it is the last one, else copies; both allocations zero on drop). `PartialEq` compares contents so `Command` stays comparable; `Debug` prints `Answer(…)`.
- `is_masked_prompt` (fn) — `(&str) -> bool`: does git's wording say this answer is a secret ("password"/"passphrase", case-insensitively)? A username is asked in the open.
- `host_of` (fn) — `(&str) -> Option<String>`: the host out of git's prompt, for the box's title; the user part is dropped.
- `credential_needed` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, &str, pulse_seed: bool)`: open the prompt, but only for the write actually running and not while it is cancelling. Every test pins `pulse_seed`; the running app draws it.
- `credential_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress) -> bool`: `Enter` → `AnswerCredential` + close + clear, `Esc` → `CancelCredential` + `CancelJob` (the write stops), `Ctrl+U` clears, `Ctrl+C` falls through to quit, **everything else is swallowed** — no global binding can fire from inside the prompt.
- `credential_paste` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &str) -> bool`: a bracketed paste lands in the field when the prompt is open.

## update::undo
`u`/`U` (ADR-0002), pure: which job the key builds, what the status line says, and the drift guard's box. `graft_ops` decides what an undo does — this only names the job (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06).
- `UndoStart` (enum, `pub(crate)`, m7/30o R3) — `Launched` / `Asked` (the undo-after-push box is up) / `Refused` (worded on the status row): what one `u`/`U` did, so no caller counts commands.
- `start_undo` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(state, commands, redo, next_id) -> UndoStart`: the one start every `u`/`U` goes through (graph, J's `u`, J's `⏎` on the ← u/← U row). A write in flight → `Failed("wait for <running> to finish")` (was silent before m7/30o, V1).
- `undo_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, redo: bool, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`; a wrapper over `start_undo`. Refused while another write is in flight (worded, m7/30o), otherwise sets `operating` and `Status::Busy("undo · esc cancels")` and dispatches `Command::Undo`/`Redo` with `force` off. `Stoppable::Yes` since m5-history-and-ghost-preview/06 gave `graft_ops::restore::execute` a between-step stop check and a roll-back.
- `undone` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, jobs::undo::Undone, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`; drops an answer that is not the job in flight. `Done` → `consequence::landing`: `Status::done(<the entry's own description>)` — with `U brings back your edit to fix.txt` as its keys when the run replaced an edit (m6-conflicts/77); after a redo, no keys and `; a backup keeps …` in `what` (m6-conflicts/80) — `undo_landing_owed` set when nothing is live, and the refresh the entry asked for; `Incomplete` → `Status::Sticky` and still the refresh (it ran); `Nothing { reason, redo }` → `Status::Done` (nothing to undo is an answer, not a failure), with keys `U redoes <root operation>` when a redo waits (m6-conflicts/77); `Drift` → the guard's confirm box (its consequence line is `drift_consequence`), and `Status::Ready`: the answer ends its own `undo · esc cancels` (m6-conflicts/69); `Cancelled { clean, .. }` → `Status::Done` when the roll-back finished (nothing is wrong, so it is an acknowledgement) and `Status::Sticky` when it did not, plus `Refresh::Everything` either way; `Failed` → `Status::Sticky`, and a refresh only where git may have run. `OperationLive { kind }` (ops' undo gate refused: the step is from before the live operation, m6-conflicts/57) → `live_refusal(kind, "⇥ banner")` — no keys for a bisect, which has no banner — and no refresh.
- `after_push_chosen` (fn) — `(state, chosen, repush: Option<PushParams>, next_id)`: `1` undoes locally, `2` re-pushes the old tip, and a cursor on any other index is a `debug_assert!` naming how many choices the box had (m5/25).
- `operation_ended` (fn) — `(&mut AppState)`: a resume answered `Done`/`Incomplete`, so `in_progress` goes `None` and `banner_focus` off **in the same update**, and `AppState::ended_by_answer` marks it; `u` right after `--continue` is an undo, not a refusal (gap row 569, m6-conflicts/06).
- `reread_disagrees` (fn, `pub(super)`) — the next working-tree read spends `ended_by_answer`; if git still reports the operation, `Status::sticky("git says a <kind> is still in progress · Ctrl+R")`, and the read's `in_progress` stands.
- `land_after_undo` (fn, `pub(super)`) — pays `AppState::undo_landing_owed`; called by `update::took_fact` on the **newest** working-tree read only (`status_read::answered`), after `in_progress` is replaced. An operation live again, `banner_focusable`, no modal and no graph overlay (Map, journal, stash list hide the banner) → `banner_focus` and the `Done` line's `keys` = `banner::continue_words`: `undo: finish merge of feature ✓ · c finishes the merge`, so `c ⏎` finishes it again (keymap §2.6a: the same landing as a launch into that state). Spends the flag in every case; a failed newest read drops it too (ticket 41).
- `update::keys` `u`/`U` in J (m7/24-b, V8; one outcome since m7/30o R3): `u`/`U` and `⏎` on the ← u/← U row both call `journal::undo_from_j` — J closes only on `Launched`; `Asked` leaves J under the push box (Esc returns to the same row; choice 1 or 2 launching closes J); `Refused` leaves J up with the words on its status row.
- `undo::lands_on` (private module, `update/undo/lands_on.rs`, m8/13 ruling 2; was `undo::landing`, renamed so it is not confused with `consequence::landing`, review m8-13 Major 3) — `of(&JournalEntry) -> Option<Landing>`: where a `Done` `u`/`U` lands, read from its own entry (an undo has no `Operation`): `HEAD` moved (other `HEAD`, or its branch moved) → `Head`; else the first branch/tag (name order) moved and still existing → `Commit(its tip)`; else a row-less ref moved (`refs/stash`) → `Head`; else `None` (cursor and pane stay). `came_back(&JournalEntry) -> Vec<Oid>`: branch/tag tips the entry made exist again, tagged `RowTag::PutBack`. `undid` pays it through `banner::finished_lands_on`, beside `undo_landing_owed` (which still owns the banner focus). The journal's time machine (`reverted_to`) does not land (unchanged).
- `in_progress_refusal` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(&AppState) -> Option<Status>`: the sentence `u`/`U` answer with while an operation is live (`docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.6), `live_refusal(kind, "⇥ banner")`; `update::banner::banner_key` calls it so a **focused** banner refuses in words instead of swallowing the key (m5/25). **`None` when `shell_merge_known`** (keymap §2.6a's one exception, m6-conflicts/57): the key goes to `graft_ops`, whose gate lets through only a step journaled inside this same merge.
- `shell_merge_known` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: the live operation is a merge `commit_editor::live_operation` calls `Begun::InShell` **and** the journal read has landed (`Loading::Ready`). Before that read, `live_entries` is empty for every merge, so "begun in the shell" is not yet known and `u` refuses as before. The rule is here, not in `live_operation`, because the commit editor's words treat "not read yet" as `InShell` on purpose. Read by `in_progress_refusal` and `banner::abort_pressed`.
- `live_refusal` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(InProgress, Option<String>) -> Status`: `Sticky "finish or abort the <kind> first"` with the given keys. The one wording, matching `graft_ops::undo::NotInvertible::OperationLive`'s (`operation_live_words_match_ops`); used by `in_progress_refusal`, `undone`'s `OperationLive` arm and `preview::preview_refusal` (through `live_refusal_words`, `pub(super)`, the same words without a `Status`).
- `revert_to_forced` (fn) — the journal's time machine past the drift guard: unstoppable for the reason the first attempt was, since a jump is several restores and `Esc` between two of them would leave the repository somewhere nobody asked for (m5/08).
- `reverted_to` (fn) — `(state, JobId, jobs::undo::RevertedTo, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`. `Done` → `consequence::landing` (keys `U (N times) brings back …` when a step replaced an edit, N = `presses`, m6-conflicts/77; fitted to 80 columns, m6-conflicts/80) and a full refresh; `Drift` → `Status::Ready` (the answer ends its own `Busy`, m6-conflicts/69) and the guard's confirm box, carrying the target entry so the retry aims at the same place; `Failed` → `Status::Sticky` naming how many of how many steps ran, and a refresh only when something did (m5/08).
- `consequence` (private module, `update/undo/consequence.rs`, m6-conflicts/80) — the drift box's consequence words and their fit rule, and the landing after a forced run; pure text, moved out of `update/undo.rs` whole.
- `consequence::drift_consequence` (pub(crate) fn, m6-conflicts/77) — `(drift, redo, presses, width, lines) -> String`: the drift box's line under what moved — `Undo replaces your edit to fix.txt; a backup keeps it, and U brings it back.` (n paths: `your edits to 3 files`; `whole_tree`: `the whole working tree`; `whole_index`: `the whole index`; both: `the whole working tree and index` (m6-conflicts/80); `U (N times)` when `presses > 1`), `Undo leaves your edits since as they are.` when nothing is replaced but the tree or index drifted, `A backup is taken first, and U brings this state back.` for refs/HEAD/merge only. A redo names no key (`u` after a redo undoes the root operation). Fitted to `lines` wrapped lines at `width` (60, the narrowest box's wrap): the one path is cut from the left, then `a backup keeps it, and ` is dropped.
- `consequence::landing` (fn, `pub(super)`, m6-conflicts/80; was 77's `replaced_keys`) — `(what, replaced, redo, presses) -> Status`: `Done(what)` when nothing was replaced; else keys `U brings back your edit to fix.txt` (`U (N times)` when `presses > 1`), or for a redo no keys and `what; a backup keeps …` (`keys` is the next key, review m7). Fitted to 79 cells (the 80-column footer less its indent): the path cut from the left, then `it`/`them` for it, then `what` cut with `…`, so the key always shows.
- `interrupted_notice` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: the footer line for entries a previous run left part-written. An entry this launch's recovery closed with its set-aside stash still listed (`AppState::interrupted_edits`) adds the status line's own clause, `operate::edits_clause` — ` · your edits are back · a copy stays in stash@{n}` or ` · your edits are in stash@{n} · S p puts them back` (30c); an entry closed by an earlier launch, its stash still listed, keeps ` · edits in stash@{n}` (m6-conflicts/74). Unfitted: rung 1 of `notice_line` (`usize::MAX` cells); tests pin it. Derived from the journal's own entries (`JournalEntry::is_interrupted`, minus anything a later entry reverses), not from what a read happened to recover, so it survives every re-read until `J` (m3/06 review, Major 1). **Minus `AppState::live_entries`** (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/14): `is_interrupted` says yes to a live conflict too — the bytes are identical — and claiming a merge standing two rows above was interrupted is the one thing this line must never do.
- `interrupted_notice_in` (fn, `pub(crate)`, 30d) — `(&AppState, cells) -> Option<String>`: `interrupted_notice` fitted to the footer row's text width; its only caller is `view::frame::bars::footer_notice` (`columns − 1`), picked every draw so a resize re-picks. `columns` is the footer row's own width: `view::frame` passes `primary_pane_area(state, area)`'s width, the graph half when the detail pane is open (30f). A clause recovery worded (`AppState::interrupted_edits`) takes its stash index from `refs.stashes()` each draw, by the put-back commit; gone from the list → no `stash@` (30f).
- `notice_line` (fn, `pub(crate)`, 30d) — `(description, clause: Option<&str>, cells) -> String`: the relaunch notice's ladder, first rung that fits in display cells: `an operation was interrupted: <d> · <clause> · J to inspect` → `interrupted: <d> · …` → description cut with `…` (≥ 10 cells left) → `interrupted · <clause> · J to inspect` (else that, cut by the footer). The clause and `J to inspect` are never cut; the description is sanitized before it is measured.

## update::banner
- `finished_lands_on` / `lands_on_changes` (fns, `pub(in crate::update)`, m7/24-a) — `finished_lands_on_head` generalised to an `operate::Landing`: owes the graph landing on `Commit(c)` (or `HEAD`); `lands_on_changes` opens the changes pane with the cursor on the file's `UNSTAGED` row (`KeptCursor`), also for a file that is staged too: that row's diff is the edit put back (m7/30p R2, `journal_put_back::a_staged_path_put_back_lands_on_its_unstaged_row`).
The operation banner: the honest interim for a conflict without a resolver
(m5-history-and-ghost-preview/09, `docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.6a). Pure —
it reads `AppState::in_progress`, `mergetool` and the working-tree status,
and asks the loop for everything that touches disk. M6's resolver pane
replaces it, keeping the same letters for the same verbs.
- `unmerged_paths` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Vec<PathBuf>`: every unmerged path from the working-tree status, in git's own order. Empty while the status has not arrived; the banner says so rather than guessing.
- `first_conflicted` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<PathBuf>`: the first of them — what `M` and `e` act on from the banner.
- `banner_view` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<graft_ui::graph_screen::BannerView>`: the drawn banner whenever `in_progress` is `Some` **and** the kind has one (`banner_focusable`). `kind` is the word (`MERGING`/`REBASING`/`CHERRY-PICKING`/`REVERTING`), `step` the detail's `n of m`, `conflict` the first unmerged path (read by `hint_context` for `M`/`e`, never drawn since m5/20), `focused` = `AppState::banner_focus`, `skippable` = the kind is not a merge.
- `graph_screen_on_frame` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: is the graph screen itself the thing being drawn this frame? `screen == Graph && state.graph_overlay().is_none()` — no longer a hand-written mirror of `view::frame`'s match (review M3, m5/31). The one place that question is asked, so the row budget and the header agree about who carries the banner word.
- `tab_banner_rows` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> u16`: what the banner costs in the **Tab** layout — `0`, `1` drawn, `2` focused; `0` again when the graph tab is the one shown, where the graph header already carries the word. `view::frame::tab_banner_area`, `update::geometry::detail_split` and `AppState::graph_rows` all read this one number (m5/25 — three copies of the arithmetic disagreed, and the mouse landed a row off).
- `tab_rows_above_graph` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> u16`: the rows the Tab layout puts above the graph itself (the tab strip, plus the banner when it is drawn there), for `rows_above_graph` and `graph_rows`.
- `banner_focusable` (fn) — `(InProgress) -> bool`: whether the kind has a banner to focus. A bisect does not: no Continue/Skip/Abort shape, so focusing one would claim the keyboard for a mode with no keys in it.
- `continue_words` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(InProgress) -> Option<String>`: the key words a `Done` line carries over a focused banner — `c finishes the merge`, `c continues the <kind>`, `None` for a bisect. One source for `resolver::every_file_done` and `undo::land_after_undo` (ticket 41).
- `continue_refusal` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: `"<n> files still conflicted"` (singular counted), or `None` when `c` may run. `c` asks `status_read::owed` first and answers `CHECKING` while a read is out. `continue_refused` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(String) -> Status`: that refusal as the sticky line with its keys.
- `take_focus` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, KeyPress) -> bool`: `Tab`/`Shift+Tab` **into** the banner. The detail pane's cycle is Graph → Files → Diff → Graph; the step that would land back on the graph lands on the banner instead, and with the detail pane hidden one `Tab` from the graph does it. Called before the detail pane's own `Tab`, so the two never both move. A mode with a door out and no door in would strand `c`, `s` and `a`.
- `banner_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`, sits **right after `preview_key`** in `update::keys::key_event` and before the context menu. **Modal while focused**: `true` for every key but `Ctrl+C` (§2.1's one exception). `c` continue, `s` skip, `a` abort, `M` mergetool, `e` edit — both refuse in words (`nothing conflicted`) rather than silently do nothing when there is no unmerged file, and the hint bar drops them in that state. `Tab`/`BackTab`/`Escape` give the keyboard back; only `Escape` also dismisses a `Sticky` (a failed resume's words stay through `Tab`, m6-conflicts/12 — the stop itself is `AppState::stopped` and survives both); `Tab` is the next step of the cycle so it sets `detail.focus = Graph` (without it `Tab` bounced Diff↔banner and the graph was unreachable), `BackTab` steps back to `Diff`, and `Escape` leaves the pane focus where it found it. Everything else is swallowed on purpose: `u` mid-rebase would undo the entry *before* the live one, and `S` would stash a tree full of conflict markers. `u`/`U` ask `undo::in_progress_refusal` **first** and, when it answers `None` (a shell-begun merge, m6-conflicts/57), send the undo through `undo::undo_key`; the banner keeps the keyboard. Plain `q` goes to `quit::asked`, the graph's own quit path, so a stop, a relaunch or an undo landing on the banner still gets the quit box (review m8-13 Major 1).
- `abort_pressed` (fn, private) — `a`: the abort confirm box, cursor on `[ Cancel ]`. On a merge `undo::shell_merge_known` calls begun in the shell its lines are `<head> goes back to before the merge` and `u puts the merge back` (m6-conflicts/57: the abort is journaled as `AbortMerge` and undoable); `<head>` is `head_word` — HEAD's branch short name, else its 7-character sha, else `HEAD`. Every other operation keeps the old lines.
- `continue_pressed` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, ReturnTo, next_id)`: `c`, from the banner or the resolver (m6-conflicts/31). Status read out → `CHECKING`; `continue_refusal` → its words; a merge → `finish::open_finish`; a write running → sticky `a write is already running`, nothing sent (m6-conflicts/65); else `--continue` (from the resolver: closes it, banner focused). From `ReturnTo::Resolver` the words go on the notice row, not the status line.
- `resume` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, graft_ops::apply::Resume, next_id) -> bool`: queues `Command::Resume` with a `Busy` line, unstoppable — a half-run `--continue` is worse than a slow one.
- `resumed` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, jobs::operate::Operated, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: the answer. Dropped unless `operating` **and** `AppState::resuming` both name its id (one guard); takes `resuming`, clears `operating` and `remote.running`; the finish summary's own `--continue` answers first (`finish::resume_answered`; a refusal keeps the summary open and nothing else runs). `Done` → `resumed_words`, `undo::operation_ended`, refresh `Everything`, graph focused, and `AppState::land_on_head` set with `head_landing` naming the refresh's refs read and walk (m6-conflicts/12); `Conflicted` again (a rebase stopping on the next commit) → `live_entries` from its `entry`, `stopped_again` (`ThisRun`/`again`, banner focus, status `Ready` — or `Sticky "stopped again: <git's words>"` only when git gave a stalled reason), refresh `Everything`, then `land_in_resolver` on the first conflicted path; `StagingNotKept` → closed as `Done`, then the fallback's sticky line led by what `resumed_words` put first (30b); `Clashed` → `operation_ended` + `clash::clashed` (m6-conflicts/74); `Failed` with `edits` → `edits_left_aside` (operation over, the line says where the edits are); other `Failed` → `graft_ops`' own words with `a abort` as the key, because the operation is still live, refreshing the scope the job reports.
- `land_in_resolver` (fn, `update::banner::stop`, crate-internal) — after a stop (`operated`, `resumed`): with anything unmerged, opens the resolver on the stop's first path from the banner (`OpenedFrom::Banner`, so `Esc` returns there); nothing unmerged, a resolver already open, or a box open → leaves the banner (m6-conflicts/18).
- `resumed_words` (fn) — `(&graft_ops::apply::Resume, &JournalEntry) -> Status`: the one wording of a resume that closed its entry, keyed on the `Resume` sent **and** the entry's status (a failed continue closes `Aborted` too). Continue → `<d>`; a finishing skip → `<d>, skipped a commit` (true whichever commit it was; m6-conflicts/78 — it read `last commit skipped` until a first-of-three skip proved it wrong); an abort → `aborted: <d>` (a shell merge's `AbortMerge` entry, `Committed`, keeps its own `abort merge of …`); all `Done`, tick drawn by the renderer. A continue/skip over `Aborted`/`Pending` → `Sticky "<d> did not finish: <reason>"`, never a tick. The `Incomplete` arm takes the same words (m6-conflicts/68).
- `pending_view` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<graft_ui::PendingView>`: a rewrite stop's pending todo rows of **this branch** (`RewriteProgress::pending_here`), newest first, capped at 6, the rest counted; `followers` from `RewriteProgress::followers` as `(branch, author, commits)`, sanitized (07c, 07d). `None` only when nothing at all is pending.
- `pending_rows_taken` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> u16`: the rows the still-to-apply block takes above the graph, mirroring `view::frame`'s carve (half the rows left, 0 below 3). `update::geometry::rows_above_graph` and `AppState::graph_rows` both add it, so a click under the block lands on the row drawn there (m7-power-tools/07d, review m7-10-to-07c Major 2).
- `continued_in_git` / `land_after_continue_in_git` (fn, crate) — `c`'s shell hand-over came back: ask one working-tree read, then land finished (`✓ git <kind> --continue finished`) / stopped again (`stopped_again` + resolver) / non-zero and still stopped (`… exited <code> · still stopped`) (07c).
- `open_mergetool` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, Option<&Path>) -> bool`: `Command::OpenMergetool { path }`, or — with no `merge.tool` configured — the refusal `no merge.tool configured · e edit …`, **before** any hand-over.
- `edit_conflict` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, Option<&Path>) -> bool`: `Command::OpenConflictEditor { path }`. The line to open at is read by the loop (`event_loop::outside::Handover::edit_conflict`), not here: `update` is pure and may not read a file.
- `cursor_conflict` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<PathBuf>`: the conflicted path under the changes pane's cursor, so `M`/`e` on a `CONFLICTS` row act on that row rather than on the first conflict.
- `update/banner/stop.rs` (m6-conflicts/24, pure move out of `banner.rs`, review m6-amend-11-12 M2): the stop's wording and the head landing. Its public items are re-exported from `banner`, so their paths are unchanged:
- `stop_reason` (fn) — `(&Stop, Option<(&InProgressDetail, usize)>) -> String`: the stop line's words (m6-conflicts/12): `merge stopped · 1 file conflicted`, `rebase stopped again at step 4 of 7 · …`, `… still in progress from a previous run · step 3 of 7 · …`, `… in progress · begun outside Graft · …`. The live read is used only when its kind is the stop's; count part dropped at 0.
- `stop_wordings` (fn) — same arguments, `-> Vec<String>`: `stop_reason` first, then shorter wordings, each saying the count short (`13 conflicted`) and dropping whole parts — the origin goes short (`from the shell`, `from last run`), then goes, then the step, then the count (m6-conflicts/24, review M1; m6-conflicts/61). Repeats skipped. The last carries no number.
- `stop_line` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: `stop_reason` for `AppState::stopped`, worded from the last working-tree read; with nothing stopped, a stash clash's first rung (`update::clash::stop_wordings`, m6-conflicts/72b).
- `stop_line_wordings` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<StopLadder>`: `stop_wordings` for the same stop, or the clash's ladder; a git stop whose Pending entry set edits aside gains a first rung `<full> · edits aside`, so the part drops first (m6-conflicts/74; `stop_line` is that ladder's first rung); `view::rows::stopped_line` sanitizes them into `GraphView::stopped` (`StopWording`).
- `one_tab_to_banner` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> bool`: `!modal_owns_keys() && tabs_to_banner(state) == Some(1)` (m6-conflicts/70; same answers as before, now read off the one reading of the cycle) — when `Tab` really lands on the banner.
- `TabTarget` (enum, crate), `tab_target` (fn, crate), `tabs_to_banner` (fn, crate), `tab_keys` (fn, crate) (m6-conflicts/70, `banner/stop/tab.rs` since 30m) — the one reading of the `Tab` cycle: where one `Tab` lands from here (`Graph`/`Files`/`Diff`/`Banner`, `None` under a modal or with no pane and no banner); how many reach the banner (asked through `banner_view`, as `take_focus` asks); `"⇥ ⇥ banner"`. `view::rows` reads `tab_target` for the changes pane's `⇥` word and `banner_context(state, banner)` sets `Banner { merge }` from the live kind. n1 cause (not fixed): an untracked file's body is `graft-git` `diff/read.rs` `file_diff`, which returns hunk-less for `FileStatus::Untracked` with no git spawned (M3 deferral).
- `refs_answered` (fn, `pub(in crate::update)`) — spends `head_landing`'s refs id when that read answers; a refs answer with another id leaves the landing owed. `walk_started` (fn, `pub(in crate::update)`, no id) — spends the walk half when the **awaited** walk starts, whichever refresh issued it: a `Ctrl+R` replacing the walk before it started used to leave the landing owed for good (m6-conflicts/24, review Minor 3). The private `land_on_head` runs once both are in: `History::seek(refs.head())`, graph cursor, clears the flag.
- `RewriteStop` (struct, 07b, `banner/stop.rs`) — `progress: graft_ops::rewrite::RewriteProgress`, `edit: Option<EditStop>`. `read_rewrite_stop` (fn, crate) — `(&RepoHandle, &dyn Stop) -> Option<Result<RewriteStop, String>>`, on the job thread (operate job, working-tree read); `None` outside a rebase. `stop_takes_focus` (fn, crate) — banner focus at a stop: every banner kind but an `edit` stop. `edit_stop_of`, `progress_unread` (fns, crate) — the edit read of a stop; the `couldn't read the rewrite's progress: <why>` line. `edit_stop_lands_on_head` / `relaunch_lands_on_head` (fns, `pub(in crate::update)`) — an `edit` stop's landing: graph focus, cursor on `HEAD` (owed to the refresh, or the launch reads).

## update::resolver
The conflict resolver's state machine (m6-conflicts/04), pure and **modal**: while it is open it claims every key but `Ctrl+C` and every mouse event, so nothing reaches the graph behind it. `B` (base) and `d` (start over) are m6-conflicts/05's, alongside the whole-file shapes (`o`/`t` on a `UD`/`DU`/`AU`/`UA`/`DD`, or a binary `UU`/`AA`). **Split by concern** (m6-05 review, Major 6) into private child modules — `whole_file`, `take`, `base`, `restart`, `walk`, `scroll`, `answers`, `geometry` under `update/resolver/` — re-exported, so every item below is still `update::resolver::…`. Fixes in the same round: a card answers `n` (crosses to the next file) and refuses `d` in `ops`' `NotMarkerShape` words; a take waits while `base_job` is busy (`still loading the base`), and a hand-back or fresh read drops the base load in flight; `d` is refused while any write runs, before the box and again at its accept (`update::confirm`).
- `ResolverState` (struct) — `finish_owed: bool` (m6-conflicts/60: the last file of a merge owes the summary; see `finish_if_owed`), `path`, `opened_from: OpenedFrom`, `file: Option<Arc<graft_git::conflict::ConflictFile>>` (`None` until the first read lands, and for a whole-file shape), `whole_file: Option<WholeFileShape>` (`Some` exactly when `file` is `None` and the read has landed), `how: Option<UnmergedHow>` (the status shape, set when the read lands; `B` on `BothAdded` answers `no base (added on both sides)` from it with no job), `cursor_block`, `top`, `base_shown`, `job` (the read or take in flight), `reading: bool` (`job` is a read — opening or re-read — not a take; words the refusal a key gets meanwhile: `still reading the file` vs `still writing the last take`, m6-07 F7), `base_job: Option<JobId>` (the `B` → `LoadBase` job — its **own** field, never `job`: a base load must never race an open read or a take answer for the same id), `staging` (the `⏎`/whole-file `o`/`t` → `Stage`/`ResolveWholeFile` operation's id), `restarting: Option<JobId>` (the `d` → `RestartConflict` operation's id, never confused with `staging`, which advances to the next file rather than reopening this one), `notice: Option<(String, bool)>` (the line under the document; `true` = a problem).
- `WholeFileShape` (struct) — `how: UnmergedHow`, `binary: bool`, `on_disk: Option<jobs::conflict::OnDisk>` (the capped glimpse of the file on disk, raw — sanitised at the view, never here; `None` when nothing is there), `start_over: jobs::conflict::StartOver` (ops' gate answer for `d`, asked when the card opened; m6-conflicts/30).
- `OpenedFrom` (enum) — `Banner` or `Pane`: where `⏎` was pressed, and so where `Esc` gives the keyboard back.
- `open_resolver` (fn, child `open`, which also holds the private `reopen` — decides the refusal before taking the resolver, so a refused switch leaves it as it was) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, PathBuf, OpenedFrom, next_id) -> bool`; refuses **in words** with a box open, with the resolver already open, or with a write running, and pushes `Command::OpenConflict`.
- `land_on_clash` (fn, `pub(crate)`, child `open`) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, first: PathBuf, next_id) -> bool` (m6-conflicts/72b): closes `S`, opens the changes pane on `first` under `CONFLICTS` (focus `Files`), opens the resolver `OpenedFrom::Pane` so `Esc` lands on that row. `false` (nothing done) with a resolver or box open.
- `resolver_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`; `false` only when the resolver is closed or the key is `Ctrl+C`. Below `graft_ui::conflict_resolver::{MIN_COLUMNS, MIN_ROWS}` (`fits`) only `Esc` acts — nothing else is drawn to choose from.
- resolver `u`/`U` (m6-conflicts/85 m-i) — plain `u`/`U` in `act` → private `undo_answered`: no command, `state.status` untouched; the notice row (info) says where undo is — an operation live → `undo::live_refusal_words`; a stash or set-aside clash → `clash::undo_clause` (its landing notice's clause); else `esc first · u undoes from the graph`. No key bound.
- resolver `c` (m6-conflicts/31) — plain `c` in `act` → `leave::finish_pressed` (child `leave`, with `Esc`'s `escape`/`close`, m6-conflicts/37): refused sticky while a take lands or a file read is out (`handover::handover_refusal`), else `banner::continue_pressed(.., ReturnTo::Resolver, ..)`. Not in `done::refuses`.
- `resolver_mouse` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, MouseInput, next_id) -> bool`; always `true`. While a box (confirm or commit editor) is up it does nothing: the box owns the mouse as it owns the keys (m6-conflicts/26). The wheel scrolls `WHEEL_LINES` (the same constant `update::mouse` uses); a left click on the file list opens that file (`select_file`), on the document puts the cursor on the block under it — body rows counted from the document area's own origin (`view::resolver::resolver_split`), so the list above or beside it moves nothing (m6-conflicts/13).
- `listed_files` / `ListedFile` / `RowState` / `file_cursor` / `select_file` (m6-conflicts/13, child `files`) — `listed_files(&AppState) -> Vec<ListedFile { path, state: RowState }>` is the one source of the list's order and states: the union of `ConflictSet::paths` when `Ready`, unmerged paths not listed, and the open path, **sorted byte-wise** (git's own order, over `as_encoded_bytes`, not `Path`'s `Ord`) — so a file keeps its row when it is done (m6-conflicts/25). `RowState` (`Unread`, `Open { open, of }`, `AllTaken`, `WholeFile { how }`, `Binary`, `Done`): status not arrived and not open → `Unread`; not unmerged → `Done`; the open file from its document; else `ResolverState::seen`. `file_cursor(&AppState) -> usize` is a function of the open path. `select_file(state, commands, index, next_id) -> bool` opens row `index` keeping the list; refused in words while a read/take/base/write runs; a `Done` row opens read-only (m6-conflicts/25). `DoneShape` (`Text { unstaged }`, `Binary { unstaged }`, `Removed`, `Unreadable { why }`) is `ResolverState.done` — `Some` while a done file is open. `done_refusal(&AppState) -> Option<String>` — `"<path> is done · d starts it over"` while one is; `act` asks it first for `o t b ⏎ B M n N`, and `d`, `e`, `Esc`, scrolling act as on any file. `write_not_landed(state, id)` releases `staging`/`restarting` when a write the resolver sent was refused or failed (they were left set, refusing every later file switch). `ResolverState` gains `files: Loading<Arc<ConflictSet>>`, `set_job`, `seen`; the set is read (`Command::ReadConflictSet`) on open and after every stage, and kept across `n`, a file switch and a restart.
- `whole_file_keys` (fn) — `(UnmergedHow, binary: bool, &SideNames) -> String`; the key text a whole-file card offers per shape (`binary` overrides `how`), a side named by its one name (`t keeps theirs · feature`).
- `side_names` (fn, private child `names`) — `(&AppState) -> SideNames`: the one builder of each side's name (m6-conflicts/10, ADR-0043 §4). Merge/pull `yours · <HEAD>` / `theirs · <MERGE_HEAD>`; rebase `upstream · <onto>` / `yours · <sha7> <subject>`; cherry-pick `picked · …`; revert `reverting · …`; resolver open with no operation = stash pop, theirs `stashed · stash@{n}` for a clash Graft recorded (`update::clash::stash_name`, m6-conflicts/72b), `stashed` alone for a shell pop. A branch-like side is named by a local branch (current first, else alphabetical), else a remote badge, else its 7-char sha (so detached HEAD reads `yours · 3f2a1b7`).
