Read by `view::resolver`, `view::rows::resolver_context` (for `roles`), the took-line (`took theirs · feature in …`) and the whole-file keys. A theirs git cannot name (`InProgressDetail::theirs` is `Err`) is drawn as that error, `theirs · could not read MERGE_HEAD: …` (m6-conflicts/23). `fixtures` (`#[cfg(test)]`): `oid`, `commit`, `refs(detached)`, `operation(kind, theirs)` (wraps `theirs` in `Ok`).
- `side_keys` (fn, `pub(crate)`, child `names`) — `(&AppState) -> String`: `o <ours role>  t <theirs role>` from `side_names(state).roles` (`o upstream  t yours` in a rebase). The keys of every resolver status line that offers a card's `o`/`t`: `whole_file_both_refused`, `restart_box`'s whole-file refusal, `take`'s card refusal (m6-conflicts/23, review m6-amend-10-22 M2; `walk`'s rule 3 no longer carries keys since m6-conflicts/61). `update::resolver::tests::no_resolver_status_carries_a_bare_ours_or_theirs` scans the resolver's source for a literal naming a side outright.
- `conflict_opened` / `taken` / `handed_back` (child `answers`; `taken` and `take_landed` in its child `answers::taken`, m6-conflicts/36) / `resolved_advance` (child `resolved`, with `⏎`'s private `mark_resolved` and the three landings `to_next` / `close_after_last` / `stay_after_last`, m6-conflicts/37) (fns) — the four answers: the read landed or refused (a whole-file/binary shape fills `whole_file`, never closes the resolver; `Opening::Done` settles the done card, and a failed done read words the card rather than closing, m6-conflicts/25 — except a `Done` answer while an unmerged document is on screen, i.e. `$EDITOR`/a mergetool resolved it, which closes with `<path> resolved outside` as `not_unmerged` did); a take landed or failed (`TakeFailure::reread` → the document is thrown away and read again, never written over); `$EDITOR` or the mergetool gave the terminal back (**both** re-read, which `M` did and `e` never did); the `Stage`/whole-file `ResolveWholeFile` behind `⏎`/`o`/`t` finished, so on to the next conflicted file or out (no banner → `"all conflicts resolved"` with no `c continue`, `KNOWN_GAPS.md` row 229). Since m6-conflicts/13 the next file is the next not-`Done` row of `listed_files` after the one staged, wrapping, with notice `<file> done ✓ · ▸ <next>`; nothing left → with a banner the resolver **stays** and reopens the same file read-only (its done read), notice `every file done ✓ · c finishes the merge` / `c continues the rebase|cherry-pick|revert`, or `checking the files` while a status read is out (m6-conflicts/31); no banner (stash pop) → closed, status `every file done ✓`; with `AppState::clash` (m6-conflicts/72b) a pop or set-aside launches `Operation::DropPutBack` (its `Done` answer reads `every file done · stash@{0} dropped ✓ · u brings it back`, via `clash_drop` in `operate::operated`), an apply sets `every file done · stash@{0} kept ✓`.
- `every_file_done` / `finish_if_owed` / `finish_refused_notice` / `set_notice` (fns, `pub(crate)`, child `resolved`) — `every_file_done(&AppState) -> Status` is `Done { what: "every file done", keys: banner::continue_words(kind) }` (`c` only with a banner and the banner or a resolver holding the keys); `finish_if_owed(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, next_id)` (m6-conflicts/60) spends `ResolverState::finish_owed`: called once at the tail of `update::answer`, it opens the summary through `banner::continue_pressed(ReturnTo::Resolver)` when nothing is in flight (`settling`, `in_flight`, `queued`, `staging`, `restarting`, `job`) and `!status_read::owed`; with a `continue_refusal` it drops the open (the notice already says why); `finish_refused_notice(&str) -> (String, bool)` is `c`'s refusal as the notice row says it (`<reason> · ⏎ marks done`); `set_notice` writes the open resolver's notice row (m6-conflicts/31). After the last file (`stay_after_last`): `reopen` (now `-> bool`, true when it opened) rebuilds the state; for a merge it sets `finish_owed`, the notice `CHECKING` while the status read is out, the status `<file> done ✓ · every file done` (other kinds `· c continues the <kind>`). `finish_owed` is cleared by `status_read::abandon`, `write_not_landed`, `done_unreadable`, the keys `Esc` `j` `k` `↑` `↓` `n` `N` `d` `e` `M` (`cancel_owed_finish`, `pub(super)` in `resolved` beside `finish_if_owed` since m6-conflicts/65), a file switch (`reopen`) and the resolver closing.
- `DoneOpen` (struct, child `done`) / `start_over_refusal` (fn, `pub(crate)`, child `restart`) — `ResolverState.done: Option<DoneOpen { shape: DoneShape, start_over: jobs::conflict::StartOver }>` (m6-conflicts/28): a done file carries ops' own gate answer, asked by the job before the read. `start_over_refusal(&AppState) -> Option<String>` is **the one predicate** for `d`: a whole-file card's own ops answer (`WholeFileShape.start_over`, raw words; no app copy of ops' rule, m6-conflicts/30), a done file's `git can't bring back the conflict in <path>: <why>` (leading `<path>: ` stripped, m7-power-tools/17), else `None`. It feeds `restart_box` (refuses in words, no box; otherwise the box by `StartOver::Can { kind }` — `Markers`: `the conflict comes back with all its markers` / `takes and edits in this file are lost`, `git checkout --merge -- <p>`; `WholeFile`: `the choice comes back: keep or delete` / `edits since the choice are lost`, `git checkout --ours|--theirs -- <p>`; on a done file the title is `Start <p> over` (no `?`) and the second line `your choices in <p> are dropped` (m7-power-tools/17); either second line gains ` · not undoable while the <kind> is open` when one is, m6-conflicts/58), `HintContext::Resolver.restart`, the identity row's `· d starts it over`, the done card's keys and `done_refusal` (`<path> is done` with or without `· d starts it over`).
- `ResolverState.dropped` (field, `usize`) — queued takes a failed take dropped while its re-read is out; `answers::landing_notice` says `<n> queued takes dropped` once the re-read lands (review m6-amend-15 M2).
- `handover` (private child) — `e`/`M` (`edit_here`, `mergetool_here`) and `Esc`: refused with `a take is still landing · try again in a moment` while a take is in flight or queued (`takes_refusal`, asked **before** `write_refusal`, whose `still writing the last take` would otherwise answer), so a hand-over's re-read or a close never drops them (m6-conflicts/28 R6, review m6-amend-15 M1).
- `restart_landed` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, JobId, next_id)`; the `d` → `RestartConflict` write landed: reopens the same file fresh, unlike `resolved_advance`, which moves to the next one.
- `base_loaded` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, Result<(Arc<ConflictFile>, usize), (String, bool)>)`; `B`'s `LoadBase` answered: `Ok` attaches the bases and sets `base_shown`; `Err((words, problem))` leaves `base_shown` false and sets `notice` with the job's bool — a problem only for a git `diff3` failure. An `AA` file is answered in `toggle_base` with no job, notice not a problem.
- `in_flight` / `queued` / `land_last` / `TAKE_QUEUE_CAP` (m6-conflicts/15) — `ResolverState.in_flight: Option<(TakeAt, Take)>` is the take whose `TakeSide` is out, `Some` exactly when `job` is a take; `queued: VecDeque<(TakeAt, Take)>` the takes pressed meanwhile, in order, at most `TAKE_QUEUE_CAP` (64; the 65th is refused `still writing the last take`), **empty whenever `in_flight` is `None`** — `take_landed` sends the front against the document that landed in the same update, so every refusal keyed on `job` covers the queue. A failed take or a `Changed` re-read drops both (`… · <n> queued takes dropped`); so does a hand-back re-read. Any other job in flight (read, base load, git write) still refuses a take in its own words. `land_last: bool` — `N` crossed here, so the opening read lands the cursor on the last block. Private `take::pending_file(&ResolverState) -> Option<Result<ConflictFile, NoSuchBlock>>`: `file` with `in_flight` then `queued` applied; a take whose block the pending document lacks is refused with notice `no block <i> · re-read the file`, nothing sent.
- `update::events` files (review m6-amend-16 M3, pure moves; re-exported from `events`): `events.rs` `AppEvent`, `Progress`, `Task`, `Product`, `GitCheck`, `CommitGraphOffer`; `events/command.rs` `Command`, `TakeRequest`, `TakeAt`.
- `update::keys` files (review m6-amend-16 M3, pure moves): `keys.rs` dispatch, the order a key is offered to handlers (`key_event`, `place_claim`, `pane_and_overlay_key`, `graph_key`); split further in m7-power-tools/34: `keys/escape.rs` `escape_key` (`Esc`'s whole graph ladder), `keys/acknowledge.rs` `acknowledge`/`dismiss_unseen` (re-exported at `update::keys::acknowledge`), `keys/refresh.rs` `refresh_key` (`Ctrl+R`, re-exported at `update::keys::refresh_key`), `keys/commit.rs` `commit_key` — `c`/`a`: `c` is `banner::continue_pressed(.., ReturnTo::Banner, ..)` wherever `commit_editor::pane_c_acts`, else `commit_editor::open`, m6-conflicts/52, …) and `acknowledge` (clears `Copied`/`Done`/commit-graph written; called by `key_event` and by `mouse::mouse_event` on any button down, m6-conflicts/42; skipped while a modal owns the bottom row or `AppState::unseen` stands, so a `Done` that landed under a box is drawn before it is cleared, m6-conflicts/78) and `dismiss_unseen` (`pub(super)`, `(&mut AppState, &KeyPress) -> bool`: with no modal up, the first key after a held failure is drawn clears `unseen`; `Esc` is consumed doing it and cancels nothing, m6-conflicts/78); `keys/bound.rs` `bound_actions` (`#[cfg(test)]`, re-exported); `keys/table_check.rs` (`#[cfg(test)]`, m8-polish-and-ship/09, reworked m8/10) `every_bound_action_is_a_row` (each `ActionId` in `bound_actions` is a row of `graft_ui::keymap::defaults()`) and `no_action_is_listed_twice`; `keys/predicates.rs` the pure `is_*`/`motion_for`/`RemoteKey` tests; `keys/actions.rs` `cycle_tier`, `strip_rows_for`, `copy_selected_sha`, `toggle_compare_selected`; `keys/view_change.rs` `ViewChange`, `view_change`.
- `update::keys::view_change::with_stash_entries` (fn, `pub(in crate::update)`) — `(&View, &[Oid]) -> Option<View>`: the view with `Inclusion::with_stashes(<every entry>)`, or `None` when unchanged (m7-power-tools/16). Called from `update`'s refs answer (`stash_entries_read`, via `History::change_view` + `ShowRows`) and from `history_started` (a walk started after the refs landed asks with the entries from its first request), so a stash's index and untracked commits are never rows.
- `TakeAt` (enum, `update::events`, m6-conflicts/16) — `Block(usize)` | `Every`; `TakeRequest.at` (was `block`). `Ctrl+O`/`Ctrl+T` (`take::take_all_key`, dispatched in `act` ahead of the non-plain swallow; `Ctrl+Alt` still swallowed): done file → `done_refusal` (`done::refuses` includes them); whole-file/binary card → `this card is one choice · <side_keys>`; `take_refusal`; nothing open in `pending_file` → notice `nothing open in <file>`; else one `(Every, take)` entry — sent, or queued (counts 1 against the cap and in `queued takes dropped`). Cursor stays at the press. At landing (`take_landed`) an `Every` sets notice `took <side name> in <n> block(s)` (`n` = the request document's `open()`), plus ` · ⏎ marks <file> done` when nothing is open or queued. An `Every` landing with no block side resets a standing `Status::Done` to `Ready`, so an earlier block's `k of m open` cannot contradict the header (review m6-amend-16 M1). `pending_file` replays `Every` as `take_all`; `jobs::conflict::take` sends it to `apply_take_all`.
- `ResolverState.settling` (field, `Option<Take>`, m6-conflicts/17) / `O`/`T` (child `settle`: `settle_key` the press, `settle_landed` the landing, private; routed by `take::take_keys`, which holds every take key — m6-conflicts/33) / `j`/`k`/`↓`/`↑` (`files::step_file`, private) — `O`/`T` settle the whole file: a done file refuses (`done::refuses` gains `O`/`T`, drops `n`/`N`); a card is its own `o`/`t`; `take_refusal` (which also refuses while `settling` is `Some`: `still writing the last take`); open blocks → one `TakeAt::Every` sent or queued, `settling = Some`; nothing open and nothing in flight → `mark_resolved` (`⏎`); nothing open behind a take in flight → `settling = Some` only. `answers::take_landed` sends the `Stage` (via `mark_resolved`) when the queue drains and `settling` is set; a failed/`Changed` take clears it and appends ` · nothing staged` (`drop_takes` clears it too). Invariant: `settling.is_some()` ⇒ `in_flight.is_some()`. `step_file` opens `file_cursor ± 1` through `select_file`, no wrap. `scroll_key` no longer takes `j`/`k`/arrows (`PgUp`/`PgDn`, `g`/`G`, `Home`/`End`, wheel). On a done file `n`/`N` cross straight (`cross_target` → `select_file`) or set the notice `NO_MORE_THIS_WAY`.
- **A take advances at the keypress** (m6-conflicts/15): `o`/`t`/`b` accepted → cursor to `after_take(&pending, block)` and scrolled into view; `None` → the cursor stays, notice `all blocks taken · ⏎ marks <file> done`. `take_landed` keeps the cursor where the keys put it (only keeps it on screen) and sets that notice when `file.open() == 0` and nothing is queued.
- `next_block` (fn) — `(&ConflictFile, Option<usize>, forward) -> Option<usize>`: the next block **taken or not**, `None` past the end (`from: None` → first/last). `n`/`N` walk it, so a taken block can be re-taken by keyboard.
- `after_take` (fn) — `(&ConflictFile, taken) -> Option<usize>`: the next open block after `taken`, wrapping; `None` when none is open.
- **The cross rule** (child `walk`, keymap §2.6 `:267`): past the last block (`N`: before the first) — 1. the next row of `listed_files` not `Done`, from this file's row, wrapping, never itself (private `walk::cross_target(&AppState, forward) -> Option<usize>`) → `select_file` it, notice `▸ <file>`, `n` lands on its block 0, `N` on its last; refused in `select_file`'s words while a take is in flight; 2. else this file's first (`N`: last) open block, notice `wrapped · <k> still open`; 3. else the info notice `walk::NO_MORE_THIS_WAY` (`pub(crate) const`, `no more conflicts this way`; m6-conflicts/61), cursor unmoved, `state.status` untouched, so the bar keeps `⏎ done` (a card: its side keys); once every file is done the finish row is drawn in its place; the notice keeps today's lifetime (ticket 61 Decisions). A card goes straight to rule 1. `next_open_block` and `next_conflicted_file` are **removed**: nothing crosses in `unmerged_paths` order any more.
- `scroll_block_into_view` (fn) — `(&Layout, block, top, height) -> usize`: `top` moved as little as it must, and when it must move at all the block's header lands on the **first** row (in the three panes, its rule row: `block_row` means that there, m6-conflicts/14). Child `scroll`'s `block_of` maps `Aligned { block, .. }` to its block and `PaneTitles` to none, so a click in any pane picks the block.
- `resized` (fn, child `geometry`) — `(&mut AppState)`: on `Event::Resize`, `top = scrolled_to(cursor block).min(max_top)`, so crossing 180 columns (card ↔ three panes, different row counts) keeps the cursor block on screen (m6-conflicts/14). Called from `update`'s Resize arm.
- `banner_rows` / `fits` (fns) — the rows the operation banner keeps above the resolver (1 while an operation is live: mid-rebase, `REBASING step 3 of 7` is what says which side is which), and whether the rest clears the widget's floor.

`B base` / `d start over` are live footer hints and in `update::keys::bound::BOUND_ACTIONS`.

## update::finish
The finish summary (m6-conflicts/19, app half built by 34): `c` on a resolved **merge**'s banner opens it instead of committing; `y` in it commits (m6-conflicts/59; `⏎` in the subject too). Rebase, cherry-pick and revert still `c` → `Resume::Continue` directly. Modal like the resolver: while open it claims every key but `Ctrl+C` and every mouse event. Keys are §2.6b's only.
- `FinishState` (struct) — `AppState.finish`: `job` (the summary read in flight), `summary: Loading<Arc<FinishSummary>>`, `cursor`, `top`, `expanded: Option<(usize, Loading<Arc<DiffView>>)>`, `diff_job`, `focus`, `subject: TextField`, `body: TextArea`, `from_draft` (the fields came from `finish_draft`, so the summary's message does not overwrite them), `notice: Option<(String, bool)>` (words, is-a-problem), `committing`, `editing`, `return_to`.
- `FinishFocus` (enum) — `Files` | `Subject` | `Body`; `next()` is `⇥`'s cycle.
- `ReturnTo` (enum) — `Banner` | `Resolver`: where `Esc` goes back to.
- `Choices` (struct) — `AppState.choices: BTreeMap<PathBuf, Choices>`: `takes`, `whole: Option<Side>`, `edited` — what the user chose per file, for the rows' words; a file with no entry (a relaunch, a file resolved outside) reads `ResultIs` words. Written on **answers**, never keys (child `choices`, `pub(crate)`, m6-conflicts/35): `record_take` in `resolver::answers::taken::take_landed` (`Block(i)` sets `i`; `Every` — `O`/`T` included — sets each block the request's document had open and the landed one took); `record_whole` in `resolved_advance` from `ResolverState.whole_side`; `record_edited` on an `Ok` handover (resolver `e`, banner/changes-pane `e` via `AppState.conflict_editing`, mergetool); `forget` in `restart_landed`. Cleared in `undo::operation_ended` (Done and abort) and, with `finish_draft`, by `update::working_tree_read` whenever a read finds no operation (a merge finished in the shell, m6-conflicts/36).
- `AppState.conflict_editing` (field, `Option<PathBuf>`) — the path `banner::edit_conflict` handed to `$EDITOR`, taken when `Product::HandedOver` answers (it carries no path); `Ok` marks it `edited` (m6-conflicts/35).
- `ResolverState.whole_side` (field, `Option<Side>`) — the side a whole-file `o`/`t` asked for, riding with `staging`; recorded as `Choices.whole` when that write lands, cleared by `⏎`'s stage and `write_not_landed` (m6-conflicts/35).
- `FinishAnswer` (enum) — `Merged(Option<String>)` (the words `Done` shows, `merged <from> into <into>`) | `Kept` (a refusal: the summary stays open with git's words) | `Closed`.
- `open_finish` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, ReturnTo, next_id)`: takes `finish_draft` into the fields if one was kept, pushes `ReadFinishSummary`. Called by `banner::continue_pressed` on a merge, from the banner or the resolver (`ReturnTo::Resolver`, m6-conflicts/31).
- `finish_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`; in `keys::key_event`'s modal chain after the boxes. `Ctrl+C` passes; every key is swallowed while committing; below the widget's floor (`fits`) only `Esc` acts. `Ctrl+⏎` commits, `Ctrl+E` hands the message to `$EDITOR`, `⇥` moves focus forward and `⇧⇥`/BackTab back (`FinishFocus::prev`), `Esc` closes (keeping a draft if anything was typed). Files: `y` commits, `⏎`/`space` expand a row's staged diff, `j`/`k`/arrows move, PgUp/PgDn move by the list's height. Subject: `⏎` commits. Body: `⏎` is a newline.
- `finish_mouse` (fn) — wheel ±3, a click puts the cursor on the row under it (`graft_ui::finish_summary` hit test); does nothing under a live box.
- Children (m6-conflicts/36, pure moves): `answers` (the job answers below), `keys` (`finish_key`, `paste`, `commit`, `toggle_diff`, `close_keeping_draft`), `mouse` (`finish_mouse`), `words` (`split_message`, `compose`, `by_you_words`), `choices`. The parent keeps the types, `open_finish`, `resized`, `fits` and the scroll helpers, and re-exports each child's public fns at their old paths.
- `summary_landed` / `diff_landed` / `job_ended` / `editor_returned` / `resume_answered` (fns, child `answers`) — the answers. `resume_answered(state, id, &Operated) -> Option<FinishAnswer>`: a `Failed`, or a `Conflicted` with `stalled: Some` and no paths (how a `commit-msg` hook's refusal arrives for an edited message — `git commit --cleanup=whitespace -F -` failed; an unedited message is `Resume::Continue`, which runs `git merge --continue`, `graft-ops/src/apply/resume.rs`), keeps the summary open with the words; a stop with new conflicts closes it keeping the draft; every non-`Kept` answer (`Merged`, and `Closed` from `Incomplete` or `Conflicted`) closes the resolver under it — committed lands on the graph (m6-conflicts/31), a new stop opens its own resolver (m6-conflicts/37).
- `paste` / `resized` / `fits` / `by_you_words` / `split_message` / `compose` (fns) — bracketed paste into the focused field; re-fit on resize; the widget's floor; a by-you row's words (`choices` first, else `ResultIs`); message ↔ subject/body.
- `AppState::finish_draft` (field) — the subject/body `Esc` kept, taken back by the next `open_finish`. Cleared with `choices` by `undo::operation_ended`.

## update::status_read
The working-tree read the status waits on (m6-conflicts/26, review m6-amend-13 Minor 1). Private module.
- `CHECKING` (const, `pub(crate)`) — `"checking the files"`: the `Busy` line both the last file's close and `c` show while a read is out, so the line and `c` never disagree.
- `ask` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(&mut AppState, JobId) -> Command`: the only builder of `Command::ReadWorkingTree`; records the id in `status_read`.
- `owed` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — whether a read is out.
- `answered` / `settle` / `abandon` (fns, `pub(super)`) — the read ended: clears `status_read` if it was the newest; `settle` turns a `CHECKING` line into the verdict (`resolver::every_file_done`, or `banner::continue_refused`); `abandon` turns it into `Ready` when the read never answered. The same for a resolver notice row saying `CHECKING` (m6-conflicts/31): `settle` writes the verdict there (none when `c` may run: the finish row takes over, m6-conflicts/60), `abandon` clears it and cancels an owed summary (`finish_owed`).

## update::preview
Ghost Preview's state machine (PROMPT.md §1.7, ADR-0038, design note hard choices 1, 3, 5), pure. A preview is a **modal overlay over a bounded slice**: it never loads more history, and it never runs anything — only `Confirm` does.
- `PreviewSubject` (enum) — `Operation { op: Box<Operation>, opened_by: &'static str }` or `RevertTo(EntryId)`. `opened_by` is the key that opened it, so a `Moved` refusal can say which key to press again.
- `PreviewRequest` (struct) — `subject` + `resident: HashSet<Oid>`: what to plan, and every commit the app can draw without reading anything.
- `Planned` (struct) — `plan: PreviewPlan` + `anchors: Vec<Commit>` + `runs: Option<Box<Operation>>`: what the job came back with. `runs` is `plan_as_run`'s completed op (a rebase's line and drops, m7/05b; `None` for a revert-to); `preview_produced` moves it into `PreviewSubject::Operation { op }`, so the op `y` confirms is the one the picture was drawn from.
- `PreviewFailure` (enum) — the preview job's `Err`: `Refused(Refusal)` (what `graft_ops::preview::plan` refused, i.e. the write's own `check`) or `Failed(String)` (a read failed; the line the `preview failed:` box prints) (m6-conflicts/67).
- `PreviewState` (struct) — `subject`, `resident`, `slice_top`, `job`, `plan: Loading<Arc<Planned>>`, `rows: Vec<Row>`, `view_start: usize`, `selected_row: Option<usize>`, `ghost_index`, `clipped`, `undrawable: Option<Oid>`, `cursor`, `return_to_journal`, `refused` (m6-conflicts/86: a stash preview refused by `plan` keeps its box, the refusal as `plan = Failed(words)` under one `[ Close ]`, drawn without the `preview failed:` prefix; every other subject still closes with a status line, m6-conflicts/67). A stash apply/pop subject draws its own box — `Preview: apply|pop stash@{n}`, `working tree:`, the conflict line, the fate row (`stash@{n} stays in the list` + the undo line, or `stash@{n} is dropped · u brings it back`), the command line, `Cancel`/`Apply|Pop` — with `y apply|pop` on its edge (the footer's `again` slot), and `y` runs it through `operate::launch` (stoppable, as `⏎`/`p` did before; not `launch_unstoppable`). The box is drawn over the `S` overlay (`view::frame::overlays::draw_stash`), which stays open under it, so `esc` lands on the same stash. **`view_start` is where the user's own screen begins inside `rows`**, and `selected_row` is the commit the key was pressed on: the slice is three screens wide so a ghost has somewhere to hang, and drawing the picture from `rows[0]` showed a user scrolled into history the screen *above* theirs (m5/17 F1). `cursor` is the *choice* cursor inside the box (`0` Cancel, `1` Confirm) and has nothing to do with a row.
- `PreviewState::outcome_label` (fn) — `-> String`: what the predicted block is called on screen, in the user's words — `after rebase`, `after merge`, `after cherry-pick`, `after revert`, else `the result`. Drawn on a row of its own above the first ghost (`view::preview::label_entry`) and nowhere near the word *ghost*, which is ours, not the interface's (m5/20 item 4).
- `HISTORY_CANNOT_STOP` (const) — why `Esc` is not offered for a confirmed history operation.
- `PUT_BACK_CANNOT_STOP` (const, m7/30o) — the same for a put-back from J: `runs to the end · u undoes it after` (no banner to name). A put-back's confirm closes J only once the launch is `Ok`.
- `MIN_BOX_ROWS` (const) — 4; mirrors `graft_ui::preview_box`'s crate-private constant.
- `BoxRect` (struct) — `x`/`y`/`width`/`height` + `bottom()`: where the box goes, in plain numbers. `update` names no drawing type (`update::tests::update_cannot_reach_the_world`), so the dock arithmetic is mirrored here and kept in step by `tests::a_click_lands_on_the_button_the_widget_drew`, which renders the real widget and reads the buttons back off the buffer.
- `preview_refusal` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: why a preview cannot open now — `operate::InFlight::words` while a write runs, `undo::live_refusal_words` while git is mid-operation; `None` when it can. Split out of `open_preview` so the stash-branch name prompt can refuse inside itself (m6-conflicts/69, conductor 2026-09-25).
- `open_preview` (fn) — `(state, commands, subject, return_to_journal, next_id) -> bool`. Refuses in words (`Status::Sticky { reason: preview_refusal(..), keys: None }`, never `Done`) while a write runs or git is part-way through an operation — the latter worded by `undo::live_refusal(kind, None)` (no keys: the overlays hide the banner; m6-conflicts/57 deleted `kind_word`); silent over an already-open preview. Snapshots the resident rows **before** `state.preview` is set, so the box's own rows have not yet shrunk the window it is measured in.
- `preview_key` (fn) — modal: claims every key but `Ctrl+C`. `←`/`→` choose, `Enter` acts, `Esc` closes (and cancels a plan in flight). `R` (rebase-preview-keeps-drops): when `keeps_drops` is `Some`, closes the preview and calls `rewrite_editor::open_rewrite_from_rebase` with the as-run params; otherwise swallowed.
- `keeps_drops` (fn, `pub(crate)`, rebase-preview-keeps-drops) — `(subject, plan, head_branch: Option<&str>) -> Option<&RebaseParams>`: `Some` iff the subject is a `Rebase` whose `drops` are non-empty, `plan.already_in` is `Drop`, and its branch is `head_branch` (full ref name). The key, the `R to keep them` row and the footer's `PreviewBox::keep` all ask it (via a private `keeps_drops_here(state)` over the landed plan and `current_branch`, which also requires `acts()` — `None` in the undrawable box, 30n m1). Below `PREVIEW_MIN` `Enter` is a no-op — nothing acts on a picture nobody can see.
- `preview_click` (fn) — a click on a button is that choice; every other click is swallowed, because the rows behind a modal picture are not selectable.
- `preview_produced` (fn) — `(state, id, Result<Arc<Planned>, PreviewFailure>)`. A `Refused` answer closes the box (the journal back if it was opened from there) and sets the status from `operate::refusal_status` — no box: the preview is modal and `S` would need `esc` first (m6-conflicts/67). A `Failed` answer keeps the `preview failed:` box. Otherwise builds the slice, lays it out once with `graft_graph::layout`, fills `rows`/`ghost_index`/`clipped`/`undrawable`. Stale job ids dropped.
- `confirm_preview` (fn) — runs the **same** `Operation` the picture was drawn from, moved across rather than rebuilt (hard choice 3); `RevertTo` becomes `Command::RevertTo`. A `Recover` subject (J's put-back, m7/24-a2) closes J first (`journal::close`): the landing is on the graph.
- `close_preview`, `preview_badges`, `preview_box`, `preview_box_rect`, `preview_rows_taken`, `fits` (fn). `preview_box`'s `Recover` arm (m7/24-a2, J1): title `recover::preview_title` in every state; lines `recover::preview_lines` (now / after y / your files / u undoes it), the plan's commands, `Cancel`/`Put back`; footer `again = y put it back`, `confirm_key` false, `keep` false. Drawn over J by `view::frame::overlays::draw_journal`, J's bar blanked under it.
- `HistoryKey` (enum) + `is_history_key`, `history_key` (fn) — `m` (merge the selected branch into the current one) and `r` (rebase the current branch onto the selection), `docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.3. Both end in a preview, never in a write. Refusals are sentences: `m needs a commit row`, `r needs a branch checked out`, `already on it`, and on a stash row `a stash isn't a branch · ⏎ opens it` (`RefBadges::is_stash`, m8-polish-and-ship/14). Inside an open `Operation` preview (m6-conflicts/11, keymap §2.3b), `preview_key` treats a plain `y`, or the opening `m`/`r` again (private `confirm_key`), as Confirm wherever the cursor is — swallowed while planning, below `PREVIEW_MIN` or when the plan cannot act; never in a journal `RevertTo` preview. `preview_box` sets `PreviewBox::confirm_key`/`again` for the footer — only while the plan can act or is still planning, never on a closing box or a can't-act plan (m6-conflicts/24, keymap §111).
- `is_take_key`, `take_key` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `o` on the graph (keymap §2.3, m7-power-tools/11): the cursor commit taken onto `HEAD`'s branch as `Operation::CherryPick(verbs::pick_params(..))`, `opened_by` `"o"`, through `open_preview` — the same op slot 2's `⏎` opens. Not a `HistoryKey`. Refused before any preview, in `operate::refusal_status`'s words from `PickParams::check_commit`: `HEAD`'s row, a merge; `o takes a commit · this row isn't one` on the uncommitted row or a stash row. A one-commit `CherryPick` preview is titled `Preview: take <sha7> "<subject>" onto <b|HEAD>` (private `take_title_of`, applied in `preview_box` for planning, ready and closing boxes alike); the bar stays `y confirm`.

## update::preview::slice
The bounded slice (design note hard choice 1), pure over what it is handed.
- `PREVIEW_ANCHOR_CAP` (const) — 64: more commits outside the view than that and the preview says so rather than reading them.
- `Slice` (struct) — `commits`, `ghost_at`, `clipped`.
- `SliceError::MissingParent(Oid)` (enum) — the one case that cannot be drawn: a ghost's real parent that is neither resident nor an anchor. The box then says `preview cannot be drawn here: <sha7> is not loaded · esc` and offers `[ Close ]` — never a truncated shape, never a panic.
- `build_slice` (fn) — `(resident, anchors, plan) -> Result<Slice, SliceError>`. A ghost goes immediately above the topmost slice row among its **transitive** real parents, and a chain comes out newest-first. Anchors go last with their own parents cleared — and counted into `clipped`, because history plainly continues below them. Parents outside the slice are clipped and counted too: `graft_graph::layout` requires every merge parent to be present, so clipping is mandatory, and the count is what the box's `<n> lanes continue outside this view` line reports.
- `BadgeOverlay` (struct) + `badges_for_preview` (fn) — the refs shown where they **would** land: each `RefMove`'s name removed from its `from` commit and added to its `to` (real or ghost); `to: None` is a delete and needs no special case; `HeadMove` moves the `HEAD` mark. Borrows the live badges rather than rebuilding them.
- `short_of` (fn) — `refs/heads/main` → `main`; a remote shorthand is left alone.

## update::remote
`f` fetch, `p` pull, `P` push, and `Esc` on any running write (m4-remotes/05, design note §5). Builds `Operation::{Fetch,Pull,Push}` from `AppState::remotes`; `graft_ops` decides what runs and words most failures.
Split by concern (m6-06 review Major 2), every item re-exported from `update::remote` so its path is unchanged: the root holds the state, the facts read and `f`/`p`; `remote/push_box.rs` is `P` and its box; `remote/after_push.rs` is `u` on pushed work, the re-push and `pushed_since`/`pushed_to`; `remote/in_flight.rs` is `Esc`, progress, `cancelled`, `remote_done`/`remote_failed`.
- `RejectedPush` (struct) — `params: PushParams` (the push refused non-fast-forward) and `sent: Option<Oid>` (`HEAD`'s tip then, read only from `RefsRead::Current` refs; `None` otherwise) (m6-conflicts/49); `leaves: Option<(Oid, Oid, LeavesRead)>` — what the lease takes off the remote, keyed `(sent, expected)` (m7-power-tools/16b).
- `Leaves` (struct) — `newest: Vec<CommitSummary>` (newest first, ≤ `LEAVES_SHOWN` = 3), `count: usize`. `LeavesRead` (enum) — `Reading(JobId)` / `Read(Leaves)` / `Failed(String)` (m7-power-tools/16b; the id since review m7-16b..21b Minor 2). `leaves_job_ended(state, id, why)` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `job_failed`/`job_cancelled` for the read in flight store `Failed(why)` (`the read was cancelled` for a cancel), so `P` no longer says `still reading` forever and the prelude's `settled` answers.
- `request_leaves` / `leaves_arrived` (`pub(super)` fn) — the settle tail asks `Command::ReadLeaves` once per lease key (`push_box::lease_key`, the one place the lease rules live); a fetch that moves `expected` re-keys and re-reads; an answer for a superseded key is dropped (m7-power-tools/16b).
- `RemoteState` (struct) — `last_rejected_push: Option<RejectedPush>` (the push the remote refused non-fast-forward; cleared on success or a branch change; offered only while the refs are current and `HEAD` is still the tip that was refused (`RejectedPush::sent`) — m6-conflicts/49), `reading` (the facts read in flight), `running: Option<Running>` (the write in flight: id, description, the `Operation` when it is one — every launched op since m6-conflicts/67, remote or not; the remote wordings filter on `is_remote` —, `stoppable`, and `opened_by` — the key that drew the Ghost Preview it was confirmed from, so a `Moved` refusal can say which key to press again), `cancelling` (the write `Esc` stopped), `diverged_upstream: Option<(String, Oid)>` (the tracking ref and tip a `p` just refused to fast-forward onto; `m`/`r` aim at it while it is `Some`, whatever row the cursor is on; cleared by dismissing the line and by any write launching — m5-history-and-ghost-preview/08; the **tip is re-read from the refs** at the tail of every `update` while the line shows, because the pull's own fetch moves it after the record is written — m5/32), and `undo_after_push_asked: Option<graft_core::journal::EntryId>` (the push entry whose undo-after-push box has already been answered, so `u` explains once per push rather than once per entry below it — m5/15))
- `request` / `request_at_launch` (fn) — ask `Command::ReadRemotes`; the launch read runs from `settle` once the repository has opened and the facts are `Idle`.
- `remotes_arrived` (fn) — lands the facts, dropping a stale id.
- `fetch_key` / `pull_key` / `push_key` (fn) — the keys; refusals are words on the status line (no remote, detached, no upstream), never a no-op. `f` while a write runs: `Status::Failed(operate::InFlight::words())` (gap row 481; its own copy of the words went in m6-conflicts/78); `p`/`P` in that state are still silent (not this ticket's).
- `repush_lease` (fn) — `(push: &JournalEntry, tracking: &str) -> Option<Oid>`: the tip the push left on the remote, `refs_after[tracking]` of its own entry; the re-push's lease (gap row 557).
- `pull_params` / `push_params` (fn) — the params from the facts; `push_params -> Result<PushParams, String>`. The lease variant needs (a) a rejection of this same push, (b) `RefsRead::Current` and no remote re-read, (c) `HEAD == RejectedPush::sent`; with (a) and a read pending it answers `Err` (`still reading the refs …` / `… the remotes …`), otherwise the plain box (m6-conflicts/49). The lease also waits for what leaves, read for its own key: `Err("still reading what leaves origin/<b> — …")` until then (m7-power-tools/16b).
- `push_box` (fn) — `(params, ahead: Option<usize>, local_tip: Option<Oid>, leaves: Option<&LeavesRead>) -> ConfirmView`: `Push main → origin/main?` with the ahead count, or the force-with-lease box naming the tip it expects; under its history warning, `leaves origin/main:` and one aligned `sha7  subject  author` row per commit (≤ 3, `+N more`), `nothing on … is lost`, or `couldn't read what leaves …: <why>`; drawn with `with_max_lines(10)` (m7-power-tools/16b).
- `busy_text` (fn) — remote operations add `ESC_HINT` to the busy line. `ESC_HINT` (const, ` · esc cancels`) — `view::frame` draws a busy line ending in it without its usual `…`.
- `Stoppable` (enum) — whether `Esc` may stop a running write: `Yes`, or `No(&'static str)` with the line that says why not. Set by whoever launches the write.
- `cancel_operating` / `stop_write` (fn) — `Esc` while any write runs: `CancelJob`, `Busy("<desc> · cancelling")`; a second `Esc` is a no-op. The commit editor's `Esc` goes through `stop_write` too. A `Stoppable::No` write is not asked to stop: the line says so and it keeps running. A standing quit wait is dropped first (`quit::drop_wait`, 13b). `remote::launched` (`#[must_use]`) now returns `Some(Command::Unstoppable(id))` for a `Stoppable::No` write.
- `cancelled` (fn) — `Event::JobCancelled` for the cancelled write: clears `operating`, `Done("<desc> cancelled")`, refreshes everything.
- `remote_progress` (fn) — git's progress onto the busy line, narrowed under 100 columns; ignored while cancelling.
- `remote_done` / `remote_failed` (fn) — the success words (`fetched`, `pulled origin/main`, `pushed main → origin/main`) and the failure line: a non-fast-forward push is remembered and gets the `p`/`P` hint; anything else is `Sticky` with `graft_ops`' own explanation.
- `pushed_since` (fn) — the ` · origin/main still has it` suffix `u` adds when the commit it undid was pushed since; `pushed_to` names that ref. Reads the branch and the push out of the **journal** (`entry_branch`, `latest_push_of`), not out of `remotes`, so it names only a ref that push actually sent (m5/25).
- `UndoAfterPush` (struct) — what the three-answer box is built from: `push` (the journal entry that sent the work), `description`, `remote_side` (read from that entry), and `repush: Result<PushParams, String>` — `Err` carries the sentence saying why there is no third choice (facts unread, off the branch, no upstream, no old tip, `re-push unavailable: entry has no remote tip`). The lease's `expected` is `repush_lease` of the push entry, never `state.remotes`' tracking tip (m6-conflicts/06).
- `undo_after_push` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<UndoAfterPush>`: decided from the journal alone (m5/25). The gate is "this entry moved a branch a later push sent"; whether a re-push is possible is `repush_for`'s answer and never suppresses the box, so a failed remote read can no longer turn `u` into a silent plain undo (rule 3).
- `undo_after_push_box` (fn) — the `ConfirmView`: `Cancel` / `Undo locally`, plus `Re-push old tip` only when `repush` is `Ok`; the third message line is the `Err` sentence when it is not. The command row is blank — the box says what it will do in words (user's decision, m5/25).

## update::journal
The undo journal as this run has read it (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). Re-read on launch, after every write, and by `Ctrl+R` — another Graft on the same worktree appends to it.
- `JournalState` (struct) — `shown`, `selected`, `expanded`, `notice_seen`, `entries: Loading<Arc<jobs::undo::JournalRead>>`, `reading: Option<JobId>` (the read in flight, held apart so a re-read does not blank the list it refreshes), `unreadable: Vec<String>` (accumulated across reads: only the read that recovered an entry can report it).
- `JournalState::read` (fn) — `(next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: marks the read pending under an id `update` chose and asks for it.
- `JournalState::newest_first` (fn) — the entries in the order the browser lists them.
- `journal_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`, the Map view's shape: `J` on the graph or the changes pane (focus moves to the graph, m6-conflicts/85 m2) opens the browser and re-reads the journal; while open, `j`/`k` move, `Space` opens one row's Command Log, `Esc`/`J` close (cancelling a read still in flight), `Enter` acts, and every other key is swallowed rather than moving the graph underneath. `Enter` is `u` on the `← u` row and `U` on the `← U` row; on any **older** row it is the **time machine** (`docs/design/02-keymap.md` §2.11): `update::preview::open_preview(PreviewSubject::RevertTo(entry.id), return_to_journal: true)`, and the browser is *hidden* rather than closed so `Esc` on the preview puts it back on the same row. Nothing runs from this key — only the preview's `Confirm` does (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/08).

## update::clash
A stash put back that clashed (ADR-0045 §2–3, m6-conflicts/72b). **Not a `Stop`**: no git state, no `c`/`a`, no banner; nothing that reads `AppState::stopped` changes.
- `refresh_clash` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&mut AppState) -> Judged`: recomputes `AppState::clash` from `graft_ops::clash::clash_candidate` over the journal as last read, `in_progress`, "anything unmerged" and `refs.stashes()`, tied to `clash_index` by `live_clash`. Waits while the journal, status, refs or index read is out. No candidate → `clash = None`, `clash_index = Unknown`; a candidate with the index unknown → `Judged::NeedsIndex`, value kept; an index read failure → no clash, status `can't tell which stash clashed · <error>` (sanitized). Whenever it leaves `clash = None`, `clash_pane_owed` is dropped (m1, m6-conflicts/82). Same entry keeps its origin; a new one is `PreviousRun` on the launch's first facts (`clash_facts_seen`), `ThisRun` after.
- `index_answered` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(state, commands, id, read, next_id)`: `Product::IndexState`'s arm; another id is dropped, a `stale` answer asks again, else stores the read and re-judges.
- `refresh_and_land` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `refresh_clash` plus the landings (asks `Command::ReadIndexState` on `NeedsIndex`): pays `clash_pane_owed` once the uncommitted row shows and no text field, confirm, preview, finish or commit editor owns the keys (m1), and on a clash newly found from the last run (no modal up) opens the changes pane on the first `CONFLICTS` row (`relaunch::land_on_the_work`). Called when the journal (`journal::journal_event`), working-tree (`update::working_tree_read`) or refs (`took_fact`) read lands.
- `clashed` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(state, launched: Option<&Operation>, EntryId, Clash, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: `operate::operated`'s and `banner::resumed`'s `Operated::Clashed` arm. Index from the launched `StashPop`/`StashApply` when its commit matches (`Running::op`), else from `refs.stashes()` by commit. Sets `clash` (`ThisRun`), `Status::Ready`, refreshes `Everything`, `resolver::land_on_clash` on the first path, notice `stash@{0} clashed in 2 files · esc, u puts it all back`; when the landing is refused (a resolver or box open) the notice goes on the status line as `Status::Waiting` (m2, m6-conflicts/82).
- `stash_name` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `stash@{n}` for a recorded clash, `None` (role alone) for a shell pop; `resolver::side_names`' stash theirs.
- `stop_wordings` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&ClashState, conflicted) -> StopLadder` (`banner::StopLadder { wordings, before_keys }`; `before_keys: true` here, false for git stops — m6-conflicts/82 M1), fullest first: `stash@{0} clashed · 1 file conflicted` (launch: `… clash from last run · …`), `… clashed · 1 conflicted`, `… clashed`, `stash clashed`. Read only by `banner::stop` (pinned by `tests/it/stop_ladder_readers.rs`).
- `kept_status` / `dropped_status` (fns, `pub(crate)`; `dropped_status(index, why: PutBackWhy)` since m6-conflicts/74; since /84 a `SetAside` reads `every file done · set-aside copy dropped ✓ · u brings it back` — what was dropped, the stash not named, ADR-0045 §3 amended) — `every file done · stash@{0} kept` / `every file done · stash@{0} dropped` + keys `u brings it back` (`Status::Done`, tick drawn by the renderer).
- `edits_back` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&Applied) -> Option<String>`: `your edits are back` when the entry set edits aside, then `· notes.md came back from the index` when `Applied::came_back` names files (m8-polish-and-ship/14); the `Status::Done::also` clause after a clean put-back (`operate::operated`, `banner::resumed_words`), m6-conflicts/74, `also` since /84.
- `is_set_aside_entry` / `is_set_aside_clash` (fns, `pub(crate)`; `set_aside_entry` / `set_aside` until m6-conflicts/84) — does this entry carry a `SetAside` put-back / is the live clash one (`resolver::side_names` → `SideRoles::SetAside`; `banner::stop` ` · edits aside`; `clashed`'s `undoes the rebase/switch too` notice), m6-conflicts/74.
- `drops` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `PutBackWhy` → does finishing drop the stash: `Pop`/`SetAside` yes, `Apply` no.

## update::relaunch
The relaunch into an operation git is still part-way through, moved out of `update::journal` (the browser) in the m6-06 review fix, Major 2. `land_on_the_work` is `pub(in crate::update)` since m6-conflicts/72b (`update::clash` and `resolver::land_on_clash` land with it).
- `LiveOrigin` (enum) — `ThisRun`/`PreviousRun`/`OutsideGraft`: whose stop it is, for `banner::stop_reason` (m6-conflicts/12).
- `land_on_live_operation` (fn, `pub(super)`) — replaces `launch_into_live` (m6-conflicts/12): keyed on git's `in_progress` alone, journal or not. Sets `AppState::stopped` (unless a stop of that kind stands), then `banner_focus` + `land_on_the_work`; leaves `Status` alone. Under a modal it only records the stop and sets `live_landing_owed`. `journal_read_landed` upgrades `OutsideGraft` → `PreviousRun` when the journal names a live entry, and lands if the working tree is in and nothing stopped yet; `working_tree_read_landed` lands on the first read, or pays an owed landing once no modal is up. Pinned by `update::relaunch::tests`.
- `launch_into_live` (removed in m6-conflicts/12; history kept below) — the relaunch takeover (PROMPT.md §9, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/14): `live_entries` non-empty **and** `AppState::in_progress` `Some` → `banner_focus` and `Status::Sticky { reason: "<kind> still in progress from a previous run", keys: Some("⏎ resolve  c continue  a abort") }` (`⏎ resolve` only while something is conflicted). Since m6-conflicts/06 it takes `(state, commands, next_id)` and also opens the changes pane (`on_working_tree` set so the pane is not pulled back to a commit) with a `KeptCursor` on the first conflicted path under CONFLICTS, or on STAGED's first file when nothing is conflicted (Decision 2a). Opens no resolver, writes nothing. Called from the **first** journal read and the **first** working-tree read, because it needs a fact from each and either can land second; both being first-reads is what keeps a conflict that happens later in the session — `update::operate`'s to announce — from being worded as a previous run's.

## update::stash
The stash overlay's state (`S`, DESIGN.md §1.11, m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06). Pure: every write it starts is an `Operation` the `ops` crate decides, per the seam rule.
- `StashPrompt` (struct) — the save prompt: `message: graft_ui::text_input::TextField`, `staged_only` (`Tab` flips it).
- `StashState` (struct) — `shown`, `selected`, `entries: Loading<Arc<Vec<graft_git::stash::StashEntry>>>`, `preview: Loading<Arc<FileListView>>` (the selected stash's files, under `Space`), `prompt`.
- `StashState::read` (fn) — `(next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: marks the list pending under an id `update` chose and asks for it.
- `StashState::rows` / `selected_entry` (fn) — the list as drawn, and the entry under the cursor.
- `stash_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`, the journal browser's shape: `S` on the graph or the changes pane (focus moves to the graph, m6-conflicts/85 m2) opens the overlay and re-reads the list; while open `j`/`k` move (dropping the preview), `Space` asks for the selected stash's files through `Command::ListFiles` with `DiffSpec::Commit`, `s` opens the save prompt, `b` opens the name prompt for `git stash branch` (`update::name_prompt`, reading the entry's own `index`/`commit` from `selected_entry`, never from `selected`), `⏎` opens the apply preview and `p` the pop preview (`update::preview::open_preview`, `opened_by` `⏎`/`p`; `y` runs, `esc` returns to the list on the same stash, `⏎ ⏎` writes nothing — m6-conflicts/86), `d` asks the confirm box first, `Esc`/`S` close (cancelling a read in flight), and every other key is swallowed rather than moving the graph underneath. `Tab`/`BackTab` are never claimed, so focus can always reach the detail pane.
- `save_refusal` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<&'static str>`: why `s` would refuse right now (`nothing to stash`, or `nothing to stash — nothing is staged` for `staged only`), `None` while the working-tree status is unread. A preview only; `Operation::check` is the decision.

## update::name_prompt
The one-field box that names a new ref (`b` branch, `t` tag, and the stash overlay's `b`), m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10. Pure: it builds an `Operation` and hands it on, per the seam rule.
- `NameKind` (enum) — `Branch { at, checkout }`, `Tag { at }`, `BranchFromStash { index, commit }`: what is being named, and everything the create needs. The stash kind carries the entry's own index rather than a cursor position, so a list that moves under it cannot rename the wrong entry.
- `NamePrompt` (struct) — `kind`, `field: graft_ui::text_input::TextField`, `refusal: Option<PromptRefusal>`: why the last `⏎` created nothing while the prompt stays open. Holds the cause, not words (m6-conflicts/78, Minor 2): the words are recomputed each frame by `refusal_words`, so `NotNow` goes when the write ends. The prompt's own, never `AppState::status`; drawn as the bottom row's problem line by `view::status::modal_problem`; cleared by the next key or paste (m6-conflicts/69, Decided 3).
- `PromptRefusal` (enum, `pub`) — `EmptyName` (`a branch needs a name` / `a tag needs a name`) or `NotNow` (`not_now`'s words, the typed name kept).
- `not_now` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&AppState, &NameKind) -> Option<String>`: why `⏎` cannot create now — every kind refuses while a write runs (`InFlight::words`); a stash branch also while git is mid-operation (`preview::preview_refusal`). `None` when it may.
- `refusal_words` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: the open prompt's refusal as drawn this frame; `NotNow` recomputed, `None` once its cause is gone.
- `open_name_prompt` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, NameKind)`: opens the box, refusing only while something else modal already has the keyboard.
- `open_name_prompt_with` (fn, m7/24-a) — `(&mut AppState, NameKind, prefill) -> bool`: the same with `prefill` typed, cursor after it (J's `b` after `⏎` offered `<branch>-restored`). A create made while J is shown closes J.
- `open_from_graph` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, tag: bool)`: what `b`/`t` do on a graph row; refuses in words (`b needs a commit row`) on the working-tree row.
- `name_refusal` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<String>`: why the typed name would be refused right now — `exists` (branches for a branch, **tags** for a tag), or `bad name: <the rule>` from the same check `Operation::check` runs. **`None` while the field is empty**: the refusal draws in the warning token, and a box that opens already complaining is the thing CLAUDE.md forbids. `Enter` on an empty name sets `NamePrompt::refusal` to `PromptRefusal::EmptyName` (`a branch needs a name`) instead (m6-conflicts/69: no longer the status line).
- `name_prompt_key` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, KeyPress, next_id) -> bool`: claims **every** key while open (m3/06 Critical 1), `Ctrl+U` clearing, `⇥` flipping the branch's checkout, `Esc` closing and running nothing, `Enter` creating — a switching branch (`⇥`) through `update::switch::switch_into` (`opened_by` `b`, m7-power-tools/10b: the preview with unsaved edits, at once otherwise). Any key first clears `NamePrompt::refusal`.
- `name_prompt_paste` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &str) -> bool`: a bracketed paste is text like any other.
- `create` (fn) — the `Operation` each kind ends in: `BranchCreate`/`TagCreate` go straight to `Command::Operate` (a create moves no history, so there is no result to draw), while `BranchFromStash` moves `HEAD`, the index and the working tree and therefore ends in a Ghost Preview like every other such write — the overlay steps out of the way as the picture opens.

## update::drag
Dragging a ref badge onto a row (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10). The gesture is three ordinary mouse events; the drop opens a menu, and the menu opens a picture, never a write.
- `DragSource` (struct) — `name`, `short`, `at: Oid`, `current`: the branch in the air.
- `DragState` (struct) — `source`, `from_row`, `over: Option<usize>` (the row under the pointer now; `view::entries` paints it `Highlighted`).
- `drag_mouse` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, &mut Vec<Command>, MouseInput) -> bool`: answers **before** `mouse::mouse_event`'s own arms, because a badge in the air owns the button. `Down(Left)` on a badge picks it up, `Drag(Left)` moves `over`, `Up` either drops (`over != from_row` → `drop_menu`) or, having never left the row, falls back to `mouse::select_graph_row` — a press without motion is still a plain click.
- `drag_key` (fn) — `Esc` cancels and is spent; any other key cancels and falls through, so a drag can never swallow a binding.
- `drop_target` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<usize>`: the row the badge is over, for the renderer.
- `drop_menu` (fn) — `(&AppState, &DragSource, target) -> Option<OpenMenu>`: the menu a drop opens, titled `<source> → <target branch or sha7>`. Every verb names both refs. A verb that cannot run is on the list **with its reason** rather than missing: `Merge feature into main` is disabled `check out main first` when the *target* is not `HEAD` — `git merge` merges into `HEAD`, so it is the target's currency that decides, not the source's. A current source offers both reset modes.
- `verb`/`drop_verbs`/`reset_verbs` (fn, private) — the label-and-`Operation` pairs, kept together so the menu's `History(i)` index and the operation it means cannot drift.

## update::radar
The Mistake Radar's own state: pure, no I/O (m2-details-and-diffs/13, 13b).
- `RadarState` (struct) — `context: Loading<Arc<jobs::radar::RadarContext>>`, `policy: graft_ops::mistake_radar::Policy` (`Policy::default()` until `[radar]` config is threaded in — see KNOWN_GAPS.md).
- `RadarState::force_push` (field) — `HashMap<String, Loading<Probed>>` by short branch name: one verdict per upstream-tracked branch (m2-details-and-diffs/15).
- `job_failed` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, JobId, &str)`: called by `update`'s `job_failed` for a crashed job (`Event::JobFailed`). Whichever of `context`, `duplicates` or a `force_push` entry was `Pending(id)` becomes `Failed(words)` — a crashed job leaves `Failed`, never `Pending`. None is re-asked on its own (requesters ask from `Idle` / for branches not in the map); `Ctrl+R` resets them (m7-power-tools/33c).
- `Probed` (struct) — `tip: Oid`, `verdict: ForcePushVerdict`: a force-push verdict with the branch tip it was asked at; placed on that tip by one `RefBadges::on` lookup while the branch still stands there, never by walking every ref (m7-power-tools/12).
- `RadarState::duplicates` (field) — `Loading<Arc<Duplicates>>`; one repo-wide probe over the newest 500 commits, not per branch (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `RadarState::duplicate_findings` (field) — `HashMap<Oid, Finding>`; rebuilt whole each time `duplicates` lands, via `probes::findings_from_duplicates` — this is where `[radar] duplicate = off` takes effect, since the probe itself is policy-blind (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `RadarState::ref_findings` (field) — `HashMap<Oid, Vec<Finding>>`; `classify_refs`'s answer by commit, worked out once per refs/context/policy change by `refresh_ref_findings`, not per row per frame (M2 review C1).
- `RadarState::force_push_findings` (field) — `HashMap<Oid, Vec<Finding>>`; the same for force-push verdicts, keyed by branch tip.
- `RadarState::force_push_not_checkable` (field) — `HashMap<Oid, String>`; the `NotCheckable` reason by branch tip, for `RadarLine::ForcePushNotCheckable`.
- `RadarState::force_push_revision` (field) — `u64`; bumped by `force_push_probed`; invalidates only the two force-push maps (m7-power-tools/12).
- `refresh_ref_findings` (fn) — `(state)`; called at the tail of every `update` (`update::settle`). Rebuilds `ref_findings` (O(refs), `classify_refs`) only when `refs`/`context` (by `Arc::ptr_eq`), `HEAD`, default branch or policy changed, and the two force-push maps (O(verdicts)) when those or `force_push_revision` changed; otherwise returns after that comparison. A probe answer no longer reclassifies every ref (m7-power-tools/12). This is what makes a frame's radar lookup O(1) per row (M2 review C1).
- `branch_facts`/`head_facts` (fns) — pure fact adapters from `AppState` for `classify_refs`; they live here, not in `view::radar`, because `refresh_ref_findings` is the only caller now (M2 review C1).
- `MAX_FORCE_PUSH_PROBES` (const) — `32`; the most force-push probes (and `git` spawns) one session asks for.
- `request_force_push_probes` (fn) — `(state, commands, next_id)`; called at the tail of every `update` beside `request_context`. Idempotent: once the context is `Ready` and the `force_pushed` kind is on, asks one `Command::ForcePushProbe` per upstream-tracked branch that could be seen — the branch `HEAD` is on and the branches on resident rows, by hash lookup, never by walking every ref — up to `MAX_FORCE_PUSH_PROBES` (m2-details-and-diffs/15; bounded by M2 review C1).
- `force_push_probed` (fn) — `(state, branch, tip, verdict)`; lands one verdict under its branch, whatever its job id — these are not tied to the selection. Bumps `force_push_revision`.
- `request_duplicates_probe` (fn) — `(state, commands, next_id)`; called at the tail of every `update` beside `request_context`/`request_force_push_probes`. Idempotent: once the context is `Ready`, asks one `Command::DuplicatesProbe(id, 500)` while `duplicates` is `Idle`; not gated on the `duplicate` kind, unlike the force-push probe (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `duplicates_probed` (fn) — `(state, id, result)`; lands `jobs::radar::run_duplicates_probe`'s answer, dropping it if `id` is stale, and rebuilds `duplicate_findings` (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `radar_event` (fn) — `(state, event)`; routes `Product::RadarContext`, `Product::ForcePushProbe` and `Product::DuplicatesProbe` to `context_landed`/`force_push_probed`/`duplicates_probed`.
- `request_context` (fn) — `(state, commands, next_id)`; asks for the radar context once the default branch's tip is known on `state` and the context is `Idle` (never asked). `Failed` is an answer: it waits for `Ctrl+R` (`reset_for_refresh` → `Idle`), never re-asked per update (m7-power-tools/33). Called at the end of every `update`, which covers both startup and refresh: neither knows the tip until the refs job answers.
- `context_landed` (fn) — `(state, id, result)`; lands `jobs::radar::run_radar_context`'s answer, dropping it if `id` is stale.
- `reset_for_refresh` (fn) — `Ctrl+R` throws the context, every force-push verdict and the duplicates probe away: a fetch is when a remote can have been rewritten (m2-details-and-diffs/15, 16).

## update::map
The Map view's own state and keys (`M`); built fresh on open, thrown away on close — cheap enough to redo every time. No drawing here — `view::map`, a later ticket, draws whatever `MapState::data` holds (m2-details-and-diffs/18).
- `MapState` (struct) — `shown: bool`, `data: Loading<Arc<crate::map::MapData>>`, `progress: Option<(u32, u32)>` (done, total rows), `selected: usize` (row under the cursor).
- `MapState::open` (fn) — `(&mut self, req: MapRequest, next_id: &mut impl FnMut() -> JobId) -> Vec<Command>`: shows the view, resets selection/progress, returns one `Command::BuildMap` under an id `next_id` hands out.
- `MapState::close` (fn) — `(&mut self) -> Vec<Command>`: hides the view and discards the data either way; a still-`Pending` build gets a `Command::CancelJob`.
- `MapState::selected_tip` (fn) — `(&self) -> Option<Oid>`: the selected row's tip commit, once `data` is `Ready`.
- `buckets_for` (fn) — `(columns: u16) -> usize`: `max(10, columns − 24)`.
- `request` (fn) — `(state: &AppState) -> MapRequest`: `buckets` off the terminal's columns, `clock`/`policy` off `AppState`, `previous_default_tips` from the radar context if `Ready` else empty; `build_map` derives `default_branch`/`remotes` itself from the `RepoStore`, so `MapRequest` carries none of that.
- `map_key` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(next, commands, key) -> bool`: `M` opens (only on the graph screen, detail focus on the graph); while shown, `M`/`Esc` close (cancelling a pending build), `↑`/`↓`/`j`/`k` move the selection, `Enter` seeks the graph to the selected row's tip and closes the view (`seek_selected`, via `History::seek`); `true` when the key was this view's to answer.
- `seek_to(next, commands, tip)` (fn, `pub(super)`) — the seek `seek_selected` makes, split out so the branch picker's `Tab` lands the same way (m7-power-tools/10): cursor on `tip` if loaded, else `History::seek` + `Command::ShowRows` on the live walk.
- `map_event` (fn, `pub(super)`) — routes `Product::Map`/`Progress::Map` to `MapState::produced`/`progressed`, dropping a stale id.
- `resized` (fn, `pub(super)`) — while shown, cancels the running build and opens a new one sized for the new width.

## update::palette
- `update::palette` (mod, `pub(crate)`, m8-polish-and-ship/11, §2.12) — `?` (help on this place) and `⌃P`/`:` (everything). `PaletteState { mode, query: TextField, cursor, opened_from }`, held boxed in `AppState::palette` (`text_field_open` includes it; `context_of` → `Palette`). `open_key` (in `keys::key_event`'s claim chain after `drag_key`; refuses over a text field or a confirm box), `palette_key` (in `dispatch` after the credential), `paste`. `rows(state, palette)` / `listing(Source, palette)` → `Vec<Group>`: `YOUR CONFIG` (his config problems) first; `?`: `IN PROGRESS` (Banner rows, while an operation is live), `HERE`, `EVERYWHERE`; `⌃P`: `ACTIONS` plus `Theme: dark|light|high contrast`. Each key spelled with what pressing it does in `opened_from` (`Keymap::acting`), `—` when none. Filter: `graft_core::matcher` over label and search words; a query that is a chord bound there heads the list. `⏎` runs the row's **default** chord through `dispatch_key` (as its key would, refusals included); a theme row emits `Command::Retheme(kind)`. `view` / `view_of` → `graft_ui::palette::PaletteView`; `bar()` the bottom row while up. Drawn by `view::frame::boxes::draw_palette` before the modal boxes.
- `Command::Retheme(ThemeKind)` (variant, m8/11) — the loop's, never a job (`jobs::runner` `not_a_job`): `turn::rethemed` takes it out before `carry_out`.

## update::picker
The branch picker (`C`, m7-power-tools/10; keymap §2.16). A text field over the graph, **not** a `GraphOverlay` (it replaces nothing: the graph above stays drawn); `text_field_open` names it while shown.
- `PickerState` (struct) — `query: TextField`, `list: Loading<Arc<SwitchList>>`, `selected` (index into `rows()`), `under_preview` (a switch preview it opened is up), `note` (why `⏎` did nothing), `last_click` (row and ms, for a double-click). `rows()` → `graft_ops::switch_list::filter_switch_rows`; `selected_row()`; `is_previous(row)`.
- `shown(state) -> bool` (fn) — up and not hidden under its preview.
- `open_picker(state) -> Vec<Command>` (fn) — opens it, `Command::ReadSwitchList` under a local id.
- `picker_key(state, commands, key) -> bool` (fn) — while shown claims every key but `Ctrl+C`: printable keys typed (cursor to the best row), `Backspace`, `↑`/`↓`, `⏎` (`here` → note `you're on <b>`; `preview::preview_refusal` → its words as the note; else `switch::switch_into(…, "C")`: a preview hides the picker, an instant launch closes it), `Tab` (close, `map::seek_to` the tip), `Esc` (close, `CancelJob` a pending read). Hidden: `C` opens it on the graph screen, graph focused, no overlay or modal up. Routed in `keys::key_event` after the context menu, before the commit editor.
- `listed(state, id, result)` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `Product::SwitchList`; a stale id is dropped.
- `picker_box_in(state, pane) -> (x, y, w, h)` (fn) — where the box goes in the graph pane: over the rows the graph draws (below `geometry::rows_above_graph`, above the footer and the commit-graph line), then `graft_ui::picker::picker_box`. Read by both the drawing and the mouse.
- `picker_mouse(state, commands, mouse) -> bool` (fn, `pub(super)`) — while shown claims every mouse event (modal): a left click on a row selects it, a second on the same row within 500 ms switches (`⏎`'s path). Called first-ish in `mouse::mouse_event`, before the context menu.
- `preview_closed(state)` / `preview_confirmed(state)` (fns, `pub(super)`) — called by `preview::close_preview` and `refused` (shown again) and `confirm_preview` (closed): the picker's parallel of `return_to_journal`.

## update::switch
The switch rule (m7-power-tools/10b): one function every switch goes through — `.` Checkout, the name prompt's `⇥`, and (ticket 10) the branch picker. Detached target → preview; changed tracked files in the last status read → preview with the set-aside line (`OnEdits::SetAside`); otherwise launched at once with `OnEdits::Refuse`. Untracked files alone never count.
- `SwitchTarget` (enum) — `Branch(full name)`, `Track { remote: Tip, local }`, `Detached(Oid)`, `NewBranch { name, at }`.
- `switch_to(state, target, opened_by, next_id) -> Vec<Command>` (fn) — the rule. `opened_by` is the key the preview names (`.`, `b`). Every refusal is worded on the status line by the path that met it; refs not read yet → `Status::Waiting` (the push box's sentence, duplicated: that constant is private to `remote::push_box`).
- `switch_into(state, commands, target, opened_by, next_id) -> bool` (fn, `pub(super)`) — the same, saying whether the preview opened or the write launched (the name prompt restores itself on `false`).
- `previews(state, target) -> bool` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — rules 1–2: detached, or staged/unstaged entries in `AppState::working_tree`.
- `target_of(op) -> Option<SwitchTarget>` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — the switch a `Checkout` names; `None` for every other kind.
- `instant_refused(state, op, refusal, next_id) -> Option<Vec<Command>>` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `operate::operated`'s failed arm: a `Refuse` switch refused `DirtyTree` (edits made since the read) opens the `SetAside` preview of the same switch instead of a refusal line. `opened_by` from `remote.running.opened_by`, else `.` for a checkout, `b` for a create.
- `landed_words(op) -> Option<String>` (fn, `pub(crate)`) — `on <b>` / `on <local> (new, tracks origin/x)` / `on <nb>` for a `BranchCreate` with `checkout` (`b <name> ⇥`, review m7-16b..21b Minor 1); `None` for a detached checkout (today's words) and every other kind. `operate::done_status` draws `on <b> ✓ · [your edits are back · ]u goes back`; the staging-not-kept line uses it as its `what`.
- `GOES_BACK` (const, `pub(crate)`) — `u goes back`.

## update::strip
The overview strip's only key (`O`, m2-details-and-diffs/20); no other state.
- `strip_key` (fn, `pub(super)`) — `(next, key) -> bool`: `O` on the graph screen (plain, detail focus on the graph, map not shown) flips `AppState::strip_shown`; `true` once handled.

## update::place
Blame places and their trail (m7-power-tools/20e; split in 20i). `place.rs` the state, `open_blame`, `blamable`; `place/answer.rs` the job's request and answer and `blame_arrived`; `place/trail.rs` the trail's life (`trail_back`, `leave`, `after_commands`, `show_again`, `esc_goes_back`, `end_for_compare`) — every way off the trail ends a `⏎` landing still on its way: `leave` calls `abandon` first, before any early return, so `Esc` off the last place, a refused read, a write (`after_commands`) and a new pin (`end_for_compare`) all drop it; `show_again` calls `abandon` itself (20n, gap M7/20k-q3); `place/blame_state.rs` `BlameState`'s methods (`row_count`, `cursor_line`, `matches`, `ignore_line`, `view`); `place/from.rs` what `B` blames; `place/keys.rs` a shown place's keys and mouse; `place/rows.rs` the rows as drawn; `place/write.rs` `is_write`. Tests: `place/tests.rs` (builders) + `place/tests/{open,navigation,pinned_base,keys,mouse,hints,esc_back}.rs`.
- `esc_goes_back(state) -> bool` (fn, `pub(crate)`, `trail.rs`) — `Esc` on the graph screen would show the hidden trail now: trail hidden, no held failure, none of the graph's own open things (drag, modal, menu, overlay, operation, Explain, compare base, search, Focus). The one predicate: `show_again(state, commands) -> bool` (the pane's `Esc` only — `escape_key` no longer calls it and `debug_assert!`s `!esc_goes_back`, since `detail_key` runs before its ladder, m7-power-tools/34; drops a landing still seeking, as `leave` does, withdrawing its seek — 20k), `detail_key`'s pane-close guard and `view::rows::back_to_blame` read it (20i, keymap §2.1 pane clause).
- `BlameState::cursor_line() -> Option<u32>` — the final line under the cursor once read.
- `BlameState::ignore_line()` — the answer's list; while a re-read runs, the last answer's count worded for the option chosen (20i Minor 7).
- `BlameState::view` — sanitises `Failed` words (`sanitize_line`) before the picture gets them.
- `Place` (enum) — `Blame(Box<BlameState>)` / `History(Box<HistoryState>)` (m7-power-tools/21).
- `Trail` (struct) — `places` (newest last, `TRAIL_MAX` = 16, oldest dropped), `shown` (false after `⏎` landed: waiting under the graph), `origin`; `is_live`, `hidden`, `on_screen`/`top`/`top_mut` (a blame place only; `None` when history is on top), `history_on_screen`, `top_history`, `top_history_mut`, `place_shown` (either kind). `AppState::trail` holds it. `AppState::graph_overlay` asks `place_shown()`, so a history place is drawn in the real frame too (m7-power-tools/21b).
- History place (m7-power-tools/21): `place/history.rs` `HistoryState` (`rows`, `selected_row`, `range_at`, `top_line`, `view`, `title`), `HistoryFrom` (`Lines` from blame's `L` / `File` from the pane's `F`), `TraceLoad`, `FileLoad`, `open_history`, `f_key`, `next_change`; `place/history_keys.rs` `history_key`/`history_mouse` (j/k, PgUp/PgDn, Tab, n/N, v, w (`ignore_whitespace`, 21c), L, B, ⏎, Esc; `NO_MORE_CHANGES`); `place/history_answer.rs` `TraceRequest`, `trace_arrived`, `whole_file_arrived`. Jobs: `Command::TraceHistory`/`Product::Trace` (`jobs::detail::read_trace`, `jobs/detail/history.rs`: working-tree lines mapped to `HEAD`, the enclosing function, else the block) and `Command::ReadWholeFile` (`ignore_whitespace`)/`Product::WholeFile(WholeFileAnswer { view, sizes })` (`read_whole_file`; a binary diff also reads `blob_sizes`, 21c); routed by `jobs::runner::dispatch_history`. Tests: `place/tests/history.rs`, `tests/it/history_place.rs`, `tests/it/m7_walk/history.rs`.
- `Origin` (struct) — where `B` was pressed: spec, path, line, `section: Option<WorkingTreeSection>` (the changes-pane section the line counts in, from `DiffCursor::section`, handed to `show_line` on leaving; 20m), focus, and `base: Option<CompareBase>` — a pinned compare base **set aside** while the trail lives, pinned again when it ends (20's pinned-base ruling). Leaving restores graph cursor, spec, file, focus and line via `show_line` only if something moved.
- `BlameState`, `BlameLoad` (`Reading { job, want, came_from, ignore_listed }` / `Failed(String)` / `Ready { blame, rows }`), `Want`, `BlameRequest`, `CameFromRequest`, `BlameAnswer`, `BlameFailure` (`Untracked` / `Binary` / `Failed`) — a place and its job (`Command::ReadBlame`, `Product::Blame`, run by `jobs::detail::read_blame`, cancellable; its errors mapped by `jobs::detail::blame::blame_failure`, `jobs/detail/blame.rs`). `Reading` keeps `^`'s `came_from` so `w`/`i` resend it; a `Want::CameFrom` answer with no line fails the place `can't tell where line <n> came from in <sha7> · esc back`.
- `BlameFrom` (enum), `NoBlame` (enum), `blame_from(cursor, head, staged_and_unstaged)` (fn) — design note B1's table: what `B` blames from the diff cursor, or why not.
- `came_from(hunks, new_line) -> u32` (fn) — where a line came from in the parent: the hunk's first old line (a pure add: its start, ≥ 1), else shifted by the hunks before.
- `open_blame(state, from) -> Vec<Command>` (fn) — push a place for `from` and ask for it.
- `blamable(state) -> bool` (fn) — `B` would answer: pane shown and focused, a diff cursor — commit-graph offer or not (m7-power-tools/36). The bar's `B blame` reads it.
- `place_key(state, key) -> Vec<Command>` (fn) — a shown place's keys: `j/k`, arrows, `PgUp/PgDn`, `g/G`, `space` ages, `/` find, `n/N`, `w`, `i` (only with the ignore file), `⏎` that commit (trail hidden), `^` before it, `Esc` (find → held status → back).
- `trail_back(state) -> Option<Vec<Command>>` (fn) — pop the newest place, cancelling its read; the last one leaves the trail. Not the spec's `-> bool`: leaving is `show_line`'s commands.
- `is_write(command) -> bool` (fn) — exhaustive over `Command`: true for `Operate`, `Resume`, `RevertTo`, `Undo`, `Redo`, `TakeSide`, `OpenExternalEditor`, `OpenConflictEditor`, `OpenMergetool`. Any of them in an event's commands ends the trail (`after_commands`).
- `TRAIL_MAX` (const) — 16.
- Key chain: a shown place is claimed in `update::keys::key_event` after the banner (`place_claim`), every key but `?`, `q`, `⌃C`, `⌃R`; `detail::keys::place_key` holds the pane's `Esc` (show a hidden trail) and `B` rungs; `escape_key` never sees `Esc` with a hidden trail (`place_key` took it; a `debug_assert!` holds that, m7-power-tools/34); `mouse.rs` gives a shown place every mouse event.
- `GraphOverlay::Place` — after `Finish` and `Resolver`; drawn by `view::frame::overlays::draw_place` (the whole frame, its bar on the last row); `view::rows::place_context` its bar (`HintContext::Blame`, or `HintContext::History { rows, on_file }` for a history place, 21b), `back_to_place` (fn, `pub(crate)`; was `back_to_blame`) the `esc back to blame` / `esc back to history` lead, by the trail top's kind.

## update::detail
The detail pane's state and transitions; pure (no I/O, no clock). Drawing is `detail-pane-frame`'s. Split into files, public items unchanged (m2 review M8): `state.rs` (`DetailState` and its caches, `PaneSize`, `DetailSpec`, `Loading`, `DiffKey`, `Bounded`), `ops.rs` (`impl DetailState`), `control.rs` (`LocalJobs`, `detail_event`, `effective_layout`, `tab_hint_rows`, `open_explain`/`close_explain`, the diff-length helpers), `keys.rs` (`detail_key` and its region-local key maps — the pane claims `space` in both lists: the files list folds a directory, the diff claims it with no effect in `diff_option_key`, so it never reaches the graph (m7-power-tools/20l), plus `enter_opens` — whether `⏎` has anything left to open, which the hint bar reads too so the two cannot disagree, m5/31).
- `LOCAL_JOB_BIT` (const) — top bit of every id `update` issues itself, so it never meets a runner-issued id.
- `Side` (enum) — `Old`, `New`: which side of a diff a line number counts in (`cursor.rs`, m7-power-tools/20b).
- `LineRef` (struct) — `{ side: Side, line: u32 }`, 1-based in that side's file.
- `DiffCursor` (struct) — `{ spec, section, path, old_path, binary, at: Option<LineRef> }`: what the focused pane's cursor is on. `binary` from the diff once arrived, else from the file list's missing counts on a file that is not untracked.
- `diff_cursor` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> Option<DiffCursor>`; `None` with the graph focused, the pane hidden, or the files cursor on no file. `at`: added/context → `New` new number; removed → `Old` old number (side by side: a removed line paired with an added one → the pair's `New`); a heading → its hunk's first changed line (first added, else first removed); a file row (files focused) → the file's first changed line from the loaded diff, `None` before it arrives.
- `show_line` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, DetailSpec, &Path, Option<LineRef>, Option<WorkingTreeSection>) -> Vec<Command>` (`landing.rs`, m7-power-tools/20b; `section` 20m): graph cursor to the spec's row (`Commit(c)` → `c`; `Compare { head }` → `head`, base kept; `WorkingTree` → the uncommitted-changes row), pane opened on the spec, `path` selected — searched among **all** rows, its folded ancestor directories unfolded (20m) — then the line cursor on `at` (nearest row at or before it on that side; `None` = the first change) with the diff focused; the hunk heading tops the view if the cursor row then fits, else the cursor sits a third down. A seek that misses → sticky `can't show <short> on the graph: <words>` (`not in this view`, `the history walk has stopped`, `no history has loaded`), nothing moves. A path not in the spec's files → sticky `<path> isn't among <short>'s files`, the pane as it opens. `WorkingTree` with a clean tree → sticky `can't show the uncommitted changes: there are none`. `Commit(c)` while a compare base other than `c` is pinned → sticky `can't show <c> on its own while compare base <base> is pinned · x on the base clears it`, nothing moves (20g; a pinned base would turn the landing into a compare). `section` (`WorkingTree` only; `debug_assert!` `None` otherwise): `Some(s)` → the path's row in `s`, `at` in that row's numbering (no row in `s` → the path's first row, `at: None`); `None` → `at` counts **on disk**: a path with one row takes it as given; a partly staged path (Staged and Unstaged rows, no Unmerged) is selected on its Unstaged row, and once that diff is in, `disk_line_in` either keeps it there (an unstaged line) or moves the landing to the Staged row at the line's index number (`Selecting` again, `section: Some(Staged)`); an Unmerged row is the first row, as before. Callers: blame `⏎` passes `None`; the trail's leave passes `Origin::section`.
- `disk_line_in` (fn, `pub(crate)`) / `DiskLine` (enum, `pub(crate)`: `Unstaged(u32)`, `Index(u32)`) — `(&FileDiff, u32) -> DiskLine` (`landing/disk.rs` since 20o, with `index_line`; `select`, `row_is`, `unfold_to`, `say_missing` in `landing/select.rs`; 20m), pure: disk line `L` against the Unstaged (index → disk) diff. Added line at `L` → `Unstaged(L)`; context line → `Index(old_no)`; outside every hunk → `Index(L − Σ(new_lines − old_lines))` over hunks ending above `L` (a pure deletion's `new_lines == 0` hunk ends at `new_start`); a truncated diff with `L` below its last shown line → `Unstaged(L)`.
- `LineLanding` (struct) / `LandingStage` (enum: `Seeking(Oid)`, `Opening`, `Selecting`, `Placing`) — the pending landing on `AppState::line_landing`, with `section: Option<WorkingTreeSection>` (`show_line`'s; 20m); one at a time, a new `show_line` replaces it. `abandon(state, commands)` (fn, `pub(crate)`, 20k) drops it and withdraws the graph's seek it started (a `ShowRows` for the viewport replaces the find), so the walk's answer does not move the graph; its callers are `show_again` and `leave` (which every other way off the trail goes through: `trail_back`, `answer::refuse`, `after_commands`, `end_for_compare` — 20n). `advance` (fn, `pub(crate)`) moves it on, called at the end of `update::settle` after `follow_selection`. `LandingWatch` (`pub(crate)`) wraps every key and mouse event in `update::answer`: a key or click that moves the graph cursor or its seek, the working-tree flag, the pane's shown/spec or its files cursor drops a landing it did not start itself.
