- `LocalJobs` (struct) — `next_id`: hands out `LOCAL_JOB_BIT` ids.
- `DetailSpec` (enum) — `Commit(Oid)`, `Compare { base, head }`, `WorkingTree`; `diff_spec` maps it to git's `DiffSpec`.
- `CompareBase` (struct) — `oid`, `row`: a pinned compare base (`x`, m2-details-and-diffs/11); `AppState::compare_base` holds it.
- `toggle_compare` (fn) — `x` on a row, or `Shift`/`Ctrl`+click on a second one: no base pins one and opens the pane on `Compare { base, head: base }`, focus kept (m7-14); the same `oid` again clears it (no command); a different `oid` opens `DetailSpec::Compare` and keeps the base pinned.
- `Loading<T>` (enum) — `Idle`, `Pending(JobId)`, `Ready(T)`, `Failed(String)`.
- `DiffKey` (struct) — spec, entry, options, max_lines: one file's diff; the diff cache's key.
- `Bounded<K, V>` (struct) — small map that evicts its oldest entry when full; `new`, `get`, `insert`, `clear`.
- `DetailState` (struct) — spec, shown, focus, files (`Loading` of view + git list), diff, `diff_cache` (8), `contained` (64), options, mode, word_tint, max_lines (5,000), body_top, `pane_size` (`PaneSize`, per-orientation, ADR-0028).
- `DetailState::compare_followed` (field) — `Option<Oid>`: the cursor's commit when a pinned compare last followed it (m7-14); an unmoved cursor leaves a picked compare alone. On the base row the pane follows to `Compare { base, head: base }`. The base row's badges start with `x base` (`view::entries::row_entry`); `update::geometry::badge_at` skips it. `update::place::blame_from` takes `split: Option<Oid>`: a compare's old side blames the split.
- `DetailState::evil` (field) — `Loading<graft_ops::mistake_radar::probes::EvilVerdict>`: the evil-merge verdict for `evil_at`'s commit (m2-details-and-diffs/14).
- `DetailState::evil_cache` (field) — `Bounded<Oid, EvilVerdict>` (16): verdicts already probed; never cleared by `refresh`, because a merge's tree cannot change.
- `DetailState::evil_at` (field) — `Option<Oid>`: which commit `evil` is about; what `view::radar` matches a row against.
- `DetailState::probe_evil` (fn) — `(&mut self, selected: Option<Oid>, &Policy, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: idempotent, called once at the tail of every `update` (`selection::follow_selection`, which passes the selected merge whether or not the pane is open — M2 review M3, so the graph row is marked too; never a stash commit, and nothing until the refs are read — m6-conflicts/66). Nothing when nothing is selected or the evil-merge kind is off (a pending probe is cancelled); cache hit → `Ready`, miss → `Pending` + `Command::EvilProbe`. Asks only when the evil state is `Idle` or belongs to another commit: a `Failed` (crashed) probe for the selected commit waits for the selection to move (m7-power-tools/33c) or a `refresh` (20o).
- `DetailState::evil_job_failed` (fn) — `(&mut self, JobId, &str)`: the evil probe's job crashed → `evil` is `Failed(words)` (drawn `evil merge: not checkable — <why>`), never left `Pending`; not cached (m7-power-tools/33c).
- `DetailState::evil_probed` (fn) — stale-dropped by id; caches the verdict and shows it.
- `DetailState::explain_requested` (field) — `Option<Oid>`: `Explain` chosen from the context menu on a marked row (`context-actions-menu`, m2-details-and-diffs/12); read and cleared by `open_explain` (m2-details-and-diffs/17).
- `DetailState::explain` (field) — `Option<graft_ui::detail::ExplainView>`: the open Explain panel, set by `open_explain`, cleared by `close_explain`.
- `DetailState::explain_highlight` (field) — `Vec<Oid>`: the commits `Explanation::highlight` named; `view::entries` fades every other row while `explain` is `Some`.
- `DetailState::kept_cursor` (field) — `Option<KeptCursor>`: where the changes-pane cursor was when a refresh started; set by `refresh`, spent by `files_arrived`, `None` otherwise.
- `KeptCursor` (struct, re-exported from `update::detail` since m6-conflicts/06) — `{ path: Vec<u8>, section: Option<WorkingTreeSection>, position: usize, body: usize }`. Without it every write dropped the cursor on the first file — usually one under STAGED, where the next `Space` means the opposite write (m3/04 review M7).
- `PaneSize` (struct) — `right_columns`/`below_rows: Option<u16>`; `for_layout(&DetailLayout)` picks the orientation's value, `None` for `Tab`. Persisted via `crate::layout::load_split`/`save_split` (m2-details-and-diffs/10b).
- `effective_layout` (fn) — the pane's actual `DetailLayout` for a screen size: breakpoint kind first, then that kind's `PaneSize` override folded back in (two-step compute, `view/frame.rs` and `detail_key` both use it).
- `tab_hint_rows` (fn) — `(&AppState) -> u16`: the rows the **Tab** layout reserves for the pane's own hint bar — `1` while the Detail tab is up, `0` everywhere else (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/29, ADR-0037 §4). Below 100 columns the shown tab owns the screen and the graph screen is not drawn, so its footer is not there to borrow; the pane that owns the screen owns the keys and draws its own `HintContext` into a reserved bottom row, at the cost of one row of pane content. `0` on the Graph tab (the graph's own footer is already there) and `0` while the operation banner has focus (it is modal and already draws its keys on the bottom row, `tab_banner_rows` == 2). `view::frame::tab_content_area`/`draw_tab_hints` and `update::geometry::detail_split` read this one number, the same way they share `tab_banner_rows` — three copies of a row budget is how a click lands a row off (m5/25).
- `DetailState::resize_pane` (fn) — `<`/`>` (ADR-0028): steps the focused divider by 4 cells, clamped to the pane's and the graph's minimums; `false` (no change, no command) in `Tab`, hidden, or at a bound.
- `DetailState::set_pane_cells` (fn) — divider drag: sets the orientation's cells straight from the pointer, same clamps and no-op cases as `resize_pane`.
- `DetailState::set_options` (fn) — new `DiffOptions`, re-shows the cursor's diff (cache hit or `ReadFileDiff`). Backs `w`/`[`/`]`. Keeps the line cursor on the same side and line number, and its offset in the view (`kept_line`, m7-power-tools/20b).
- `DetailState::load_all` (fn) — `max_lines = usize::MAX`, re-shows the cursor's diff. Backs `L`. Keeps the line cursor's line as `set_options` does.
- `DetailState::kept_line` (field) / `KeptLine` (struct, re-exported) — `{ at: LineRef, offset }`: the line `w`/`[`/`]`/`L` left the cursor on, spent by `diff_arrived` (or at once on a cache hit); any other diff shown first (`show_cursor`) drops it (m7-power-tools/20b).
- `DetailState::body_cursor` (field + fn) — the diff region's line cursor, a flattened body row. **Every spec** has it and the view draws it (`▎` beside the sign, the carrier the changes pane already had) since m7-power-tools/20b; only the changes pane writes from it (`operate::diff_unit`/`cursor_line` check the spec). `move_body_cursor` backs `j`/`k`/`↑`/`↓`/PgUp/PgDn/`g`/`G` in every diff: the cursor leads, the view scrolls only when the cursor would leave it.
- `DetailState::scroll_body` (fn) — clamps `body_top` by a signed delta against the diff's line count. The fallback of `move_body_cursor` when the body's height is unknown (`0`).
- `DetailState::scroll_body_by` (fn) — same move as `scroll_body`, content length looked up from whatever diff is currently loaded. Backs the mouse wheel over the diff body (`detail-resize-and-persistence`).
- `DetailState::dragging` (field) — a divider drag is in progress: set by a mouse `Down` on the seam, cleared by `Up`. Never persisted (`detail-resize-and-persistence`).
- `DetailState::open` (fn) — shows the pane on a spec, cancels a pending list, asks for files (and contained-in once per oid).
- `DetailState::close` (fn) — hides the pane, focus back to the graph.
- `DetailState::selected_entry` (fn) — the files cursor's git entry, if any.
- `DetailState::follow` (fn) — selection moved while shown: re-open on the new spec, keeping focus.
- `DetailState::refresh` (fn) — `Ctrl+R` and every write's reload: clears both caches, notes the changes-pane cursor in `kept_cursor`, re-asks the spec; a `Failed` evil probe goes back to `Idle` so the next `probe_evil` asks once more (20o).
- `DetailState::files_arrived` (fn) — stale-dropped by id; `Ok` → `Ready`, first diff asked (or the message shown). On the changes pane the cursor comes back where `kept_cursor` says: same path in the same section, else the same place down that section, else the fresh list's own cursor. The line cursor comes back only when the file did (m3/04 review M7).
- `DetailState::move_files_cursor` (fn) — `j`/`k` on the files list: cache hit → `Ready`, else cancel + `ReadFileDiff`.
- `DetailState::diff_arrived` (fn) — stale-dropped by id; `Ok` cached + `Ready`, `Err` → `Failed`. A submodule diff takes its row's `changed_inside`/`untracked_inside` first, by the raw diff's path (`update::detail::inside_facts::with_inside_facts`, private; also on a cache hit; review m7-16b..21b Minor 3) — `graft-ui`'s body draws the diff's own facts.
- `DetailState::contained_arrived` (fn) — `(id, oid, result)`: cached by commit whatever the selection now is, but only the query in flight (`contained_job`); one `load` or `abandon` cancelled is dropped by id (G-settle).
- `DetailState::contained_job` / `DetailState::abandoned` (`pub(crate)` fields, G-settle) — the "contained in" query in flight (cancelled by `load` on another commit); `abandoned`: `abandon` cancelled the spec's reads, so `follow` to the same spec reads again and the body draws `reading…`.
- `DetailState::abandon` (fn, `pub(crate)`, G-settle) — mid-burst: `CancelJob` for the pending file list, diff, contained-in and evil probe; the pane keeps what it shows. Idempotent.
- `DetailState::cycle_focus` (fn) — `Tab`/`Shift+Tab`: Graph→Files→Diff→Graph while shown.
- `open_explain` (fn) — `(&mut AppState, next_id) -> Vec<Command>`: answers `DetailState::explain_requested`, if any this tick; opens the pane on that commit, resolves its finding via `view::radar::findings_for` (the same selected-row lookup `view::detail::commit_header` uses — a marked row is always the cursor row) and `graft_ops::mistake_radar::explain::explain`, and fills `DetailState::explain`/`explain_highlight`. A stale request (no finding) still opens the pane; `explain` is left alone (m2-details-and-diffs/17).
- `close_explain` (fn) — `(&mut AppState)`: clears `explain`/`explain_highlight`, leaving the pane open on the same commit. `Esc`'s first press while Explain is open (`update::detail::escape_priority`, ahead of ADR-0023's own `Escape if detail.shown` arm).

## search
- `Query` (struct) — a case-insensitive literal needle; `parse` (None when blank), `text`, `matches(id, subject, author, body)` (sha prefix only when all hex).
- `SearchState` (enum) — `Running{hits}` / `Done{hits}` / `Cancelled{hits}` / `Failed(String)`; `describe` words each distinctly ("N matches so far", "no matches", "search cancelled, N matches found", "search failed: …").
- `Scanned` (enum) — how `scan` ended: `Finished` or `Stopped`.
- `scan` (fn) — walks every ref chunk by chunk, handing each chunk's hits to `found`; stops on `stop` or when `found` returns false.
- `Search` (struct) — the search on screen: query, owning job, hit batches (`Arc` so state clones stay cheap), current hit, prior cursor/top for Esc, `seeking`/`missed`; `hits`, `hit`, `step(forward)` (wraps), `line` (footer text).

## repository
- `RepositoryFacts` (struct) — what one open repository told Graft.
- `RepositoryFacts::path` (field) — working tree root, or the repo dir itself if bare.
- `RepositoryFacts::bare` (field) — no working tree.
- `RepositoryFacts::head` (field) — where `HEAD` points.
- `RepositoryFacts::mailmap` (field) — the repository's resolved `.mailmap`, for the author chip.
- `RepositoryFacts::commit_graph` (field) — `CommitGraphFact` (m1-the-graph/54).
- `CommitGraphFact` (enum) — `Present { commits }`, `Absent { declined }`, `Shallow` (a non-empty `ShallowList`: git reads no commit-graph there, so the offer is `NotNeeded` and `B`/`X` are not taken; m7/20j), `Unreadable(msg)` (shown as a problem, no offer; also an unreadable shallow file).
- `Head` (enum) — where `HEAD` points, one step before the sanitiser.
- `facts` (fn) — read facts about an open repository.

## config
Internal split (m1-the-graph/72c, public paths unchanged): `types` (`Config`,
`Ui`, `Graph`, `Radar` and their value enums — `ThemeChoice`, `RendererChoice`,
`IconSet`, `RadarSetting`), `load` (`Loaded`, `ConfigWarning`, `ConfigError`,
`load`, `load_from`, `default_path` and the parsing internals), both
re-exported from `mod.rs`.
- `Config` (struct) — the config file's settings.
- `Config::ui` (field) — `[ui]`: how Graft looks.
- `Config::graph` (field) — `[graph]`: how history is drawn.
- `Config::diff` (field) — `[diff]`: the detail pane's diff options at startup (m2-details-and-diffs/10).
- `Config::radar` (field) — `[radar]`: Mistake Radar per-kind severity overrides (m2-details-and-diffs/13).
- `Config::theme` (field, m8-16) — `[theme.<kind>]`: `ThemeOverrides`, read off the raw table by `config::theme::overrides` (serde skip).
- `ThemeOverrides` (struct, m8-16) — `dark`/`light`/`high_contrast: Vec<(Token, Rgb)>`, key order; `for_kind(ThemeKind)`. Kinds `dark`, `light`, `high-contrast` or `high_contrast` (`config/theme.rs`).
- `theme_problems` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-16) — `(&ThemeOverrides, no_color) -> Vec<String>`: every contrast miss in all three kinds as `[theme.<kind>] <ContrastMiss::words>`; empty under `NO_COLOR`. Appended after `keymap_from`'s list by `Arrival::first(config, no_color)`.
- `Config::keys` (field) — `[keys]`: `Keys { suspend_on_ctrl_z }` (ADR-0002), the one key whose meaning is a preference (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05).
- `Radar` (struct) — `[radar]` section: one `RadarSetting` per `MistakeRadarKind` (`foxtrot`, `backward`, `accidental_pull`, `evil`, `duplicate`, `force_pushed`, `risky_state`).
- `Radar::policy` (fn) — the `graft_ops::mistake_radar::Policy` this section describes: `Policy::default()` with only the set keys overridden; loaded into `AppState.radar.policy` by `event_loop::startup::terminal_and_state` (m2-details-and-diffs/13c).
- `RadarSetting` (enum) — `Default`/`Off`/`Info`/`Warning` for one Mistake Radar kind.
- `Diff` (struct) — `[diff]` section: `context: u8` (default 3), `ignore_whitespace: bool` (default false), `word_tint: bool` (default true); loaded into `DetailState::options`/`word_tint` by `event_loop::startup::terminal_and_state`.
- `Ui` (struct) — `[ui]` section.
- `Ui::theme` (field) — `ui.theme`.
- `Ui::icons` (field) — `ui.icons`.
- `Graph` (struct) — `[graph]` section.
- `Graph::renderer` (field) — `graph.renderer`.
- `Graph::default_branch` (field) — forced default branch full ref name; `None` lets detection decide.
- `Graph::max_lanes` (field) — `graph.max_lanes`: lanes drawn before folding behind `+N`. `None` (unset, the default) follows the terminal's width; a set value below 2 is a parse error (m1-the-graph/44).
- `DEFAULT_MAX_LANES` (const) — 8.
- `ThemeChoice` (enum) — `ui.theme` values.
- `ThemeChoice::overridden` (fn) — the override to hand `ThemeKind::select`.
- `RendererChoice` (enum) — `graph.renderer` values.
- `RendererChoice::overridden` (fn) — the tier the user forced, or `None` for detected.
- `IconSet` (enum) — `ui.icons` values. `IconSet::glyphs() -> graft_ui::graph_screen::Icons` is the one translation into what the widgets take; read once into `AppState::icons` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33).
- `Loaded` (struct) — what `load` found.
- `Loaded::config` (field) — settings in effect: file values over defaults.
- `Loaded::path` (field) — file the values came from, or `None`.
- `Loaded::warnings` (field) — unrecognised keys, in file key order.
- `Keys::remaps` (field, m8-09b) — `Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>`: each `[keys.<ctx>] <verb> = "chord"` (or a list) as `("<ctx>.<verb>", chords)`, for `Keymap::with_overrides`. In **sorted** order (toml without `preserve_order`), so a collision's winner follows that order, not the file's. `serde(skip)`: filled by `load`'s typed parse (`config/keys.rs`).
- `keymap_from` (fn, `pub(crate)`, m8-09b) — `(&Loaded) -> (Keymap, Vec<String>)`: the keymap and every config problem as words, the file's warnings first, then `KeyWarning::words`. Passes `KeySwitches { suspend_on_ctrl_z }` from `[keys]` (m8/09c D2).
- `ConfigWarning` (enum) — a config problem worth telling the user, non-fatal. `NotAKey { path }` (m8-09b): a `[keys.<ctx>]` value that is not a string or a list of strings. `LaneColour { path }` (m8-16): a `[theme.<kind>] lane-N` line. `BadColour { path, value }` (m8-16): a theme value not `"#RRGGBB"`. Unknown theme kind/token → `UnknownKey`.
- `ConfigError` (enum) — a config problem that stops startup.
- `load` (fn) — load config from the default location, or defaults if none.
- `load_from` (fn) — load config from an explicit path (tests, future `--config`).
- `default_path` (fn) — where `load` looks: XDG or `~/.config` path.

## repos
- `RepoStore` (struct) — the open repository, and what has been worked out once.
- `RepoStore::new` (fn) — build a store for a path, with an optional forced branch.
- `RepoStore::path` (fn) — the path Graft was pointed at.
- `RepoStore::shared` (fn) — the shared open repository handle.
- `RepoStore::refs` (fn) — the refs and their tips (`RefSnapshot`), read once, dropped by `refresh`; first paint's branches, seeds, walk tips and `refs::read`'s badges share it.
- `RepoStore::branches` (fn) — the repository's branches, resolved from `refs`.
- `RepoStore::table` (fn) — the repository's one `Arc<CommitTable>`, opened once, usually kept across `refresh` (rebuilt only when the commit-graph's presence changed).
- `RepoStore::opens` (fn) — how many times the repository has been opened from scratch, or its id table rebuilt for a changed commit-graph.
- `RepoStore::common_dir` (fn) — the repository's common directory, shared by every worktree; where `crate::layout`'s split file lives (m2-details-and-diffs/10b).
- `RepoStore::workdir` (fn) — the working tree's root, `None` for a bare repository. Every porcelain path is relative to it, so anything handed to another program is joined onto it first (m3/05 review M2).
- `RepoStore::refresh` (fn) — mark cached refs and conclusions (e.g. default branch) stale; returns `Result<Refreshed, StoreError>`, rebinding the id table to a new `CommitTable` when the commit-graph's presence changed since it was built (m2-details-and-diffs/21, m1-the-graph/54).
- `Refreshed` (struct) — `refresh`'s report: `table_rebound: bool`, true when a new id table replaced the old one.
- `StoreError` (enum) — why the store could not answer (open, refs, branches, id table, commit-graph status).

## cli
- askpass mode — with `GRAFT_ASKPASS_SOCKET` set and exactly one argument (git's prompt), the binary answers git's credential question through `run::askpass::respond` and exits, never loading config and never starting the TUI: 0 answered, 1 the user cancelled (nothing on stdout, so git gives up), 2 anything else, with one `graft: …` line on stderr (m4-remotes/05, wired here in m4-remotes/06).
- `Invocation` (enum) — what the user asked for; `Askpass { socket, prompt }` is the mode above.
- `CliError` (enum) — what the arguments could not be made sense of.
- `USAGE` (const) — what `--help` prints.
- `ASKPASS_SOCKET_VAR` (const) — `GRAFT_ASKPASS_SOCKET`, the env var git's askpass child is given.
- `from_process` (fn) — parse the real process's arguments; askpass mode is decided first.
- `askpass` (fn) — `(Option<OsString>, &[OsString]) -> Option<Invocation>`; the mode's rule on its own, so it is testable without a process.
- `parse` (fn) — parse an arbitrary argument list.

## view
- `picker` (mod, `pub(crate)`) — `picker_view(state) -> Option<PickerView>` (m7-power-tools/10): the picker worded for `graft_ui::picker`; `None` unless shown. Kind from `current`/`is_previous`/`SwitchKind`; right column `tracks …`/`built on …`/`no copy of yours yet`; who = the author's first name; a failed read joined to one line. Drawn by `frame::boxes::draw_picker` in the graph arm after the preview and menu, at `update::picker::picker_box_in`. While shown, `rows::hint_context` answers `HintContext::Picker` (`type to find  ⏎ switch to it  esc close  ⇥ show it on the graph  ↑↓ row`).
Internal split (m1-the-graph/72b, public paths unchanged): `frame` (tier/screen
pick, `view`/`view_with_graphics`/`tier_in_force`/`draws_pixels`), `rows`
(`graph_view` and its assembly), `entries` (building each `GraphEntry`,
`uncommitted_shown` — the uncommitted row takes the pane's top row: pinned, or a one-row pane with the cursor on it, 48), `status` (`status_view`), `detail` (`detail_view`,
m2-details-and-diffs/07), `map` (`map_view`, m2-details-and-diffs/19),
`journal` (`journal_view`, m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06), `stash`
(`stash_view`, m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06), `strip`
(`strip_marks`, m2-details-and-diffs/20), `hit` (`badge_index_at`) and
`name_prompt` (`name_prompt_view`), both m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10 —
plus the pre-existing `strips`,
all re-exported from `mod.rs`. `frame` is itself split into private children (m6-07 review, Major 4; no item's behaviour or public path changed, and `frame::…` paths below still resolve through `frame.rs`'s re-imports): `frame.rs` (the entry points and the screen match), `frame/layout.rs` (graph + detail split, tab layout, `primary_pane_area`, `pixels_above_overlays`), `frame/overlays.rs` (journal, stash, map), `frame/conflicts.rs` (the resolver, its banner and footer), `frame/bars.rs` (`draw_status_or_hints`, `draw_box_bars`/`bar_rows` — the bar under a live box, rulings 07b Q1 — the tab strip, banner and hint row, `footer_notice`, `banner_lines`) and `frame/boxes.rs` (commit editor, Ghost Preview, `.` menu, `draw_modal_boxes` and the ADR-0040 bands). `frame` is `pub(crate)` so the geometry mirror's tests can compare against the rects the drawing path hands out (`frame::detail_rects(state, layout, area)`, the one place the Tab layout's banner rows come off the pane — m5/25).
- `resolver` (private mod) — the conflict resolver's view model, and **the one place** its repo bytes are sanitised (m6-conflicts/04): `resolver_view(state, columns) -> Option<DocView>`, `resolver_layout(state, placed: Placement, columns) -> Option<Layout>` (arrangement from `arrangement(state, placed)`: `placed.arrangement()`, m6-conflicts/14 S5, except a done file is always `Card` — its sides are gone, m6-conflicts/25; the frame's draw calls the same fn; recomputed per frame rather than cached on `ResolverState`, which derives `PartialEq` and is compared whole by the hint sweeps), `resolver_title(path, Identity) -> String` (m6-conflicts/13: `notes.txt · 2 conflicts · 1 open`, or for a card `gone.txt · modified on yours · main, deleted on theirs · feature`; where the file is among the others is the list's to say), `Identity` (`Blocks { open, of }`, `Whole(&str)`), `how_words(how, binary, &SideNames) -> String` (`binary · ` prefix for a binary file), `resolver_split(state, banner_rows) -> (FileListView, Placement, ResolverAreas)` (the one split both the draw and the mouse use — since m6-conflicts/14 the draw takes its placement and doc width from it too, not a second split; it reads `state.size`, which equals the frame area), `file_list_view(state) -> FileListView` (the one place the list's names are sanitised) — the explainer is gone (m6-conflicts/10): `DocView.names` comes from `update::resolver::side_names`, and each region is numbered by `ConflictFile::first_lines` per projection. `whole_file_view(state, open, &WholeFileShape, columns) -> DocView` (m6-conflicts/05) builds a whole-file card's one `RegionView::WholeFile`: `how`/`keys` from `whole_file_keys`, the file's lines sanitised through `whole_file_line`. A card's `RegionView::WholeFile.note` (m6-05 review, Major 4) is an unnumbered line under its text: `binary · <size>` in place of a binary file's bytes, or `… first N lines of <size> · e opens the whole file` when the glimpse was cut. `size_words(bytes) -> String` (`812 B`, `64.0 KB`, `4.2 MB`, rounded). `absent_on(how, Side, &SideNames)` (private, m6-conflicts/14) fills `WholeFile.ours_absent`/`theirs_absent`: `(deleted on <side>)` for the deleted shapes, `(not on <side>)` for the added ones. `drawn_for_tests(state) -> Vec<String>` (`#[cfg(test)]`, `pub(crate)`) — the resolver frame as rows, so `update` tests never name ratatui.
- `view::resolver::finish_row` (fn, `pub(crate)`) / `resolver_area` (fn) — (m6-conflicts/60) `finish_row(&AppState) -> Option<FinishRow>`: the finish row's words (`✓ every file done — c review and finish the merge` / `… c continue the <kind>`, full and short) while the resolver is open with no summary, no problem notice, no status read out, a banner, no `continue_refusal`, not a bisect; set on every `DocView` literal. `resolver_area(&AppState, banner_rows) -> Rect`: the resolver's area, shared by `resolver_split` and the finish row's click test.
- `view::rewrite_editor` (mod, m7-power-tools/08-b) — `editor_area(&AppState) -> Rect` (the frame less the bar row), `rewrite_view(&AppState, ascii) -> Option<RewriteEditorView>` (the one view the draw and `rewrite_mouse`'s hit test share, built from `RewriteEditor::view_facts`: title, rows and notes, the message block, AFTER grouped per branch from the plan's ghosts, its facts — `built on yours: … follows` (dependents only), one row per fork and each branch at the old tip (`fork_rows(plan, true)`, m8/14c: a `would conflict` one led by `!` in `FactTone::Warning`, other stays rows and the old-tip row `FactTone::Muted`, never `left behind:`; a following fork's AFTER note is `follows`), `on origin: …`, the forecast; each an `AfterFact` with its tone — or the cursor row's changes), `rewrite_context(&AppState) -> Option<HintContext>` (`RewriteMessage` with the field open, else `RewriteEditor { narrow: columns < 120, can_start: AFTER checking or ready }`). Drawn by `view::frame::overlays::draw_rewrite`: the editor, then its own bar. `view::rows::rewrite_offered` (private) decides the graph bar's `R rewrite` (ruling 10: HEAD on a branch, graph focus, nothing live, and the row off the default chain — or HEAD is the default branch, or the radar context isn't ready); `back_to_place` leads a `Rewritable` row with `esc back to …` as any row.
- `view::finish` (mod, m6-conflicts/19) — `finish_view(&AppState) -> Option<FinishView>` (the one view the draw, the keys' keep-on-screen and the mouse's hit test share), `summary_area`, `finish_context` (`expandable` = the cursor is on a row git combined on its own; `space` works on a by-you row too, unadvertised), `finish_hints` (test-only since 59: `footer_hints`, or only `esc back` below the floor), `finish_bar(state)` (`committing…` while the commit runs, else empty — the keys are in the summary's frame). Drawn by `view::frame::conflicts::draw_finish`: the banner line, the widget, its own bar.
- `view::resolver` files (m6-conflicts/27, pure moves; paths unchanged): `view/resolver.rs` `resolver_view`, layout, title, `how_words`, file list, regions; child `resolver/whole_file.rs` the whole-file and done cards (`whole_file_view`, `done_card`, `absent_on`, `size_words`); child `resolver/tests.rs` (test-only) the view tests and `drawn_for_tests`, re-exported as `view::resolver::drawn_for_tests`.
- `hit` (private mod) — `badge_index_at(state, column, row, now_seconds) -> Option<usize>`: which ref badge, if any, is drawn at a screen cell (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10). It builds the frame's `GraphView` — **the frame's own, from `view::rows::graph_view`**, since m5/33 fixed the seam where `view::frame` overrode the icons and the lane cap *after* building it and the hit-test measured a view nothing drew (review M1) — and asks `graft_ui::graph_screen::{row_at, badge_spans}` — **the same packing the renderer draws from**, not a copy of it. The obvious alternative, mirroring the packing in `update::geometry` the way that file mirrors `detail_pane`'s split, was ruled out for this ticket: two implementations of where a badge is drawn is how a badge becomes grabbable a column away from where it looks. `update` reaches it through `crate::view`, which is established (`geometry::uncommitted_shown`, `mouse::strip_marks`) and clears the purity guard, which forbids only `ratatui` itself inside `update/`.
- `name_prompt` (private mod) — `name_prompt_view(state) -> Option<NamePromptView<'static>>` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10): the title says what is being named and where (`New branch at a1b2c3d`, `New tag at …`, `Branch from stash@{0}`), because a modal box covers the row the cursor was on and nothing else on screen still says which commit this is. Only a plain branch gets the checkout toggle — `git stash branch` checks out the branch it makes and a tag is not something to be on, so those two draw no option rather than one that would not be obeyed. `view::frame::draw_name_prompt` draws it in all five layouts, immediately before the credential prompt.
- `preview` (private mod) — drawing one open Ghost Preview (m5/08): `preview_entries(state, preview, now) -> Vec<GraphEntry>`, which builds the whole labelled slice first and only then windows it from `picture_start` (`pub(crate)`, m7/37: `(at_start, label_at, at_selected, visible) -> PictureStart { start, label_pinned }`, drawn-entry positions; a topmost label above the user's top row starts the picture when the target fits under it, else the target is the last row and the label is pinned over the top row) rather than from `rows[0]` (windowing first and inserting the label after hands the screen one row more than it has, and the row it loses is the bottom one — ticket 17 F1 again) — `PreviewState::view_start`, slid down only as far as it must to keep `selected_row` inside the rows the box left the graph (m5/17 F1). The highlighted row is `selected_row`, the commit the key was pressed on, and `graph_view` passes `reveal: None`: a modal picture has no hovered row. While a preview is open `view::rows::graph_view` draws the preview's slice instead of the live window and sets `HintContext::Preview`, so the picture the box describes and the rows on screen are the same picture. A ghost row is `RowPaint::Ghost` with `ghost_lanes` = the lanes the ghost node itself touches (its own, those closing into it, every edge leaving it) — a real lane merely crossing the row keeps its solid glyph — and draws `—` for both sha and age, because the commit does not exist yet. `compare_marked`/`marker` are never set on a preview row: the compare pin and the Mistake Radar are about history as it is. `view::frame::draw_preview` docks `PreviewBoxWidget` at the bottom of the graph area, over rows `AppState::graph_rows` already gave up. Every overlay `view::frame` draws goes through `clear_and_tint` (private): the rectangle is **cleared** and then tinted `SurfaceRaised`, because `set_style` repaints colour and leaves symbols, so a tint alone leaves whatever the widget declines to write showing through wearing the overlay's background — which is what the widgets' degraded paths do (`PreviewBoxWidget` below 40x12 and `CredentialPrompt` below 7 rows write one warning line; `Confirm` below 2 rows writes nothing). Pinned by `view::frame::tests::the_confirm_box_never_shows_what_was_under_it` and `…::the_credential_prompt_never_shows_what_was_under_it`. `view::frame::draw_context_menu` clears a wider region than it tints — the whole **band** the `.` popup stands on, the popup's rows from its left edge out to the end of the pane (`primary_pane_area`, `overlay_band`, both private to `view::frame`). The popup is only as wide as its longest label, so clearing its own rectangle is not enough: the graph rows underneath keep drawing their date and sha columns beside each verb, which reads as if the verb were bound to that sha. Pinned by `it::preview::the_context_menu_is_opaque_on_every_row_it_draws` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/12, found by the user by eye in the capture).
- `strip_marks(state) -> graft_ui::overview_strip::StripMarks` — the overview strip's marks over the rows `state.history` holds now: `Merge` on a row with 2+ parents, `Radar(sev)` from `view::radar::marker_for` (already gated on the radar context being `Ready`), `Head` on `RefBadges::head()`'s row, `SearchHit` for each search hit landing on a resident row (hits are collected into a `HashSet` once per frame, not scanned per row — M2 review M10). `rows_total` is `History::rows`, `viewport` is `top..top+graph_rows` (`view/strip.rs`: the history rows drawn, under the pinned uncommitted row when there is one, 48). Empty when there is no history yet. Wired into `frame` (m2-details-and-diffs/20): when `state.strip_shown` and not the pixel tier, `view::frame::render_graph_screen` renders `GraphScreen` into a scratch buffer and blits everything but its rightmost column into the frame, then draws `OverviewStrip` into that freed column — columns stay put because `graph_screen::layout::Columns::compute` lays sha/when/author out from `area.right()`, so narrowing the real `Rect` would shift them; the pixel tier draws no strip. `update::strip_bounds(state)` gives that column's `Bounds`; a left click there calls `graft_ui::overview_strip::row_for_cell` and `select_row` instead of the ordinary graph click, and the wheel is untouched (falls through to `scroll()` as before).
- `journal_view(state, now_seconds) -> graft_ui::journal_screen::JournalView` — J's rows, newest first (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06), from `state.journal.list` since m7/24-b: `●` rows as below; `$` rows the list's words with git's subject; `✗` rows `<sha7> "<subject>" · was <b>'s tip · no branch holds it` / `· <b> is gone` / `· was HEAD`, `+N under it`; lost edits `your edit to <p> · replaced by this undo · kept in its backup`. `enter_words` from the row alone (`⏎ undo this`, `⏎ redo this`, `⏎ revert to before this`, `⏎ put <b> back to it`, `⏎ put <b> back to before this`, `⏎ make <b> again`, `⏎ switch back to <b>`, `⏎ put your edit back`, or none); `title_suffix` from the lost scan; `box_open` while a preview is up; `status_row` left `None` (the frame fills it). An `Aborted` entry whose reason is `aborted` reads `aborted by you · nothing changed` (M6/68). The `← u` / `← U` / `undone` marks come from `graft_ops::journal::{next_undo, next_redo, in_force_all}`, never from a rule restated here, so the browser and the key cannot disagree. A row's summary names what the entry moved (`1 ref · the index · backup saved`), leads with `failed: <git's first line>` for an `Aborted` entry and `interrupted` for one a previous run left `Pending`; the whole reason and the Command Log are under `Space`. A row in `AppState::live_entries` outranks all of them: it reads `in progress — c continue  a abort`, carries no mark (never `interrupted`, never `← u`) and has no failure line, because nothing has failed and there is no outcome to take back yet (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/14).
- `view::frame::overlays::draw_journal` (private, m7/24-b) — J over the graph area; fills `status_row` from `journal_status_row` (the bottom row's ladder: `view::status::bottom_row_problem`, else `bars::footer_notice`, fitted by `graph_screen::fit_status_line` to J's width). The `WithoutBar` wrapper (24-a2) is gone: `JournalView::box_open` blanks the bar.
- `stash_view(state, now_seconds) -> graft_ui::stash_screen::StashView` — the stash overlay's rows, newest first (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/06): `stash@{n}`, the message's first line in quotes, the age from the graph's own `relative_date`, and the file count. The save prompt's `refusal` is `update::stash::save_refusal`'s answer and the notice line is `AppState::status`'s, so the overlay and the keys cannot disagree.
- `credential_view(state) -> Option<graft_ui::credential_prompt::CredentialView<'static>>` — the credential prompt as the widget wants it (m4-remotes/06): git's own question, the field masked or in the open, and a footer naming what `Esc` cancels: `⏎ send  ⌃U clear  esc cancel <the running write's description>` (`push main → origin/main`) when that fits the box whole (`credential_area(frame).width - 4`), else `… esc cancel the <verb>` (m6-conflicts/78, Decided 2: where F9's information went once 69 gave the row under the box to its keys). `counter: None` — how long a password is, is part of what it hides. Drawn after everything else, the confirm box included, at every site (`view::frame::draw_credential`).
- `radar` (`pub(crate)` mod) — turns `AppState`'s facts into `graft-ops::mistake_radar::Finding`s and those into a marker/detail lines (m2-details-and-diffs/13b). `repo_facts(state, ctx, remotes: &[&str])` (fn) — pure fact adapter from `AppState`/`RadarContext`; `remotes` comes from `remote_names(state)`, the remote facts' names (m4-remotes/05) (`branch_facts`/`head_facts` moved to `update::radar`). `findings_for(state, id, parents, subject) -> Vec<Finding>` — the evil-merge verdict ignores the context (its probe reads none, m7-power-tools/33c); everything else is empty until `state.radar.context` is `Ready`; the ref-derived part is a lookup in `RadarState::ref_findings`/`force_push_findings`, never a ref walk (M2 review C1). `marker_for(...) -> Option<graft_ui::graph_screen::MarkerView>` — highest-severity finding. `radar_lines_for(...) -> Vec<graft_ui::detail::RadarLine>` — first `NotChecked(why)` while `state.radar.context` is `Failed(why)` (m7-power-tools/33), then one line per finding plus, on a merge with the kind on, the evil-merge line that `DetailState::evil` says (`EvilChecking`/`EvilClean`/`EvilResolved`/`EvilNotCheckable`, nothing extra when the verdict is already a finding, `EvilUnchecked` when unprobed; **no evil-merge line at all on a stash entry's own commit**, `RefBadges::is_stash`, m7-power-tools/16); called by `view::detail::commit_header` (m2-details-and-diffs/13c). A branch tip whose force-push verdict is `Rewritten` also yields a `ForcePushedRemote` finding, and one whose verdict is `NotCheckable` yields `RadarLine::ForcePushNotCheckable` (m2-details-and-diffs/15). A commit in `state.radar.duplicate_findings` also yields its `DuplicateCommit` finding (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `graph_columns(state, screen) -> u16` (`pub(crate)`, `view::frame`) — the columns the graph screen is drawn into this frame: the whole width with no detail pane, what `graft_ui::detail_pane::split_area` leaves it when there is one — ADR-0039's 2026-09-21 amendment, third instance (m5/21).
- `screen_area(state) -> Rect` (`pub(crate)`, `view::frame`) — the whole screen from `AppState::size`: the one answer off the drawing path, where a mouse event and `event_loop::graphics` have no `Frame` to ask (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33).
- `max_lanes(state) -> usize` (`pub(crate)`, `view::rows`) — the lane cap this frame folds at: `graph.max_lanes` if set, else the width breakpoint, against `graph_columns(state, screen_area(state))`. **The only copy**; `view::rows::graph_view` and `event_loop::graphics` both call it, so the text tiers, the pixel strips and the hit-test fold the same graph. There were three copies of this arithmetic until m5/33 and two of them read the session, which the hit-test cannot see.
- `divider_shown(state) -> bool` (`pub(crate)`, `view::rows`) — is there a divider for `<>` to move: the pane is open **and** the layout is a split, not the sub-100-column tab strip. `GraphView::divider_shown` (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33, review M6's width sweep: the footer offered `<>` at 40 and 80 columns, where it moves nothing).
- `view` (fn) — draw the current state to a frame: the graph screen, or the status screen.
- `view_with_graphics` (fn) — `view`, plus the gfx status in the header at the pixel tier; returns the graph rect the images go in, `None` when no pixels this frame. When `state.detail.shown`, splits the frame with `graft_ui::detail_pane::DetailLayout` and draws `DetailPane` beside/below/instead of the graph screen (`Tab` draws one side full width behind a `[Graph] Detail` strip; the graph's pixel rect is `None` while it is off screen). When `state.map.shown`, the `Screen::Graph` arm renders `graft_ui::map_screen::MapScreen` instead — no graph rows, no detail split, no pixels (m2-details-and-diffs/19); with `map.shown` false the arm is unchanged from before that ticket. **The returned rect is cut back to the rows no overlay stands on** (`frame::pixels_above_overlays`, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/34): the images are written *after* the whole frame by `event_loop::running::draw`, so the terminal composites them over every cell already drawn — a Ghost Preview box, `.` popup, confirm box or prompt standing on the graph was drawn *under* the lanes it stood on, however correct its own rectangle. That is why the user saw a preview box with no left border and a lane glyph on the `u` of `undo:` while every buffer sweep and every `.txt` capture stayed green: the character grid was right, and the two layers only meet on his screen. Each `draw_*` overlay now returns the rectangle it cleared, and the lanes keep the rows above the topmost one that overlaps them.
- `map_view(state, now_seconds)` (fn) — build a `graft_ui::map_screen::MapView` from `state.map`'s `Loading<Arc<MapData>>`: `Ready` fills the rows (current branch first for the header title, else the default branch), `Pending`/`Idle`/`Failed` return an empty view with the matching `MapStatus`. `active` marks a row whose tip lands in the newest bucket, is not current and has not merged into the default branch's row. `ascii` is left `false`; `view::frame` sets it from the tier in force, mirroring `graph_view`.
- `detail_view` (fn) — assemble a `graft_ui::detail::DetailView` from `AppState`: header from the selected row's hydrated commit (author/committer/parents in full; co-authors parsed from `Co-authored-by:` message trailers), or from `AppState::working_tree`'s counts for the uncommitted row, its `diff vs HEAD <sha7>` naming HEAD's own commit from the refs (`RefSnapshot::head`), never the graph's top row; refs unread → `diff vs HEAD`, no id (m6-conflicts/85 m-b); body is the commit message when the files cursor is on `✉ message`, else the diff under it, tracking `DetailState::files`/`diff`'s loading state. `explain` comes straight from `AppState::detail::explain`.
- `explain` (`pub(crate)` mod) — the Explain panel's view model (m2-details-and-diffs/17). `build_explain_view(e: &Explanation) -> ExplainView` — title/paragraphs sanitised, sketch from `build_sketch`; `severity` is a placeholder (`Info`) the caller (`update::detail::open_explain`) overwrites from the `Finding`, since `Explanation` itself carries none. `build_sketch(evidence, short) -> Vec<SanitizedText>` — one sanitised line per evidence entry, alternating `──╮`/`──╯` connectors between them.
- `tier_in_force` (fn) — the graph's tier from `Session` + `AppState`: F3's override, else the session's (m1-the-graph/33).
- `draws_pixels` (fn) — graph screen, pixel tier (override else session), Kitty or iTerm2 protocol — or no acked protocol at all (forced `pixel` attempts Kitty, m1-the-graph/24d).
- `graph_view(state, now_seconds)` (fn) — build a `GraphView` from the history window, cursor and status; the selection's `Ancestry` over the window lights lanes, fades rows in Focus mode, and weighs its columns as a fold priority — not a veto (ADR-0025). `now_seconds` (m1-the-graph/53) is the instant every row's date is fitted against; callers pass `state.harness.clock.now_seconds()`. Reads `super::status::bottom_row_problem` (not `problem`) for `GraphView::problem`, so the footer keeps a sticky failure's keys apart from its reason. **While a modal is open** (`status::modal_owns_bottom_row`) the problem is only the modal's own refusal and `search`, `compare_footer` and `stopped` are `None` (`rows::modal_blanked`, m6-conflicts/69 Decided 1); `reveal` is left alone — under a box `frame::bars::draw_box_bars` writes the bar over the footer, and under the preview `rows_for_frame` reveals nothing, so the footer subject never shows under a modal while the hovered row keeps its ref reveal. **The one place a drawn `GraphView` is built** (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/33, review M1): everything that decides where a cell lands is settled here — `icons`, `compaction.max_lanes` (`view::max_lanes`), `divider_shown` and the harness `banner` — because `view::hit::badge_index_at` calls it to hit-test a drag. `view::frame` adds only the fields that need the `Session` and move nothing: `ascii`, `notice`, `pixel`, `graphics`. It used to override the icons and the lane cap here, so with Nerd icons or past eight lanes a drag landed on the wrong badge. A row in `AppState::stash_only` is marked by the private `mark_stash_only` (m7-power-tools/16): node `NodeKind::StashOnly` (`□`), paint `Dimmed` unless highlighted or a drag's, badge `BadgeKind::OnlyInStash(n)` **last** (so `update::geometry::badge_at`'s ref index never reaches it). The pixel tier dims the same rows (`view::strips::Painter::row` → `Emphasis::Dimmed`; no new ring).
- `footer_context(state, rows, banner)` (fn, `view::rows`) — what the footer hints for this frame: a **focused** banner owns the keyboard, so its own list wins over whatever the cursor sits on; an unfocused one claims nothing and `hint_context` stands — wrapped by `live_wrapped` in `HintContext::OperationLive { row, c, tab_to_banner, space_stages }` (`space_stages` from `keys::edit_stop::space_stages_here`, 07d) **whenever an operation is live** (`live_operation`; banner or none, so a bisect too) and the row is a graph row's bar (`WorkingTreeRow`, `BranchRow`, `PlainRow`, `Marked`, `PaneFocused`), `c` from `live_c` and `tab_to_banner` from `banner::one_tab_to_banner` (m6-conflicts/52, decision B). `live_c(state) -> Option<LiveC>` (private) is the one mapping from `commit_editor::pane_c_acts` to the bar's `c`; `hint_context` puts it on `ChangesPane { live }` too. `banner_context(banner) -> HintContext::Banner { skippable, conflicted }` is the one place that mapping lives, because `view::frame::draw_tab_banner` needs the same context outside the graph screen and the two must not drift.
- `draw_tab_banner(frame, state, area, theme) -> Rect` (fn, private, `view::frame`) — the operation banner in the sub-100-column tab layout: its own row **above** the `Graph [Detail]` strip, and its key hints on the bottom row while it has focus; returns what is left of `area` for the strip and the pane. Below 100 columns the shown pane takes the whole screen and the graph screen — which carries the banner everywhere else — is not drawn, so a modal banner was live and invisible at 80 columns with `c`/`s`/`a` unreachable (m5/16, review C1). A modal overlay must be visible wherever it has focus, so it is drawn outside the pane rather than travelling with one. Costs **one** row of pane content while an operation is live and **two** while its banner has the keyboard; none otherwise.
- `resolver_context(state)` (fn, `view::rows`) — the resolver's `HintContext`, the **one** source for both the classifier and `view::frame::draw_resolver_footer` (which had a hard-coded copy drawing neither `B` nor `d`; m6-05 review, Major 1): a document → `Resolver { base: true, restart: true, whole_file: false, roles }`, a whole-file card → `Resolver { whole_file: true, .. }`; `roles` from `side_names`; `done` while a done file is open (m6-conflicts/25).
- `update::detail::space_folds(detail) -> bool` (fn, pub(crate), m7-power-tools/20h) — the files list has focus and its cursor is on a directory row: what `hint_context` sets `PaneFocused::folds` from, next to `enter_opens`.
- `hint_context(state, rows)` (fn, `view::rows`) — the footer's `HintContext` for this frame (`hint-bar-redesign`): `on_changes_pane` **and the pane has the keyboard** → `ChangesPane { unmerged: the cursor's own file is unmerged }` (with focus back on the graph its write keys are all refused, so the row's own bar is drawn instead — m5/31) before `on_working_tree` → `WorkingTreeRow` (the pane opens on the uncommitted row and the cursor stays there, so both hold and the pane's arm was unreachable — m5-03 review Critical 1); else the cursor row's (`RowPaint::Highlighted`) own `BadgeKind::Branch` → `BranchRow { current }`, no branch badge → `PlainRow`, wrapped in `Marked` when that row carries a `GraphEntry::marker`; then, over whichever row came out, wrapped in `PaneFocused { row, opens: detail::enter_opens(..) }` unless `graph_has_focus()` — the filter that stops a focused pane advertising the four graph keys and `⏎` (m5/31). **An overlay comes before all of it** (m5/42): `overlay_context(state)` reads `AppState::graph_overlay`, and while the Map view, the journal browser or the stash list stands where the graph stands the graph's own keys reach nothing — so with the graph focused the bar is that overlay's own list (`Map`/`Journal`/`Stash`), and with the detail pane focused it is `PaneOverOverlay { opens }`, the pane's `⏎`/`j/k` plus the two verbs dispatched above the panes (`u undo`, `/ search`). Before that the footer under an overlay was the graph's row context — nine verbs, every one of them dead. The commit editor is the one overlay `overlay_context` answers `None` for: it claims every key but `Ctrl+C` before the panes and draws no hint bar to be wrong on. `Banner`/`Preview` are other M5 groups' to produce. The divider hint is no longer part of this: `GraphView::divider_shown` carries it. Pinned from a real repository through a drawn frame by `tests/it/hint_bar.rs`, one test per reachable context.
- `status_view` (fn) — build a `StatusView` from session and app state.
- `done_line(what, also, keys) -> String` (fn, `view::status`, `pub(crate)`) — the one rendering of a `Status::Done`: `<what> ✓`, then ` · <also>`, then ` · <keys>`, each only when present (`also` since m6-conflicts/84). The graph footer (`frame::bars::footer_notice`), the stash overlay and the resolver's notice row (`every_file_done_words`) all call it (ticket 41).
- `modal_owns_bottom_row` — moved to `AppState::modal_owns_bottom_row` (m6-conflicts/78). Every bottom-row rung asks it: `graph_view`/`opening_view`, `frame::bars::footer_notice` (`None` then), `draw_status_or_hints`, and `rows::modal_blanked` (the search rung and, since 78, the commit-graph offer). Not `AppState::modal_owns_keys`: the resolver, finish screen and focused banner draw their own statuses on purpose.
- `modal_problem(state) -> Option<ProblemLine>` (fn, `view::status`, `pub(crate)`) — the bottom row's problem while a modal is open: only the name prompt's `name_prompt::refusal_words`, with no keys.
- `bottom_row_problem(state) -> Option<ProblemLine>` (fn, `view::status`, `pub(crate)`) — `modal_problem` while a modal owns the row, else `graph_problem(state.shown_status())`; what the graph footer and every `draw_status_or_hints` bar read.
- `graph_problem(status) -> Option<graft_ui::graph_screen::ProblemLine>` (fn, `view::status`) — the graph footer's own reading of `Status`: reason and keys kept apart (unlike `problem`, which joins them for the status screen/stash overlay) so `graph_screen::hints::fit_status_line` can cut the reason and keep the keys whole (`hint-bar-redesign`, ADR-0037 §4). A failure that carries no keys of its own gets `esc dismiss` (`view::status::DISMISS`) — `Esc` really does clear a `Failed`/`Sticky` status, and a keyless footer is the one outcome ADR-0037 §4 rules out (m5-03 review Major 2; pinned by `tests/it/hint_bar.rs::a_failure_still_names_the_key_that_clears_it`).
- `update::tests::keymap_edge` (`#[cfg(test)]`, m8/09d Major 3) — the behavioural keymap sweep: every row `Keymap::reachable(ctx)` gives, in every context with a fixture (overlays given three rows, panes mid-list), pressed by its default chord; it acts (≠ `F20`) or is listed — `sweep_swallowed::SWALLOWED_BY_DESIGN`, `sweep_tables::{NO_EFFECT_IN_FIXTURE, SHADOWED, ONE_CHORD_IDLE, DEFECTS}`, `sweep::NOT_YET_ANSWERED` — each with its `file:line`; a listed row that acts fails, a shadow is checked by the shadowing row's swept chord, and `ACTING_AT_LANDING` (396 on 2026-10-07) may only grow. The start screen's twin is `empty_state::sweep_tests` (list, init box in `Confirm`, open prompt).
- `bound_actions() -> &'static [ActionId]` (fn, `#[cfg(test)]`, `update::keys`, was `bound_keys()`; over `ActionId` since m8/10) — every action the graph screen and its panes actually answer, each annotated in place with the dispatcher that answers it. `update::tests::NEVER_HINTED: &[(ActionId, &str)]` names the bound actions no bar draws, each with why. `crate::spelled::Spelled` (`#[cfg(test)]`, m8/10) — `hint.key()`: a hint's key as the default keymap spells it, for test literals. Walked both ways by `update::tests`: `every_key_the_bar_draws_is_answered` (every context × `Divider` × `SWEPT_WIDTHS`, key *and* verb, no exemptions) and `every_planned_hint_is_still_unbound` (a `HintState::Planned` hint whose key gets bound fails the build until the hint is flipped to `Live`). `m merge` and `r rebase` joined it at the m5/24 seam close, when the hint tables flipped them from `Planned` to `Live` — group 08 had bound both in `preview::history_key` since m5/08. `b branch` and `t tag` joined it at m5-history-and-ghost-preview/10 on the graph and in the stash overlay, with `release drop on a row` and `esc cancel` for `HintContext::Dragging` — `⏎` is not bound during a drag, so the hint names the button, not a key that would fail `every_key_the_bar_draws_is_answered`. The banner's own `c`/`s`/`a`/`M` are in it (m5/16; group 09 bound them in `banner_key` and flipped neither table, so both walks agreed with each other and passed while the footer taught `<> resize  . more` under a modal banner). Hand-written, so it is a promise the tests hold you to, not a derivation — and **not the anchor**: `update::banner::tests::every_hint_the_focused_banner_draws_is_answered_by_banner_key` presses every hint the focused banner draws and asserts what it did, so the bar is checked against the dispatcher rather than against another table.
- `commit_editor_view` (fn) — build a `graft_ui::commit_editor::CommitEditorView` from `AppState`, including `status` (the same `Status` reading the graph footer's `problem` uses, so a hook's refusal is readable without closing the editor — m3/05 review M1): title (`COMMIT ── ⎇ <branch> ── N files staged`, or `AMEND <short> ── …`), the field halves, the option ticks, and `refusal` — the preview of why `[ Commit ]` is dimmed (nothing staged, empty subject, or a merge/rebase/cherry-pick in progress naming the shell command). The refusal is a preview only; `Operation::check` is the decision (m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05).

## event_loop
Internal split (m1-the-graph/71, public paths unchanged): `startup` (terminal
setup, `run`), `headless` (no-tty path, `run_headless`), `running` (the frame
loop, `pump`/`pump_with`), plus the pre-existing `graphics`, `redetect`,
`row_clear` — all private, re-exported from `mod.rs` where still public.
- `input` (mod) — input reader thread and event translation.
- `signals` (mod) — signal watcher thread.
- `Exit` (enum) — why the loop stopped: `Quit`, `HandedBack(KeyCode)` (a `Harness::hand_back` key), `Signal(i32)`, `Fatal(String)`, `StoppedHalfWay { job, what, after: Duration }` (m8/13b review fix; 13d: `wind_down` hands it to `Pool::stop` as the `Boxed` write, which prints its `update::quit::exit_line` once the pool is down and its answer known; `main` exits 0).
- `waiting_line(what)` (fn, `event_loop::exit_wait`, re-exported) — `<bin>: waiting for <op> to finish (up to 60 s) · send the signal again to stop it half-way`; `<bin>` is `CARGO_PKG_NAME`, `<op>` sanitised. (13b's `stopped_line` is gone: every stopped write's line is `update::quit::exit_line`, 13d.)
- `exit_wait::Pool` (struct, private) — the pool and both watchers, handed out of `run_repo` so `wind_down` stops them **after** `session.restore()` (13b review fix: no wait longer than `SHUTDOWN_GRACE` before the terminal is given back). `stop(signalled, boxed: Option<&Boxed>)` / `stop_to(stderr, …)`: signalled → one waiting line per running write (write errors ignored: after a hang-up nobody reads stderr), `shutdown(SHUTDOWN_GRACE, EXIT_WRITE_WAIT, &watch.again())`, then `boxed`'s `exit_line` (always; its `at` from `Shutdown::forced`, `None` if absent; a failed write ignored, as 13b's `main` did) and one `exit_line` per other forced write. Holds `undo: String`, the process keymap's spelling of `app.undo` (ADR-0055), spelled in `run_repo`. `Boxed<'a> { job, what, after }` (struct, `pub(super)`): the stop box's write, from `Exit::StoppedHalfWay`.
- `running::quit_clock::QuitClock` (struct, private) — the quit wait's count: `due(state, now)` starts at first sight of `quit::offer_pending`, gives `since + STOP_OFFER_AFTER`, restarts for another job, drops with the wait; `after(now)` for the exit line. The turn adds `due` to the deadlines it waits on and calls `quit::waited_long` when it passes.
- `Wake` (enum) — something that wakes the loop.
- `Wakes` (trait) — a seam so `pump` can be driven by a scripted list in tests.
- `Wake::Fence` (variant) — the terminal answered a frame's fence (`CSI 0 n`), sent by the mux's fence listener (`startup::answer_fences`) only when `answers_dsr` (m8 G-pace).
- `Waited` (enum) — `Woke(Wake)`, `TimedOut`, `Ended`: what `next_wake_by` got.
- `Wakes::now` (fn, default `Instant::now()`, G-settle) — the loop's one clock (pacer, batch budget, rest); a test source hands out wakes on a virtual timeline (`event_loop/tests/settle.rs` `Timed`).
- `rest::Rest` / `rest::Mover` / `BURST_GAP` (150 ms) / `QUIET_MIN` (50 ms) (`pub(crate)`, G-settle) — when the cursor has rested: `in_burst(now)`, `moved(now, Mover)`, `rest_first(&Wake)` (an input that is not the burst's key: `Rested` is applied before it), `deadline()` (last motion + clamp(2 × widest of the last 4 gaps, 50, 150 ms)), `rested()`. The loop waits on the earlier of `Pacer::deadline` and `Rest::deadline` while `follow_behind`.
- `Wakes::next_wake_by` (fn) — required: block until a wake or `deadline`. The loop waits here while a frame is held behind an unanswered fence or the 16 ms cap. `ChannelWakes` uses `recv_timeout` on its wake channel.
- `pace::Pacer` (`pub(crate)`, m8 G-pace, ADR-0054) — when the next frame may be written: one fence unanswered at most; 500 ms timeout (`FENCE_TIMEOUT`) draws anyway; 3 timeouts with no answer between them stop fencing for the run (any answer, however late, ends the run of timeouts — review m8-02 Major 1), then one frame per 16 ms (`FRAME_CAP`), as for a terminal that never answered DSR. `new`, `fenced`, `may_draw`, `drew`, `answered` (→ how long the fence waited), `deadline`, `owed`, `forget` (after a handover).
- `graphics::Graphics` strip cache (m8 04): `STRIP_BUDGET` (96 MiB across the three layers) with `capacity_for(STRIP_ROWS, graph height, BUFFER_ROWS)` as the per-frame floor (`keep_at_least`); `layers` fetches a strip's backdrop/emphasis (or iTerm2 band) with their `UploadKey`s. Tests: `with_strip_budget`, `strip_budget_report`, `strip_stats`. `STRIPS_KEPT` now bounds only the terminal's image counts.
- `running::Screen` (`pub(super)`) — `{ terminal, graphics }`: where a frame goes, text and images, one synchronized update. `pump_with(screen, wakes, jobs, session, state, outside, tracer)` takes it (review m8-01 Major 2: replaces the `too_many_arguments` allows). `startup::run` split into `listen` (forwarder, trace, fence listener), `outside` and `wind_down`.
- `running` split (review m8-02 Major 3; private, paths kept by re-export): `running.rs` the entry points (`pump`, `pump_with`, `pump_keeping`, `prefetch`, the free at exit); `running/turn.rs` the paced turn (`frames`, `Run`: `turn` → `idle`/`paced`, `take` → `fence_answered`/`terminal_changed` (flushes `Flush::Theme` only when the theme itself changed — a new hint under his palette choice or a forced `ui.theme` owes a frame, not a re-raster; m8/11)/`input`, `apply`, `settle_fences`, `draw`); `running/carry.rs` what the loop carries out itself (`carry_out`, `Carried`, `hands_over`, the six handovers, OSC 52); `running/frame.rs` one frame's bytes (`draw_traced`, `draw_frame`). Loop-level pace tests on a virtual timeline: `event_loop/tests/pace.rs` `Line`.
- Paced turn (`running::turn::Run`): wakes arriving while a frame is held are applied, not drawn; non-joining ones (clicks, resize…) wait until the held frame is out. Before a handover the loop settles owed fences so `$EDITOR` never reads `CSI 0 n`; after it, `forget` and draw at once. Each frame: `SYNC_BEGIN` · text · images · `SYNC_END` · fence (`running::frame::draw_frame`).
- `Wakes::waiting` (fn) — is a wake already here, never blocking; the loop asks before each idle-prefetched strip (m1-the-graph/30h). `ChannelWakes` keeps a wake it saw for `next_wake` and `unhandled_input`.
- `Wakes::joining_waiting` (fn) — required, never blocks. Is a wake queued, and does it `joins_frame`? `pump` then applies it in the same turn and draws once: a turn's wakes share one frame, up to 8 ms of updates; a handover command or a quit ends the turn (m7-power-tools/43). `ChannelWakes` peeks its `seen` wake.
- `joins_frame` (fn) — `(&Wake) -> bool`: job events, keys (press/repeat), wheel scrolls. Not clicks, drags, hover, paste, resize, focus, signals or terminal wakes: those start their own turn.
- `is_job_event` (fn) — `(&Wake) -> bool`: `Started`/`Produced`/`Progress`, or `Core::JobFinished/JobFailed/JobCancelled`; keys, mouse, resize, signals and terminal wakes are not.
- `Wake::label` (fn) — `&'static str`: the variant and the kind inside it (`Produced::Refs`, `Progress::History::Window`), never the payload; for the `frames` startup trace (m7-power-tools/12).
- `Wakes::mark` (fn) — `(&mut self, Phase)`, empty default: `pump` calls it as each phase of a frame ends. A timing seam for the `frames` perf harness (m7-power-tools/04); `ChannelWakes` keeps the empty default, so the product pays nothing.
- `Phase` (enum) — `Drained`, `Updated`, `CarriedOut`, `Drawn`: where the frame for one wake stands, in order.
- Frame trace (tests, m7-power-tools/04): `tests/it/frame_trace.rs` holds `Gate` (the `frames` verdict: asserts p99 ≤ 16 ms, prints p50/p99/max and the keys over), `FrameTrace` (`GRAFT_FRAME_TRACE=<file>`: one line per key of its phases; off when unset) and `WriteClock`, and `KeyFeed` / `SentLog` / `Sent` (m7-power-tools/04b): the harness's input thread, sleeping the script's pauses and sending each key into the loop's `Wake` channel as `event_loop/input.rs` `spawn_reader` does; a key's latency runs from its send (`a_key_sent_after_idle_is_received_within_1ms_of_its_send`). `frames` shares one `Wake` channel between the jobs and the keys (`budget_run::open_into`, `pump_until_from`) and reads `GRAFT_FRAME_CONTROL` (`a` no idle pause, `b` a core held busy, `c` no idle prefetch) and has `the_machine_alone_after_idle_and_warm`. Driven by `scripts/m7-perf-key.sh`, which exits 1 when any run failed or any `spread` line is not `ok` (`FAIL(<run> failed)`, `MISSING(k of 3)`, `OVER`; checked on canned outputs by `scripts/m7-perf-key-selftest.sh`, 12d). `frames` is split (12d): `frames.rs` (the scroll tests, `script`, `check_scale`, `check_idle_gaps`), `frames/run.rs` (`scroll`, `ready_to_scroll`, `Run`), `paced.rs` (`Paced`, the loop's stamped wakes; `Watching`), `readings.rs` (latencies, trace, startup lines, `waited`, and `unasked_wakes`/`idle_gaps` — m7/12e `idle_gap_wakes`: wakes nothing asked for in each idle pause after `LaidOut`, asserted 0), `sink.rs` (the in-memory terminal), `controls.rs` (`GRAFT_FRAME_CONTROL`, control D), `verdict.rs` (`judge`, `print_details`). `frames::after_idle::g_after_idle` (m7/12e): 100k cg, 50k refs, Unicode — laid out to the end (`LaidOut`) and startup settled, 2 s idle, `G`; latency from `G`'s send to the frame showing the last row, asserted ≤ 16 ms in release, `idle_gap_wakes=0` asserted; `GRAFT_FRAME_NO_IDLE_ADVANCE` (the "before" build of `scripts/m7-12-final-perf.sh`, `scripts/m7-12-no-idle-advance.patch`) records without asserting. `frames::journal_find` (m7-power-tools/30p-b): `j_with_a_broad_find_holds_the_frame_budget`, J at its caps (`with_reflog_of(10k cg, RECORDS_CAP, LOST_CAP)`: 2 425 rows, 425 `✗`) at 187×50 Unicode, `/step⏎` (2 000 hits) then `n`/`N`/`j`×40; two pumps (`J`, then `/`…) with the stage 2–3 wait between, unmeasured; premises fail the test; `frames: tier=Unicode refs=<rows> pane=JournalFind … hits= lost_rows=`. Driven alone by `scripts/m7-30p-perf.sh`.
- `drain_sink` (tests, m7-power-tools/42; fast tier, root of `it`): `DrainSink`, a bounded pipe drained by a thread at a fixed rate, blocking the writer when full; keeps counts and offsets (`written`, `drained_at`, `quiet_for`) and `KittyTransmit`s found in the stream (`kitty_transmits`), never the bytes. **m8 G-pace:** `answering_fences(answer)` — the drain thread calls `answer` once per `CSI 5 n` when it has drained past it (split-safe `FenceScan`); `fences_written`. Test `answers_each_fence_once_drained_past`.
- `held_key` (tests, m7-power-tools/42; heavy, root of `it`, outside the `frames::` filter): held `j` ×188 at 16 ms on `TeamSpec::MONOREPO` at 187×50 into a `DrainSink` (16 KiB; Unicode 500 KB/s, Pixel 40 MB/s), scenario B adds `J`; `SUMMARY held_key` / `VERDICT held_key` lines; red until 43 (Unicode) and 46 (Pixel). Pixel SUMMARY carries `fold_changes_held=unread` and `strip_transmits_held` is printed `unjudged` (46c: the harness cannot read the drawn fold). Driven by `scripts/fix-scroll-held-key.sh`; also by `scripts/47-scroll-acceptance.sh` (m7-power-tools/47), which runs the raw pair apart and judges it against the `M7/46-kitty-no-deflate` known-gap list. **m8 G-pace:** `answers_dsr = true` and the drain answers fences (`Wake::Fence` on the harness channel), so it measures the paced loop; `Paced` implements `next_wake_by` and drops a look's stamp when a fence answer releases a frame; new `held_j_stops_on_release_{unicode_40mb,unicode_free,pixel_free,pixel_deflate_free}` (`FREE_RATE` = `drain_sink::FREE`: drained as written, no tick). Run by `scripts/m8-pace.sh` → `docs/perf/m8-pace.txt`.
- `ghostty_shot` (tests, m7-power-tools/47; heavy): `fix_scroll_ghostty_png` writes `docs/shots/m7/fix-scroll-ghostty.png` — held `j` ×188 on `TeamSpec::MONOREPO` at 187×50, every strip on screen composited through `StripCache::composited` (ADR-0053) with `kitty_deflate` on, stacked, cropped to the graph column and the cursor ±10 rows inside the pane, over `#1E1E2E`; checked by `capture::check_bytes`. Prints `SUMMARY ghostty_png … cursor_on_screen=`. Run by `scripts/47-scroll-acceptance.sh` step 5.
- `event_loop::tests::pixel_scroll` (unit tests, m7-power-tools/46, 46c): two-layer pixel strips through `draw`; `a_capped_graph_resends_strips_only_when_the_fold_changes` guards ADR-0053 on a graph wider than the cap (fixtures `view::strips::tests::{capped, fold_plan}`).
- `frames::sink::Sink::draining` (tests, 42): a `Sink` forwarding every write to a `DrainSink`, keeping no byte (`count` panics on it); `sent`/`write_spent` as for `Sink::timed`.
- `frames::run::ready_to_scroll_at` (tests, 42): `ready_to_scroll` at a size passed in, never `GRAFT_REAL_SIZE`; `ready_to_scroll` delegates to the same body with the env's value.
- `ChannelWakes` (struct) — the real wake source: one channel, two feeder threads.
- `ChannelWakes::new` (fn) — build the channel and the sender its producers clone.
- `ChannelWakes::unhandled_input` (fn) — the terminal input queued after the wake that ended the loop, in order; the rest is dropped with the loop.
- `run` (fn) — run the event loop for a session, start to `Exit`; may be called again by a wrapper (the gallery): stdin has one reader per process, and the keys this run never handled go to the next.
- `pump` (fn) — drive the loop; testable independent of a real terminal. Draws Kitty strips at the pixel tier as two layers per strip, the layout-only backdrop and the selection's emphasis over it (ADR-0053, 46), zlib-deflated when the probe found `kitty_deflate`; drains image replies each wake (a refused deflated upload turns deflate off), frees every upload of both layers on exit (m1-the-graph/24c). Theme/cell-size changes flush every cache layer; a resize flushes nothing and retransmits nothing — both layers re-place, only changed keys rasterise (25). iTerm2 protocol: one composited band per strip, re-sent per frame, encoded once per paint (25, 46). Idle prefetch draws backdrops only.

## event_loop::outside
Handing the terminal to another program and taking it back
(m3-working-tree-undo-and-stash/05). PROMPT.md principle 8: a wrong restore
breaks the user's terminal after Graft is gone, so every path here restores
newest-first and re-enters the same modes, or ends the loop saying it could
not.
- `Outside` (struct) — what the loop needs to reach outside itself: `common_dir` and an optional `Handover`. `None` in the headless and test loops, which have no terminal to give away.
- `Handover` (struct) — `session`, `mux`, `git_dir`, `workdir` (the working tree's root: the directory `$EDITOR` is started in, and what a porcelain path is joined onto — m3/05 review M2); `edit_message` (write the message to `graft/COMMIT_EDITMSG`, run `$EDITOR`, read back what was saved), `edit_file` (open a path, on a line where the editor takes one), `suspend` (`SIGTSTP` and back).
- `Handover::edit_conflict` (fn) — `(&self, path) -> Result<(), String>`: `$EDITOR` on a conflicted file, opened at its first `<<<<<<< ` (`graft_git::history_reads::first_marker_line`, `None` when the file is already clean). The read lives here, not in `update`, which is pure (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/09).
- `edit_message_in` (fn) — `(git_dir, message, open: FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<(), String>) -> Result<String, String>`: `Handover::edit_message`'s write / open / read-back without the terminal — the message written to `<git_dir>/graft/COMMIT_EDITMSG`, `open` run on it, the file read back exactly. The loop passes `Handover::open`; `tests/it/finish.rs` passes the fixture editor, so the test goes through the real call (m6-conflicts/36).
- `Handover::mergetool` (fn) — `(&self, path) -> Result<(), String>`: `git mergetool --no-prompt -- <path>` in `workdir`, with the terminal handed over. **Every** exit is inside `run_outside_terminal`: a non-zero tool, a tool that cannot start, a tool that is killed, and a panic all go back through the same `Reenter` guard. Nothing runs the tool outside that call, which is the property `event_loop::outside::tests` asserts — on the raw terminal transcript (teardown, then the identical setup again), not on a call site.
- `Handover::continue_in_git` (fn) — `(&self, InProgress) -> Result<ExitStatus, String>`: `git <kind> --continue` in `workdir`, terminal handed over as for the mergetool; exit status returned as is (07c).
- `continue_command` (fn) — `(InProgress, workdir) -> Command`: that invocation.
- `mergetool_command` (fn) — `(path, workdir) -> Command`: the invocation, apart from the hand-over so a test can read it. `--` before the path: a file named `--tool` is a file, not a flag.
- `run_outside_terminal` (fn) — pauses the input mux **first** so the reader thread stops consuming stdin (`InputMux::hold` only detaches the listener — the thread keeps reading, and `$EDITOR` would race Graft for keystrokes), then suspends the session, runs the closure, and re-enters both. A failure to re-enter is `OutsideError::Reenter`, which ends the loop rather than drawing into a terminal Graft no longer owns.
- `OutsideError` (enum) — `Restore` (could not give the terminal away; nothing ran) and `Reenter` (could not take it back).
- `editor_command` (fn) — `(git_editor, path, line, workdir) -> Command`. `path` is made absolute against `workdir`, which also becomes the command's directory (m3/05 review M2). A `GIT_EDITOR` carrying shell syntax (`'"$`\;&|<>()*?[`) is run as `sh -c '<editor> "$@"' <editor> <args>`, the way git runs it — a quoted path with a space in it used to be split mid-word (m3/05 review M5). The line argument goes per editor family: `+N` (vim/nvim/vi/nano/emacs/emacsclient/micro/hx/helix), `--goto path:N` (code/code-insiders/codium/cursor/windsurf), `path:N` (zed/zeditor), and no line argument for anything unrecognised — a wrong flag would open a file named after it.
- `stop_this_process` (fn) — `SIGTSTP` to Graft's own process group, so the shell's job control takes over exactly as it would for any other program.

## event_loop::signals
- `WATCHED` (const) — signals watched: SIGINT, SIGTERM, SIGHUP.
- `watch` (fn) — start the signal-watching thread; returns a `Watch` (`close()`; `again() -> Again`, a `graft_git::run::Stop` that is true once another watched signal arrives — the "send the signal again" of the exit wait). The thread counts every signal and keeps watching after the loop has gone.
- `watch_resizes` (fn) — watch `SIGWINCH`, waking the loop with `Event::Resize` at the new size.
- `reraise` (fn) — restore default disposition and re-raise a signal.

## event_loop::input
- `spawn_reader` (fn) — forward the input mux's `Inputs` into the loop's wakes, on a thread of its own.
- `running::pump_with` gains `tracer: &mut trace::Tracer` (m8 G-trace; `Tracer::off()` in tests); `draw_traced` wraps every `draw` in `frames`, and puts a trace failure on the status row as `Status::sticky("trace: <reason>")`. Tests: `event_loop::tests::perf_trace`.

## jobs
Internal split (m1-the-graph/71, public paths unchanged): `types` (`Outcome`,
`Work`, `Cancel`, `JobEvents`, `Delivery`, `Dispatch`), `pool` (`Shutdown`, `Forced`,
plus the private queue/worker bookkeeping), `kinds` (the work behind each
command), `runner` (`JobRunner`) — re-exported from `mod.rs`.
- `map` (private mod) — the Map view's job (m2-details-and-diffs/18): `build_map(repos, req, progress, stop) -> Result<Arc<MapData>, String>` runs `crate::map::build_map` and stringifies its error; `JobRunner::build_map` dispatches `Command::BuildMap` under `update`'s own id (`queue_as`, mirroring the detail pane's jobs), reporting `Progress::Map { done, total }` per finished row.
- `radar` (`pub(crate)` mod) — the Mistake Radar's context job (m2-details-and-diffs/13, 13b).
  - `RadarContext` (struct) — the default branch's first-parent ancestry as a `HandleSet` over its own `Arc<CommitTable>` (private; ADR-0026's rule, housekeeping/02: a `HashSet<Oid>` here was 43 of the heavy tier's 76 B/commit heap slope), plus `previous_default_tips: Vec<Oid>` (its reflog history, newest first, capped). `on_default_chain(&Oid) -> bool` / `chain_len() -> usize` / `of_ids(chain, previous_tips) -> RadarContext` (a context over ids, with a table of its own) (fn).
  - `run_evil_probe` (fn) — `(repo, merge, stop) -> EvilVerdict`; the work behind `Command::EvilProbe`, dispatched by `JobRunner::dispatch_detail`.
  - `run_force_push_probe` (fn) — `(repo, branch, current, stop) -> ForcePushVerdict`; the work behind `Command::ForcePushProbe`. Resolves the branch's remote-tracking ref through `RepoHandle::upstream` (config `update` cannot read), then `probe_force_push`; no upstream → `NotCheckable` (m2-details-and-diffs/15).
  - `run_radar_context` (fn) — `(repo, table, default_tip, default_ref, stop) -> Result<Arc<RadarContext>, String>`; the work behind `Command::RadarContext`, dispatched by `JobRunner::dispatch_detail`.
  - `run_duplicates_probe` (fn) — `(repo, window, stop) -> Result<Arc<Duplicates>, String>`; the work behind `Command::DuplicatesProbe`, wrapping `graft_ops::mistake_radar::probes::probe_duplicates` (m2-details-and-diffs/16).
- `preview` (private mod) — Ghost Preview's reading half (m5/08): `run_preview(repos, &PreviewRequest, clock, cancel) -> Result<Arc<Planned>, PreviewFailure>` opens `git`, reads the facts the operation names (`Operation::facts_scope`) and calls `graft_ops::preview::plan_as_run` (m7/05b: the op completed as `y` will run it, returned as `Planned::runs`, and its picture), or plans the time machine from the journal's own entries (`plan_revert_to`); then reads every real commit the picture points at that is not already on screen, as **anchors**, capped at `PREVIEW_ANCHOR_CAP` — past the cap the answer is a sentence the box prints, not a shorter picture. `JobRunner::plan_preview` dispatches `Command::PlanPreview` under `update`'s own id; `JobRunner::run_revert_to` dispatches `Command::RevertTo` as a write, with `Progress::Operating`.
- `undo::JournalRead::live` (field) — `Vec<EntryId>`: the entries recovery left `Pending` **on purpose**, because git is still part-way through that very operation (`graft_ops::apply::Recovered::live`, m5-history-and-ghost-preview/14). A live entry and an interrupted one are the same bytes on disk — a `Pending` line with no outcome — so the difference is decided here, at **read** time, against git's own state; nothing on disk is migrated and an older journal reads correctly. Carried across reads unlike `Recovered::closed`, because it is a fact about git *now* that every read re-establishes.
- `undo::JournalRead::interrupted_edits` (field, 30c) — `Vec<(EntryId, graft_ops::EditsAt)>`: `Recovered::set_aside` of this read; non-empty only on the read whose recovery closed the entry.
- `undo::journal_entries` (fn) — the journal's entries for a caller that only reads them; the time machine plans from the very entries `revert_to` will run against (m5/08).
- `undo::RevertedTo` (enum) — `Done { description, total, steps, replaced, presses }` (the steps' `Applied::replaced` unioned; `presses` = total − the first step that replaced anything, since `U` walks the jump back from its last step), `Drift { description, total, drift, target }` (m6-conflicts/77: also reached from `PartWay { done: [], Drift }`, which is how `revert_to` reports step 0's drift — before 77 that fell to `Failed`), `Failed { description, done, total, error }`. `total` is read from the plan **before** anything runs, because `UndoError::PartWay` carries only `done` and "2 of 3" is the sentence the user needs (m5/08).
- `undo::run_revert_to` (fn) — `(repos, &EntryId, force, clock, cancel) -> RevertedTo` (m5/08).
- `undo::Undone::Nothing` (variant) — `{ reason: String, redo: Option<String> }` (a tuple before m6-conflicts/77): `redo` is the waiting redo's root operation description (`next_redo` then `root_operation`, from the journal `u` just read), set only for `u`'s `NothingToUndo`.
- `undo::Undone::OperationLive` (variant) — `{ kind: InProgress }`: `graft_ops`' undo gate refused (`UndoError::Nothing(NotInvertible::OperationLive)`) because the step is from before the live operation (m6-conflicts/57). Its own variant rather than `Nothing`, which the app words as a quiet `Done`: this is a refusal, worded `Sticky` by `update::undo::undone`.
- `undo::Undone::Cancelled` (variant) — `{ clean: bool, why: String, refresh: Refresh }`: `Esc` stopped the undo and `graft_ops::restore::execute` rolled back what it had applied. Its own variant rather than a `Failed`, because a cancel is not a failure; `clean` says whether the roll-back finished, and `why` is `restore`'s own sentence. `refresh` is never optional (`Everything`): usually the journal gained an `Aborted` entry, but a stop before any step (m7/01b, gap :495) may have written none — the re-read is then spare and harmless (m5-history-and-ghost-preview/06 scope item 5, built in /08).
- `conflict` (crate-visible mod) — the resolver's jobs (m6-conflicts/04, /05), thin over `graft_ops::conflict`. `open(store, path, stop) -> Result<Opening, OpenFailure>` (`Opening::Unmerged(Arc<OpenedFile>)` / `Opening::Done(Arc<DoneFile>, StartOver)`, routed on that status: unmerged → `open_conflict`, else `open_done`; `OpenFailure.done: Option<StartOver>` marks the done route, m6-conflicts/25).
